		public ManageSummaryGridTemplatesCtl(SummaryGridTemplateCollection sgCol, SummaryGridTemplate sgNew, Dictionary<Guid, SummaryGridTemplate> dicOrigTpl)
		{
			Init(sgCol, dicOrigTpl);
			SummaryGridEdit summaryGridEdit = new SummaryGridEdit(sgNew);
			summaryGridEdit.ShowDialog();

			{
				VM.AddNewTemplateToCol(summaryGridEdit.SumGridTemplate);
			}
		}

		{
			InitializeComponent();
			ManageSummaryGridTemplatesVM vM = new ManageSummaryGridTemplatesVM(sgCol, dicOrigTpl);
			VM = vM;
		}

	public class ManageSummaryGridTemplatesVM : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private enum TemplateEditAction
		{
			NewTplBasedOnDefaultTpl,
			NewTplBasedOnSelectedTpl,
			EditSelectedTemplate
		}

		private ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTemplate> _sumGridTpls = new ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTemplate>();

		private SummaryGridTemplateCollection _sgCol;

		private Dictionary<Guid, SummaryGridTemplate> _dicOrigSgTemplates = new Dictionary<Guid, SummaryGridTemplate>();

		private SummaryGridTemplate _curSgTemplate;

		private RelayCommand _closeRequest;

		private RelayCommand _cmdNew;

		private RelayCommand _cmdEdit;

		private RelayCommand _cmdSave;

		private RelayCommand _cmdReset;

		private RelayCommand _cmdCopy;

		private RelayCommand _cmdDelete;

		private RelayCommand _cmdImport;

		private RelayCommand _cmdExport;

		public ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTemplate> SumGridTpls
		{
			get
			{
				return _sumGridTpls;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_sumGridTpls != value)
				{
					_sumGridTpls = value;
					NotifyPropChange("SumGridTpls");
				}
			}
		}

		public SummaryGridTemplate CurSgTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return _curSgTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_curSgTemplate != value)
				{
					_curSgTemplate = value;
					NotifyPropChange("CurSgTemplate");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool SummaryGridCollectionChanged { get; private set; }

		public ICommand CloseRequest
		{
			get
			{
				if (_closeRequest == null)
				{
					_closeRequest = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						RequestCloseNow();
					}, (object param) => true);
				}
				return _closeRequest;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdNew
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdNew == null)
				{
					_cmdNew = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						EditTemplate(TemplateEditAction.NewTplBasedOnDefaultTpl);
					}, (object param) => true);
				}
				return _cmdNew;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdEdit
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdEdit == null)
				{
					_cmdEdit = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						EditTemplate(TemplateEditAction.EditSelectedTemplate);
					}, (object param) => CurSgTemplate != null);
				}
				return _cmdEdit;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdSave
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdSave == null)
				{
					_cmdSave = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						SaveCurTemplate();
					}, (object param) => CurSgTemplate != null && CurSgTemplate.IsDirty);
				}
				return _cmdSave;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdReset
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdReset == null)
				{
					_cmdReset = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						RevertCurTemplate();
					}, (object param) => CurSgTemplate != null && CurSgTemplate.IsDirty);
				}
				return _cmdReset;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdCopy
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdCopy == null)
				{
					_cmdCopy = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						EditTemplate(TemplateEditAction.NewTplBasedOnSelectedTpl);
					}, (object param) => CurSgTemplate != null);
				}
				return _cmdCopy;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdDelete
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdDelete == null)
				{
					_cmdDelete = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						DeleteCurrentTemplate();
					}, (object param) => CurSgTemplate != null);
				}
				return _cmdDelete;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdImport
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdImport == null)
				{
					_cmdImport = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						ImportSgTemplate();
					}, (object param) => true);
				}
				return _cmdImport;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CmdExport
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cmdExport == null)
				{
					_cmdExport = new RelayCommand(delegate
					{
						ExportSgTemplate();
					}, (object param) => CurSgTemplate != null);
				}
				return _cmdExport;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public event EventHandler OnCloseRequest;

		public ManageSummaryGridTemplatesVM(SummaryGridTemplateCollection sgCol, Dictionary<Guid, SummaryGridTemplate> dicOrigTpl)
		{
			_sgCol = sgCol;
			_dicOrigSgTemplates = dicOrigTpl;
			SumGridTpls = sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates;
			SummaryGridCollectionChanged = false;
		}

		public void AddNewTemplateToCol(SummaryGridTemplate tplToAdd)
		{
			tplToAdd.IsDirty = false;
			if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(tplToAdd.Id))
			{
				_dicOrigSgTemplates[tplToAdd.Id] = tplToAdd.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true);
			}
			else
			{
				_dicOrigSgTemplates.Add(tplToAdd.Id, tplToAdd.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
			}
			_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Add(tplToAdd);
		}

		private SummaryGridTemplate CreateTemplate(bool isNewTemplate)
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = null;
			string baseName = "";
			if (isNewTemplate)
			{
				summaryGridTemplate = _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate;
			}
			else
			{
				if (CurSgTemplate == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				summaryGridTemplate = CurSgTemplate;
				baseName = CurSgTemplate.Name;
			}
			if (summaryGridTemplate == null)
			{
				summaryGridTemplate = new SummaryGridTemplate();
			}
			SummaryGridTemplate copy = summaryGridTemplate.GetCopy();
			copy.Name = _sgCol.GetNewTemplateName(baseName);
			return copy;
		}

		private void UpdateDisplayedTplList(Guid selectedTplId)
		{
			if (selectedTplId == Guid.Empty)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == selectedTplId)
				{
					CurSgTemplate = _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i];
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void EditTemplate(TemplateEditAction curAction)
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = null;
			switch (curAction)
			{
			case TemplateEditAction.NewTplBasedOnDefaultTpl:
				summaryGridTemplate = CreateTemplate(isNewTemplate: true);
				break;
			case TemplateEditAction.NewTplBasedOnSelectedTpl:
				summaryGridTemplate = CreateTemplate(isNewTemplate: false);
				break;
			case TemplateEditAction.EditSelectedTemplate:
				summaryGridTemplate = CurSgTemplate;
				if (summaryGridTemplate == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				break;
			}
			SummaryGridEdit summaryGridEdit = new SummaryGridEdit(summaryGridTemplate);
			summaryGridEdit.ShowDialog();
			if (summaryGridEdit.OkClicked)
			{
				switch (curAction)
				{
				case TemplateEditAction.NewTplBasedOnDefaultTpl:
				case TemplateEditAction.NewTplBasedOnSelectedTpl:
				{
					SummaryGridTemplate sumGridTemplate = summaryGridEdit.SumGridTemplate;
					AddNewTemplateToCol(sumGridTemplate);
					break;
				}
				case TemplateEditAction.EditSelectedTemplate:
					summaryGridTemplate = summaryGridEdit.SumGridTemplate;
					_sgCol.UpdateSgTemplate(summaryGridTemplate);
					break;
				}
				UpdateDisplayedTplList(summaryGridTemplate.Id);
			}
		}

		private void DeleteCurrentTemplate()
		{
			if (CurSgTemplate == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = SumGridTpls.IndexOf(CurSgTemplate) - 1;
			if (num < 0)
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			if (_sgCol != null)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(Global.GetString("ManageSummaryGridTemplatesVM_DeleteSumGridTemplate"));
				stringBuilder.Append("\r\n\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("(");
				stringBuilder.Append(CurSgTemplate.ToString());
				stringBuilder.Append(")");
				if (System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(caption: SummaryGridTemplate.MessageBoxTitle, text: stringBuilder.ToString(), buttons: MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel, icon: MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation) != DialogResult.Yes)
				{
					return;
				}
				Guid id = CurSgTemplate.Id;
				if (_sgCol.DeleteTemplate(id))
				{
					if (SumGridTpls.Count == 0)
					{
						CurSgTemplate = null;
					}
					else
					{
						CurSgTemplate = SumGridTpls[num];
					}
					if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(id))
					{
						_dicOrigSgTemplates.Remove(id);
					}
				}
			}
			SummaryGridCollectionChanged = true;
		}

		private void ImportSgTemplate()
		{
			if (_sgCol != null)
			{
				_sgCol.ImportSummaryGridTemplate();
			}
			SummaryGridCollectionChanged = true;
		}

		private void ExportSgTemplate()
		{
			CurSgTemplate?.SaveAs();
		}

		private void SaveCurTemplate()
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate curSgTemplate = CurSgTemplate;
			if (curSgTemplate != null)
			{
				curSgTemplate.IsDirty = false;
				if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(curSgTemplate.Id))
				{
					_dicOrigSgTemplates.Remove(curSgTemplate.Id);
				}
				_dicOrigSgTemplates.Add(curSgTemplate.Id, curSgTemplate.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
				SummaryGridCollectionChanged = true;
			}
		}

		private void RevertCurTemplate()
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate curSgTemplate = CurSgTemplate;
			if (curSgTemplate == null || !_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(curSgTemplate.Id) || _sgCol == null || _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == curSgTemplate.Id)
				{
					_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] = _dicOrigSgTemplates[curSgTemplate.Id].GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true);
					CurSgTemplate = _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i];
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void NotifyPropChange(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		private void RequestCloseNow()
		{
			if (this.OnCloseRequest != null)
			{
				this.OnCloseRequest(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}
	}
	public class PopupComboBox : Popup
	{
		private XamComboEditor _combo;

		private List<string> _units;

		private string _selectedUnits = string.Empty;

		private bool _unitsChanged;

		private string _origUnits = string.Empty;

		public bool UnitsChanged => _unitsChanged;

		public string SelectedUnits => _selectedUnits;

		public XamComboEditor Combo => _combo;

		public event EventHandler SelectionChanged;

		private void OnSelectionChanged()
		{
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public PopupComboBox(List<string> Units, int selectedIndex, string originalUnits)
		{
			_units = Units;
			base.MinWidth = 50.0;
			_combo = new XamComboEditor();
			_combo.DropDownResizeMode = PopupResizeMode.None;
			_combo.DropDownButtonDisplayMode = DropDownButtonDisplayMode.Always;
			_combo.AcceptsArrowKeysInEditMode = true;
			_combo.ItemsSource = _units;
			_combo.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
			_origUnits = originalUnits;
			_combo.SelectedItemChanged += _combo_SelectedItemChanged;
			base.Child = new DockPanel
			{
				LastChildFill = true,
				Background = System.Windows.SystemColors.WindowBrush,
				Children = { (UIElement)_combo }
			};
		}

		private void _combo_SelectedItemChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (_combo.SelectedItem != null)
			{
				_selectedUnits = _combo.SelectedItem.ToString();
			}
			_unitsChanged = _selectedUnits != _origUnits;
			if (_unitsChanged)
			{
				OnSelectionChanged();
			}
		}

		public void OpenCombo()
		{
			base.IsOpen = true;
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class SGClipboardMetaData
	{
		public bool ContainsColumnHeaders { get; set; }

		public SGClipboardMetaData()
		{
		}

		public SGClipboardMetaData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			ContainsColumnHeaders = (bool)info.GetValue("ContainsColumnHeaders", typeof(bool));
		}

		public void GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			info.AddValue("ContainsColumnHeaders", ContainsColumnHeaders);
		}
	}
	public class SummaryGridEdit : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private enum MoveDirection
		{
			Up,
			Down,
			Left,
			Right
		}

		private readonly string _cAppTitle = Global.GetString("Global_SumGridTitle");

		private SummaryGridTemplate _sgEditedCopy;

		private SummaryGridTemplate _sgProvidedCopy;

		private bool _isUpdatingTemplateName;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox txtTemplate;

		internal XamTabControl TabControl;

		internal SumGridEditOKCancelBtns OKCancelButtons;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public SummaryGridTemplate SumGridTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return _sgProvidedCopy;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (_sgProvidedCopy != null)
				{
					try
					{
						_sgProvidedCopy.PropertyChanged -= _sgTmp_PropertyChanged;
						_sgProvidedCopy.SGTabs.CollectionChanged -= SGTabs_CollectionChanged;
						_sgProvidedCopy = null;
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
				_sgProvidedCopy = value;
				_sgProvidedCopy.PropertyChanged += _sgTmp_PropertyChanged;
				_sgProvidedCopy.SGTabs.CollectionChanged += SGTabs_CollectionChanged;
			}
		}

		public bool OkClicked { get; private set; }

		public SummaryGridEdit(SummaryGridTemplate tmp)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			bool isDirty = tmp.IsDirty;
			_sgProvidedCopy = tmp;
			_sgEditedCopy = tmp.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true);
			_sgEditedCopy.IsDirty = isDirty;
			_sgEditedCopy.PropertyChanged += _sgTmp_PropertyChanged;
			_sgEditedCopy.SGTabs.CollectionChanged += SGTabs_CollectionChanged;
			InitTabs();
			UpdateToolbar();
			OKCancelButtons.OnOkClicked += SummaryGridEdit_OKClicked;
			OKCancelButtons.OnCancelClicked += SummaryGridEdit_CancelClick;
		}

		private void InitTabs()
		{
			TabControl.Items.Clear();
			foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in _sgEditedCopy.SGTabs)
			{
				AddControlTab(sGTab);
			}
			if (TabControl.Items.Count > 0)
			{
				TabControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
			}
			txtTemplate.Text = _sgEditedCopy.Name;
		}

		private void _sgTmp_PropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateToolbar();
		}

		private void SGTabs_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.Action)
			{
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add:
			{
				foreach (SummaryGridTab newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					AddControlTab(newItem, e.NewStartingIndex);
				}
				break;
			}
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Move:
				InitTabs();
				break;
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove:
			{
				foreach (SummaryGridTab oldItem in e.OldItems)
				{
					RemoveControlTab(oldItem);
				}
				break;
			}
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace:
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < e.OldItems.Count; i++)
				{
					SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab = e.OldItems[i] as SummaryGridTab;
					SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab2 = e.NewItems[i] as SummaryGridTab;
					if (summaryGridTab != null && summaryGridTab2 != null)
					{
						ReplaceControlTab(summaryGridTab, summaryGridTab2);
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset:
				InitTabs();
				break;
			}
		}

		private bool RemoveControlTab(SummaryGridTab sgtabToRemove)
		{
			if (TabControl == null || TabControl.Items == null || sgtabToRemove == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < TabControl.Items.Count; i++)
			{
				if (TabControl.Items[i] != null && (TabControl.Items[i] as TabItemEx).Tag != null && (TabControl.Items[i] as TabItemEx).Tag is SummaryGridTab && string.CompareOrdinal(((TabControl.Items[i] as TabItemEx).Tag as SummaryGridTab).Caption, sgtabToRemove.Caption) == 0)
				{
					TabControl.Items.Remove(TabControl.Items[i]);
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool ReplaceControlTab(SummaryGridTab oldSgTab, SummaryGridTab newSgTab)
		{
			if (TabControl == null || TabControl.Items == null || oldSgTab == null || newSgTab == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < TabControl.Items.Count; i++)
			{
				if (TabControl.Items[i] != null && (TabControl.Items[i] as TabItemEx).Tag != null && (TabControl.Items[i] as TabItemEx).Tag is SummaryGridTab && string.CompareOrdinal(((TabControl.Items[i] as TabItemEx).Tag as SummaryGridTab).Caption, oldSgTab.Caption) == 0)
				{
					num = i;
				}
			}
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			TabControl.Items.Remove(TabControl.Items[num]);
			AddControlTab(newSgTab, num);
			return true;
		}

		private void AddControlTab(SummaryGridTab sgtabToAdd, int indexWhereToAdd = -1)
		{
			if (TabControl != null && TabControl.Items != null && sgtabToAdd != null && !TabControl.Items.Contains(sgtabToAdd.Caption))
			{
				int num = indexWhereToAdd;
				if (indexWhereToAdd < 0 || indexWhereToAdd > TabControl.Items.Count - 1)
				{
					num = TabControl.Items.Count;
				}
				XamDataGrid viewForTab = GetViewForTab(sgtabToAdd);
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldLayoutSettings.SelectionTypeField = SelectionType.None;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldLayoutSettings.SelectionTypeRecord = SelectionType.ExtendedAutoDrag;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldSettings.LabelClickAction = (LabelClickAction)1;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldSettings.CellClickAction = (CellClickAction)3;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldLayoutSettings.AutoGenerateFields = true;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldLayoutSettings.AllowFieldMoving = (AllowFieldMoving)2;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldLayoutSettings.DataRecordSizingMode = (DataRecordSizingMode)2;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldLayoutSettings.AllowClipboardOperations = (AllowClipboardOperations)0;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).GroupByAreaLocation = (GroupByAreaLocation)0;
				((System.Windows.Controls.Control)(object)viewForTab).Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.GhostWhite;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldSettings.AllowEdit = true;
				((DataPresenterBase)viewForTab).FieldSettings.AllowResize = true;
				TabItemEx tabItemEx = new TabItemEx();
				tabItemEx.Header = sgtabToAdd.Caption;
				tabItemEx.Content = viewForTab;
				string text = sgtabToAdd.Caption.ToString();
				text = text.Replace(" ", "");
				text = text.Replace("-", "");
				try
				{
					tabItemEx.Name = text;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				tabItemEx.Tag = sgtabToAdd;
				TabControl.Items.Insert(num, tabItemEx);
				TabControl.SelectedItem = TabControl.Items[num];
			}
		}

		private static XamDataGrid GetViewForTab(SummaryGridTab sgTab)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0005: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Expected O, but got Unknown
			return new XamDataGrid
			{
				DataSource = sgTab.ObjProps
			};
		}

		private void SummaryGridEdit_OKClicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_sgProvidedCopy = _sgEditedCopy;
			OKCancelButtons.OnOkClicked -= SummaryGridEdit_OKClicked;
			OkClicked = true;
			Close();
		}

		private void SummaryGridEdit_CancelClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OKCancelButtons.OnCancelClicked -= SummaryGridEdit_CancelClick;
			Close();
		}

		private void DeleteCurrentCellRows()
		{
			//IL_008b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0092: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (TabControl.SelectedItem == null || !(TabControl.SelectedItem is TabItemEx) || (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			TabItemEx tabItemEx = TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx;
			if (tabItemEx.Content == null || !(tabItemEx.Content is XamDataGrid))
			{
				return;
			}
			object content = tabItemEx.Content;
			XamDataGrid val = (XamDataGrid)((content is XamDataGrid) ? content : null);
			List<DataRecord> list = new List<DataRecord>();
			foreach (DataRecord record in ((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Records)
			{
				DataRecord item = record;
				if (!list.Contains(item))
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			foreach (Cell cell in ((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Cells)
			{
				if (!list.Contains(cell.Record))
				{
					list.Add(cell.Record);
				}
			}
			List<int> list2 = new List<int>();
			foreach (DataRecord item2 in list)
			{
				list2.Add(((Record)item2).Index);
			}
			DeleteRows(list2);
			UpdateToolbar();
		}

		private void DeleteRows(List<int> rowsToDelete, bool askForConfirmation = true)
		{
			//IL_00b8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00be: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (TabControl.SelectedItem == null || !(TabControl.SelectedItem is TabItemEx) || (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			TabItemEx tabItemEx = TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx;
			if (tabItemEx.Content == null || !(tabItemEx.Content is XamDataGrid))
			{
				return;
			}
			object content = tabItemEx.Content;
			XamDataGrid val = (XamDataGrid)((content is XamDataGrid) ? content : null);
			if (rowsToDelete == null)
			{
				rowsToDelete = new List<int>();
				if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Records).Count > 0)
				{
					if (!((TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag is SummaryGridTab))
					{
						return;
					}
					foreach (DataRecord item in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)val).Records)
					{
						DataRecord val2 = item;
						rowsToDelete.Add(((Record)val2).Index);
					}
				}
			}
			if (rowsToDelete.Count <= 0 || !((TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag is SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab))
			{
				return;
			}
			List<SummaryGridProperty> list = new List<SummaryGridProperty>();
			foreach (int item2 in rowsToDelete)
			{
				if (summaryGridTab.ObjProps != null && summaryGridTab.ObjProps.Count > item2)
				{
					list.Add(summaryGridTab.ObjProps[item2]);
				}
			}
			foreach (SummaryGridProperty item3 in list)
			{
				if (item3.CxsPropertyPath.StartsWith("#"))
				{
					if (askForConfirmation)
					{
						StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(Global.GetString("FrmSummaryGridEdit_ThisPropCannotBeDel"));
						stringBuilder.Append(" ");
						stringBuilder.Append(item3.VariableDescription);
						System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(caption: _cAppTitle, text: stringBuilder.ToString(), buttons: MessageBoxButtons.OK, icon: MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (askForConfirmation)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder2.Append(Global.GetString("FrmSummaryGridEdit_TheFollowingNumRowsToBeDel"));
				stringBuilder2.Append(" ");
				stringBuilder2.Append(list.Count.ToString());
				stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n\r\n");
				stringBuilder2.Append(Global.GetString("FrmSummaryGridEdit_DoYouWantContinue"));
				if (System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(stringBuilder2.ToString(), _cAppTitle, MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation) != System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.Yes)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			foreach (SummaryGridProperty item4 in list)
			{
				summaryGridTab.ObjProps.Remove(item4);
				_sgEditedCopy.IsDirty = true;
			}
		}

		private void DeleteCurrentTab(bool askForConfirmation = true)
		{
			if (TabControl.SelectedItem == null || (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag == null || !((TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag is SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (askForConfirmation)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(Global.GetString("FrmSummaryGridEdit_AreYouSureDelTab"));
				stringBuilder.Append("\r\n\r\n(");
				stringBuilder.Append(summaryGridTab.Caption);
				stringBuilder.Append(")");
				if (System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(stringBuilder.ToString(), SummaryGridTemplate.MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question) != System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.Yes)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			_sgEditedCopy.RemoveTab((TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag as SummaryGridTab);
			UpdateToolbar();
		}

		private void UpdateToolbar()
		{
			if (_sgEditedCopy != null && !_isUpdatingTemplateName)
			{
				txtTemplate.Text = _sgEditedCopy.Name;
			}
		}

		private void SummaryGridEdit_DeleteRowClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			DeleteCurrentCellRows();
		}

		private void SummaryGridEdit_DeleteTabClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)

		}

		private void PasteCxsVars()
		{
			List<SummaryGridTab> list = new List<SummaryGridTab>();
			foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in _sgEditedCopy.SGTabs)
			{
				list.Add(sGTab);
			}
			string selectedTabName = string.Empty;
			if (TabControl != null && TabControl.SelectedItem != null && (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Name != null && (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Name != null)
			{
				selectedTabName = (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Name.ToString();
			}
			List<string> list2 = _sgEditedCopy.Paste(suppressDialogs: false, selectedTabName);
			if (TabControl == null || TabControl.Items == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)TabControl.Items)
			{
				if (item != null && item.Tag != null && item.Tag != null && item.Tag is SummaryGridTab)
				{
					SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab = item.Tag as SummaryGridTab;
					if (list2.Count > 0 && string.CompareOrdinal(summaryGridTab.Caption, list2[list2.Count - 1]) == 0)
					{
						TabControl.SelectedItem = item;
					}
				}
			}
			UpdateToolbar();
		}

		private void MoveSelectedItem(MoveDirection dir)
		{
			if (TabControl == null || TabControl.SelectedItem == null || (dir != MoveDirection.Left && dir != MoveDirection.Right))
			{
				return;
			}
			SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab = null;
			if (TabControl.SelectedItem is TabItemEx && (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag != null)
			{
				summaryGridTab = (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag as SummaryGridTab;
			}
			if (summaryGridTab == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = _sgEditedCopy.SGTabs.IndexOf(summaryGridTab);
			if (num != -1 && (dir != MoveDirection.Left || num > 0) && (dir != MoveDirection.Right || num < _sgEditedCopy.SGTabs.Count - 1))
			{
				int num2 = num;
				if (dir == MoveDirection.Left)
				{
					num2--;
				}
				if (dir == MoveDirection.Right)
				{
					num2++;
				}
				_sgEditedCopy.SGTabs.Remove(summaryGridTab);
				_sgEditedCopy.SGTabs.Insert(num2, summaryGridTab);
				TabControl.SelectedItem = TabControl.Items[num2];
				_sgEditedCopy.IsDirty = true;
				UpdateToolbar();
			}
		}

		private void MoveSelectedRow(MoveDirection dir)
		{
			//IL_0063: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			XamDataGrid val = null;
			if (TabControl.SelectedItem != null)
			{
				object content = (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Content;
				val = (XamDataGrid)((content is XamDataGrid) ? content : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab = (((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Parent as TabItemEx).Tag as SummaryGridTab;
					int num = -1;
					int num2 = 0;
					foreach (DataRecord item3 in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)val).Records)
					{
						DataRecord val2 = item3;
						if (((Record)val2).IsSelected)
						{
							num2++;
							num = ((Record)val2).Index;
						}
					}
					if (num2 <= 0)
					{
						_ = 1;
					}
					if (num != -1)
					{
						switch (dir)
						{
						case MoveDirection.Up:
						{
							if (num == 0)
							{
								return;
							}
							SummaryGridProperty item2 = summaryGridTab.ObjProps[num];
							summaryGridTab.ObjProps.Remove(summaryGridTab.ObjProps[num]);
							summaryGridTab.ObjProps.Insert(num - 1, item2);
							((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
							((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
							((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
							((DataPresenterBase)val).Records[num - 1].IsSelected = true;
							((DataPresenterBase)val).ActiveRecord = ((DataPresenterBase)val).Records[num - 1];
							break;
						}
						case MoveDirection.Down:
						{
							if (num > summaryGridTab.ObjProps.Count - 2 || !((((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Parent as TabItemEx).Tag is SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab2))
							{
								return;
							}
							SummaryGridProperty item = summaryGridTab2.ObjProps[num];
							summaryGridTab2.ObjProps.Remove(summaryGridTab2.ObjProps[num]);
							summaryGridTab2.ObjProps.Insert(num + 1, item);
							((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
							((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
							((DataPresenterBase)val).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
							((DataPresenterBase)val).Records[num + 1].IsSelected = true;
							((DataPresenterBase)val).ActiveRecord = ((DataPresenterBase)val).Records[num + 1];
							break;
						}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			_sgEditedCopy.IsDirty = true;
			UpdateToolbar();
		}

		private void SummaryGridEdit_LeftClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{

		}

		private void TabSelection_Changed(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SummaryGridTab bindingToSumGridTab = null;
			if (TabControl.SelectedItem != null && TabControl.SelectedItem is TabItemEx && (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag != null && (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag is SummaryGridTab)
			{
				bindingToSumGridTab = (TabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Tag as SummaryGridTab;
			}
			OKCancelButtons.SetBindingToSumGridTab(bindingToSumGridTab);
		}

		{
			if (_sgEditedCopy != null)
			{
				try
				{
					_sgEditedCopy.PropertyChanged -= _sgTmp_PropertyChanged;
					_sgEditedCopy.SGTabs.CollectionChanged -= SGTabs_CollectionChanged;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		private void SummaryGridEdit_TemplateTextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_sgEditedCopy != null && !_isUpdatingTemplateName)
			{
				_isUpdatingTemplateName = true;
				try
				{
					_sgEditedCopy.Name = txtTemplate.Text;
				}
				finally
				{
					_isUpdatingTemplateName = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void TabControl_MouseRightButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
			System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem menuItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridEdit_Paste,
				Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.Paste16.ToImageSource()
				}
			};
			menuItem.Click += menuItemPaste_Click;
			contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem);
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
			System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem menuItem2 = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridEdit_DeleteTab
			};
			menuItem2.Click += menuItemDeleteTab_Click;
			contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem2);
			System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem menuItem3 = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridEdit_DeleteRow
			};
			menuItem3.Click += menuItemDeleteRow_Click;
			contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem3);
			TabControl.ContextMenu = null;
			TabControl.ContextMenu = contextMenu;
			TabControl.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
			e.Handled = true;
		}

			case 9:
				((System.Windows.Controls.Button)target).Click += SummaryGridEdit_DownClick;
				break;
			case 10:
				((System.Windows.Controls.Button)target).Click += SummaryGridEdit_DeleteRowClick;
				break;
			case 11:
				OKCancelButtons = (SumGridEditOKCancelBtns)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TabbedSummaryGrid : System.Windows.Controls.UserControl, ITabbedSummaryGrid, IComponentConnector
	{
		private class ConfigSchema
		{

			public static string ReadStringElement(XmlReader reader)
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				reader.Read();
				while (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
				{
					if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Text)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("TabbedSummaryGridSchema_NotASimpleElement"));
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(reader.Value);
					reader.Read();
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}

			public static bool ReadBooleanElement(XmlReader reader)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				string strA = ReadStringElement(reader);
				if (string.Compare(strA, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.Compare(strA, "false", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("TabbedSummaryGridSchema_BooleanValueExpected"));
			}

			public static void ReadDictionary(XmlReader reader, Dictionary<string, string> dict)
			{
				string name = reader.Name;
				while (reader.Read() && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !(reader.Name == name)))
				{
					if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
					{
						if (reader.Name == "Entry")
						{
							ReadDictionaryEntry(reader, dict);
						}
						else
						{
							ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
						}
					}
				}
			}

			private static void ReadDictionaryEntry(XmlReader reader, Dictionary<string, string> dict)
			{
				string name = reader.Name;
				string text = string.Empty;
				string value = string.Empty;
				while (reader.Read() && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !(reader.Name == name)))
				{
					if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
					{
						continue;
					}
					string name2 = reader.Name;
					if (!(name2 == "Key"))
					{
						if (name2 == "Value")
						{
							value = ReadStringElement(reader);
						}
						else
						{
							ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						text = ReadStringElement(reader);
					}
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					dict[text] = value;
				}
			}

			public static void GetTabRowInfoFromConfig(ref Dictionary<string, List<SingleRowInfo>> ProvidedRowInfo, string xmlConfig, SummaryGridTab tab)
			{
				XPathNavigator xPathNavigator = new XPathDocument(new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(xmlConfig))).CreateNavigator();
				string xpath = "TabbedSummaryGrid/Tabs/Tab";
				foreach (XPathNavigator item in xPathNavigator.Select(xpath))
				{
					string empty = string.Empty;
					try
					{
						empty = item.SelectSingleNode("Caption").Value;
					}
					catch
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (empty != tab.Caption)
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (XPathNavigator item2 in item.Select("Rows/Row"))
					{
						string empty2 = string.Empty;
						string empty3 = string.Empty;
						string underlyingPath = string.Empty;
						string detailtab = string.Empty;
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator4 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Name");
						try
						{
							empty2 = xPathNavigator4.Value;
						}
						catch
						{
							continue;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator5 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Path");
						try
						{
							empty3 = xPathNavigator5.Value;
						}
						catch
						{
							continue;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator6 = item2.SelectSingleNode("UnderlyingPath");
						try
						{
							if (xPathNavigator6 != null)
							{
								underlyingPath = xPathNavigator6.Value;
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator7 = item2.SelectSingleNode("DetailTab");
						try
						{
							if (xPathNavigator7 != null)
							{
								detailtab = xPathNavigator7.Value;
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator8 = item2.SelectSingleNode("EEStatus");
						string eeStatusPath = string.Empty;
						try
						{
							if (xPathNavigator8 != null)
							{
								eeStatusPath = xPathNavigator8.Value;
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						string baseURI = item2.BaseURI;
						XPathNodeIterator xPathNodeIterator = item2.SelectChildren("StatusReport", baseURI);
						List<string> list = new List<string>();
						foreach (XPathNavigator item3 in xPathNodeIterator)
						{
							try
							{
								list.Add(item3.Value.ToString());
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
						if (!ProvidedRowInfo.ContainsKey(empty))
						{
							ProvidedRowInfo.Add(empty, new List<SingleRowInfo>());
						}
						ProvidedRowInfo[empty].Add(new SingleRowInfo(empty2, empty3, underlyingPath, detailtab, list.ToArray(), eeStatusPath));
					}
				}
			}

			public static Dictionary<string, List<SingleRowInfo>> GetRowInfoFromConfig(string configXml)
			{
				Dictionary<string, List<SingleRowInfo>> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, List<SingleRowInfo>>();
				XPathNavigator xPathNavigator = new XPathDocument(new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(configXml))).CreateNavigator();
				string xpath = "TabbedSummaryGrid/Tabs/Tab";
				foreach (XPathNavigator item in xPathNavigator.Select(xpath))
				{
					string empty = string.Empty;
					try
					{
						empty = item.SelectSingleNode("Caption").Value;
					}
					catch
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (XPathNavigator item2 in item.Select("Rows/Row"))
					{
						string empty2 = string.Empty;
						string empty3 = string.Empty;
						string underlyingPath = string.Empty;
						string detailtab = string.Empty;
						string eeStatusPath = string.Empty;
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator4 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Name");
						try
						{
							empty2 = xPathNavigator4.Value;
						}
						catch
						{
							continue;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator5 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Path");
						try
						{
							empty3 = xPathNavigator5.Value;
						}
						catch
						{
							continue;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator6 = item2.SelectSingleNode("UnderlyingPath");
						try
						{
							if (xPathNavigator6 != null)
							{
								underlyingPath = xPathNavigator6.Value;
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator7 = item2.SelectSingleNode("DetailTab");
						try
						{
							if (xPathNavigator7 != null)
							{
								detailtab = xPathNavigator7.Value;
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator8 = item2.SelectSingleNode("EEStatus");
						try
						{
							if (xPathNavigator8 != null)
							{
								eeStatusPath = xPathNavigator8.Value;
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						string baseURI = item2.BaseURI;
						XPathNodeIterator xPathNodeIterator = item2.SelectChildren("StatusReport", baseURI);
						List<string> list = new List<string>();
						foreach (XPathNavigator item3 in xPathNodeIterator)
						{
							try
							{
								list.Add(item3.Value.ToString());
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
						if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(empty))
						{
							dictionary.Add(empty, new List<SingleRowInfo>());
						}
						dictionary[empty].Add(new SingleRowInfo(empty2, empty3, underlyingPath, detailtab, list.ToArray(), eeStatusPath));
					}
				}
				return dictionary;
			}

			public static SummaryGridTemplate GetSummaryGridTemplateFromConfig(string configXml, bool useProvidedRowInfo, CxsCase curCase = null, string filter = "")
			{
				SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = new SummaryGridTemplate(isDefaultTemplate: true);
				XPathNavigator xPathNavigator = new XPathDocument(new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(configXml))).CreateNavigator();
				string xpath = "TabbedSummaryGrid/Tabs/Tab";
				foreach (XPathNavigator item in xPathNavigator.Select(xpath))
				{
					string text = string.Empty;
					try
					{
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator3 = item.SelectSingleNode("Caption");
						if (xPathNavigator3 != null)
						{
							text = xPathNavigator3.Value;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool result = false;
					try
					{
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator4 = item.SelectSingleNode("ExcludeNameProperty");
						if (xPathNavigator4 != null)
						{
							bool.TryParse(xPathNavigator4.Value, out result);
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					bool result2 = false;
					try
					{
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator5 = item.SelectSingleNode("ExcludeGroupProperty");
						if (xPathNavigator5 != null)
						{
							bool.TryParse(xPathNavigator5.Value, out result2);
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab = new SummaryGridTab(summaryGridTemplate, !result, !result2);
					try
					{
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator6 = item.SelectSingleNode("UseProvidedRowInfo");
						if (xPathNavigator6 != null)
						{
							bool result3 = false;
							if (bool.TryParse(xPathNavigator6.Value, out result3))
							{
								summaryGridTab.ReadRowInfoFromXml = result3;
							}
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					summaryGridTab.Caption = text;
					summaryGridTab.Name = text;
					summaryGridTab.IsDirty = false;
					summaryGridTab.CaptionFromOrigXml = text;
					bool isHorizontal = true;
					bool flag = false;
					try
					{
						if (item.SelectSingleNode("Horizontal") != null)
						{
							isHorizontal = bool.Parse(item.SelectSingleNode("Horizontal").Value);
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						summaryGridTab.IsHorizontal = isHorizontal;
					}
					foreach (XPathNavigator item2 in item.Select("Columns/Column"))
					{
						string text2 = string.Empty;
						string text3 = string.Empty;
						string empty = string.Empty;
						string text4 = string.Empty;
						bool isCurrency = false;
						string empty2 = string.Empty;
						string text5 = string.Empty;
						bool? flag2 = null;
						bool? usesUnits = null;
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator8 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Name");
						if (xPathNavigator8 != null)
						{
							text2 = xPathNavigator8.Value;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator9 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Path");
						if (xPathNavigator9 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						string value = xPathNavigator9.Value;
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) || summaryGridTab.GetPathsAddedByDefault().Contains(value))
						{
							continue;
						}
						empty = value;
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator10 = item2.SelectSingleNode("UnderlyingPath");
						if (xPathNavigator10 != null)
						{
							text4 = xPathNavigator10.Value;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator11 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Caption");
						if (xPathNavigator11 != null)
						{
							text3 = xPathNavigator11.Value;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator12 = item2.SelectSingleNode("DataType");
						if (xPathNavigator12 != null)
						{
							empty2 = xPathNavigator12.Value;
							XPathNavigator xPathNavigator13 = item2.SelectSingleNode("IsCurrency");
							if (xPathNavigator13 != null)
							{
								isCurrency = bool.Parse(xPathNavigator13.Value);
							}
							XPathNavigator xPathNavigator14 = item2.SelectSingleNode("UsesUnits");
							if (xPathNavigator14 != null)
							{
								usesUnits = bool.Parse(xPathNavigator14.Value);
							}
							XPathNavigator xPathNavigator15 = item2.SelectSingleNode("Description");
							if (xPathNavigator15 != null)
							{
								text5 = xPathNavigator15.Value;
							}
							XPathNavigator xPathNavigator16 = item2.SelectSingleNode("ReadOnly");
							if (xPathNavigator16 != null)
							{
								flag2 = bool.Parse(xPathNavigator16.Value);
							}
							SummaryGridProperty summaryGridProperty = new SummaryGridProperty(summaryGridTab, empty, text5, empty2);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
							{
								summaryGridProperty.CustomName = text2;
							}
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								summaryGridProperty.UnderlyingPath = text4;
							}
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
							{
								summaryGridProperty.CustomCaption = text3;
							}
							if (flag2.HasValue)
							{
								summaryGridProperty.ReadOnly = flag2.Value;
							}
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(summaryGridProperty.CxsPropertyPath) && summaryGridProperty.CxsPropertyPath.StartsWith("#", StringComparison.Ordinal))
							{
								summaryGridProperty.ReadOnly = true;
							}
							if (usesUnits.HasValue)
							{
								summaryGridProperty.UsesUnits = usesUnits;
							}
							summaryGridProperty.IsCurrency = isCurrency;
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
							{
								text5 = text3;
							}
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
							{
								text5 = text2;
							}
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
							{
								text5 = empty;
							}
							summaryGridProperty.VariableDescription = text5;
							summaryGridTab.ObjProps.Add(summaryGridProperty);
						}
					}
					XPathNodeIterator xPathNodeIterator = item.Select("Rows/Row");
					bool flag3 = false;
					foreach (XPathNavigator item3 in xPathNodeIterator)
					{
						string text6 = string.Empty;
						string text7 = string.Empty;
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator17 = item3.SelectSingleNode("Name");
						if (xPathNavigator17 != null)
						{
							text6 = xPathNavigator17.Value;
						}
						XPathNavigator xPathNavigator18 = item3.SelectSingleNode("Path");
						if (xPathNavigator18 != null)
						{
							text7 = xPathNavigator18.Value;
						}
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7) || curCase == null || useProvidedRowInfo || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text6) || summaryGridTab.ReadRowInfoFromXml)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (text6.Contains("."))
						{
							text7 = text7.Replace("." + text6, ".\"" + text6 + "\"");
						}
						try
						{
							object obj7 = curCase.InvokeAction("Top.SummaryGrid", "GetContainerInfo", new object[2] { text7, text6 });
							if (obj7 != null)
							{
								if (obj7.ToString().EndsWith("\t", StringComparison.Ordinal))
								{
									obj7 = obj7.ToString().Substring(0, obj7.ToString().Length - 1);
								}
								string[] array = obj7.ToString().Split("\t".ToCharArray());
								if (array.Length == 4)
								{
									summaryGridTab.Name = array[1];
									summaryGridTab.FlowSheetName = array[2];
									summaryGridTab.Caption = array[3];
									flag3 = true;
									break;
								}
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
					}
					if (!flag3)
					{
						summaryGridTab.ReadRowInfoFromXml = true;
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(filter))
					{
						summaryGridTab.IsResultsFilterOn = true;
						summaryGridTab.ResultsFilter = filter;
					}
					bool flag4 = false;
					foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in summaryGridTemplate.SGTabs)
					{
						if (sGTab != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sGTab.Caption) && summaryGridTab != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(summaryGridTab.Caption) && string.Compare(sGTab.Caption, summaryGridTab.Caption, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
						{
							flag4 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag4)
					{
						summaryGridTemplate.SGTabs.Add(summaryGridTab);
					}
				}
				summaryGridTemplate.IsDirty = false;
				return summaryGridTemplate;
			}

			public static void ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(string elementName)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(Global.GetString("TabbedSummaryGridSchema_UnexpectedElementInGridConfigXml"));
				stringBuilder.Append(" ");
				stringBuilder.Append(elementName);
				throw new ArgumentException(stringBuilder.ToString());
			}
		}

		internal SummaryGridControl SelectedSummaryGrid
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(_tabControl.SelectedItem is TabItemEx { HasContent: not false } tabItemEx))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return tabItemEx.Content as SummaryGridControl;
			}
		}

		[Category("Configuration")]
		[Description("Running Case")]
		public object RunningCase
		{
			get
			{
				return _RunningCase;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_RunningCase != value)
				{
					_RunningCase = value;
					OnPropertyChanged("RunningCase");
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Runtime")]
		[Description("The name of the currently selected tab")]
		public string SelectedTab
		{
			get
			{
				string result = string.Empty;
				try
				{
					if (_tabControl != null && _tabControl.SelectedItem != null)
					{
						string fullName = (_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header.GetType().FullName;
						if (!(fullName == "System.String"))
						{
							if (fullName == "System.Windows.Controls.TextBlock")
							{
								result = ((_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header as TextBlock).Text.ToString();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							result = (_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header.ToString();
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				return result;
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
					{
						return;
					}
					string a = null;
					if (_tabControl == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
					{
						string fullName = item.Header.GetType().FullName;
						if (!(fullName == "System.String"))
						{
							if (fullName == "System.Windows.Controls.TextBlock")
							{
								a = (item.Header as TextBlock).Text.ToString();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							a = item.Header.ToString();
						}
						if (string.Equals(a, value, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
						{
							_tabControl.SelectedItem = item;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Runtime")]
		[Description("The index of the currently selected tab")]
		public int SelectedTabIndex
		{
			get
			{
				int result = -1;
				try
				{
					if (_tabControl != null && _tabControl.SelectedItem != null)
					{
						result = _tabControl.SelectedIndex;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				return result;
			}
			set
			{
				try
				{
					if (_tabControl != null)
					{
						int count = _tabControl.Items.Count;
						if (value < count && value >= 0)
						{
							_tabControl.SelectedIndex = value;
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Configuration")]
		[Description("The Cxs Case")]
		public CxsCase Case
		{
			get
			{
				return _Case;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_Case != value)
				{
					if (_Case != null && _DisposeCase)
					{
						_Case.Dispose();
					}
					_Case = value;
					_DisposeCase = false;
					UpdateSgTemplateCases();
					OnPropertyChanged("Case");
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Configuration")]
		[Description("Xml representation of the summary grid configuration")]
		public string Configuration
		{
			get
			{
				return _Configuration;
			}
			set
			{
				bool flag = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_Configuration);
				bool flag2 = isXmlBlank(value);
				if (_Configuration != value)
				{
					_Configuration = (flag2 ? string.Empty : value);
					if (flag && !flag2 && UseProvidedRowInfo)
					{
						FillProvidedRowInfo(_Configuration);
					}
					if (!_isUpdatingConfigInternally)
					{
						SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = null;
						try
						{
							if (!flag2)
							{
								string text = SingleObjectToDisplay;
								if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
								{
									text = Regex.Escape(text);
									text = "^" + text + "$";
								}
								summaryGridTemplate = ConfigSchema.GetSummaryGridTemplateFromConfig(_Configuration, UseProvidedRowInfo, _Case, text);
								if (!UseProvidedRowInfo)
								{
									_providedRowInfo.Clear();
									foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in summaryGridTemplate.SGTabs)
									{
										if (sGTab.ReadRowInfoFromXml)
										{
											FillProvidedTabRowInfo(_Configuration, sGTab);
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						catch (Exception ex)
						{
							System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(Global.GetString("TabbedSummaryGrid_ErrorOccurredWhileReadingConfigXml") + " " + ex.Message, Title, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
							summaryGridTemplate = null;
						}
						if (summaryGridTemplate == null)
						{
							summaryGridTemplate = new SummaryGridTemplate(isDefaultTemplate: true);
						}
						summaryGridTemplate.IsDirty = false;
						summaryGridTemplate.Case = Case;
						_sgTemplateProvided = summaryGridTemplate.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true);
						_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate = summaryGridTemplate;
						SetCurSgTemplate(summaryGridTemplate);
						if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(summaryGridTemplate.Id))
						{
							_dicOrigSgTemplates.Remove(summaryGridTemplate.Id);
						}
						_dicOrigSgTemplates.Add(summaryGridTemplate.Id, summaryGridTemplate.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						_sgTemplateCurrent.IsDirty = false;
					}
					OnPropertyChanged("Configuration");
					if (!_isUpdatingConfigInternally)
					{
						OnNewCmbItemSelect();
					}
				}
				if (_tabControl.Items.Count > 0)
				{
					_tabControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
				}
				postTabContentMenu.Items.Clear();
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (object item2 in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
				{
					if (item2 is TabItemEx { Header: TextBlock header })
					{
						list.Add(header.Text);
					}
				}
				list.Sort();
				foreach (string item3 in list)
				{
					System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem item = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem();
					item.Header = item3;
					item.Click += delegate
					{
						SelectedTab = (string)item.Header;
					};
					postTabContentMenu.Items.Add(item);
				}
			}
		}

				}
			}
		}

		private void ReadTabConfigurations(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					if (reader.Name == "Tab")
					{
						ReadTabConfiguration(reader);
					}
					else
					{
						ConfigSchema.ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReadTabConfiguration(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			string text = string.Empty;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = null;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element || reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				switch (reader.Name)
				{
				case "Caption":
					text = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					summaryGridControl = AddGridTab(text, text);
					break;
				case "Name":
					empty = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(empty) || summaryGridControl == null || _tabControl == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
					{
						if (string.CompareOrdinal(item.Header.ToString(), text) == 0)
						{
							item.Tag = text;
							break;
						}
					}
					break;
				case "Columns":
					ReadColumnConfigurations(reader, summaryGridControl.SummaryColumns);
					break;
				case "Rows":
					ReadRowConfigurations(reader, summaryGridControl.SummaryRows);
					break;
				case "Horizontal":
					summaryGridControl.UseHorizontalOrientation = ConfigSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					break;
				case "ExcludeGroupProperty":
					summaryGridControl.ExcludeGroupProperty = ConfigSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					break;
				case "ExcludeNameProperty":
					summaryGridControl.ExcludeNameProperty = ConfigSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					break;
				default:
					ConfigSchema.ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
					break;
				}
			}
			if (summaryGridControl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SummaryGridColumn summaryColumn in summaryGridControl.SummaryColumns)
			{
				if (summaryColumn == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (SummaryGridRow summaryRow in summaryGridControl.SummaryRows)
				{
					if (summaryRow != null && summaryColumn.Path == "#Name" && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(summaryRow.DetailTab))
					{
						summaryColumn.DataType = ColumnDataType.Object;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			summaryGridControl.EndInit();
		}

		private static void ReadColumnConfigurations(XmlReader reader, SummaryGridColumnCollection Columns)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					if (reader.Name == "Column")
					{
						ReadColumnConfiguration(reader, Columns);
					}
					else
					{
						ConfigSchema.ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static void ReadRowConfigurations(XmlReader reader, SummaryGridRowCollection rows)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					if (reader.Name == "Row")
					{
						ReadRowConfiguration(reader, rows);
					}
					else
					{
						ConfigSchema.ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static void ReadColumnConfiguration(XmlReader reader, SummaryGridColumnCollection columns)
		{
			SummaryGridColumn summaryGridColumn = new SummaryGridColumn();
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					switch (reader.Name)
					{
					case "Name":
						summaryGridColumn.Name = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Path":
						summaryGridColumn.Path = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "UnderlyingPath":
						summaryGridColumn.UnderlyingPath = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "PathKey":
						summaryGridColumn.PathKey = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Attribute":
						summaryGridColumn.Attribute = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Category":
						summaryGridColumn.Category = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Description":
						summaryGridColumn.Description = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Units":
						summaryGridColumn.Units = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "ReadOnly":
						summaryGridColumn.IsReadOnly = ConfigSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
						break;
					case "UsesUnits":
						summaryGridColumn.UsesUnits = ConfigSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Hidden":
						summaryGridColumn.Hidden = ConfigSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
						break;
					case "DataType":
					{
						string value = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						summaryGridColumn.DataType = (ColumnDataType)Enum.Parse(typeof(ColumnDataType), value, ignoreCase: true);
						break;
					}
					case "IsCurrency":
						summaryGridColumn.IsCurrency = ConfigSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Caption":
						summaryGridColumn.Caption = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "TypeOverrides":
						ConfigSchema.ReadDictionary(reader, summaryGridColumn.TypeOverrides);
						break;
					case "TrueValue":
						summaryGridColumn.TrueValue = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "FalseValue":
						summaryGridColumn.FalseValue = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					default:
						ConfigSchema.ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			columns.Add(summaryGridColumn);
		}

		private static void ReadRowConfiguration(XmlReader reader, SummaryGridRowCollection rows)
		{
			SummaryGridRow summaryGridRow = new SummaryGridRow();
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					switch (reader.Name)
					{
					case "Name":
						summaryGridRow.Name = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "Path":
						summaryGridRow.Path = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "UnderlyingPath":
						summaryGridRow.UnderlyingPath = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "TypeKey":
						summaryGridRow.TypeKey = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "SecondaryPaths":
						ConfigSchema.ReadDictionary(reader, summaryGridRow.SecondaryPaths);
						break;
					case "SecondaryTypeKeys":
						ConfigSchema.ReadDictionary(reader, summaryGridRow.SecondaryTypeKeys);
						break;
					case "ColumnOverrides":
						ConfigSchema.ReadDictionary(reader, summaryGridRow.ColumnOverrides);
						break;
					case "DetailTab":
						summaryGridRow.DetailTab = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					case "StatusReport":
						summaryGridRow.StatusReportPathList.Add(ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader));
						break;
					case "EEStatus":
						summaryGridRow.EEStatusPath = ConfigSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
						break;
					default:
						ConfigSchema.ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(reader.Name);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			rows.Add(summaryGridRow);
		}

		{
			InitTabbedSummaryGrid(gridSourceName, savedTemplateColXml, useProvidedRowInfo);
		}

		{
			InitTabbedSummaryGrid();
		}
