		}

		internal void MoveToLocation(string NewTabName, string Record)
		{
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0041: Expected O, but got Unknown
			SelectedTab = NewTabName;
			if (!(SelectedTab == NewTabName))
			{
				return;
			}
			XamDataGrid xamDataGrid = SelectedSummaryGrid.XamDataGrid;
			foreach (DataRecord item in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)xamDataGrid).Records)
			{
				DataRecord val = item;
				foreach (Cell item2 in (IEnumerable<Cell>)val.Cells)
				{
					if (item2 != null && item2.Value != null && item2.Value.ToString() == Record)
					{
						((DataPresenterBase)xamDataGrid).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)xamDataGrid).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)xamDataGrid).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)xamDataGrid).SelectedItems.Records.Add((Record)(object)val);
						((DataPresenterBase)xamDataGrid).ActiveRecord = (Record)(object)val;
						return;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static string GetUniqueName(string newName, List<string> WorksheetNames)
		{
			int num = 1;
			while (WorksheetNames.Contains(newName))
			{
				string pattern = "\\([0-9]\\)";
				Regex regex = new Regex(pattern);
				if (Regex.IsMatch(newName, pattern))
				{
					newName = regex.Replace(newName, "");
				}
				if (newName.Length < 29)
				{
					newName = newName.Insert(newName.Length, "(" + num + ")");
					num++;
				}
				else
				{
					newName = newName.Remove(newName.Length - 1);
				}
			}
			return newName;
		}

		private static string GenerateExcelWorksheetName(string oldName, List<string> WorksheetNames)
		{
			string text = oldName;
			text = text.Replace("\\", "");
			text = text.Replace(":", "");
			text = text.Replace("/", "");
			text = text.Replace("?", "");
			text = text.Replace("*", "");
			text = text.Replace("[", "");
			text = text.Replace("]", "");
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = " ";
			}
			if (text.Length <= 31)
			{
				return GetUniqueName(text, WorksheetNames);
			}
			text = text.Replace("Aspen ", "");
			text = text.Replace("Aspen", "");
			if (text.Length <= 31)
			{
				return GetUniqueName(text, WorksheetNames);
			}
			text = text.Replace("Hydraulics ", "");
			text = text.Replace("Hydraulics", "");
			if (text.Length <= 31)
			{
				return GetUniqueName(text, WorksheetNames);
			}
			text = text.Replace("Material Streams", "Matl Strm");
			if (text.Length <= 31)
			{
				return GetUniqueName(text, WorksheetNames);
			}
			text = text.Replace("Object", "Obj");
			if (text.Length <= 31)
			{
				return GetUniqueName(text, WorksheetNames);
			}
			text = text.Replace(" ", "");
			if (text.Length <= 31)
			{
				return GetUniqueName(text, WorksheetNames);
			}
			while (text.Length > 31)
			{
				text = text.Remove(text.Length - 1);
			}
			return GetUniqueName(text, WorksheetNames);
		}

		private static bool isXmlBlank(string value)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
			{
				return true;
			}
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			try
			{
				xmlDocument.LoadXml(value);
				if (xmlDocument.SelectSingleNode("//TabbedSummaryGrid/Tabs/Tab/Rows/Row") == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			catch
			{
				return true;
			}
		}

		private void ComboSelectionChange(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnNewCmbItemSelect();
		}

		private string GetCurXmlConfig(SummaryGridTemplate sgTpl)
		{
			if (sgTpl == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = new UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: false);
			xmlWriterSettings.ConformanceLevel = ConformanceLevel.Document;
			xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
			XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(memoryStream, xmlWriterSettings);
			xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("TabbedSummaryGrid");
			if (sgTpl.SGTabs != null && sgTpl.SGTabs.Count > 0)
			{
				xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Tabs");
				foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in sgTpl.SGTabs)
				{
					xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Tab");
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sGTab.Caption))
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Caption", sGTab.Caption);
					}
					else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sGTab.Name))
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Caption", sGTab.Name);
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sGTab.Name))
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Name", sGTab.Name);
					}
					xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Horizontal", sGTab.IsHorizontal.ToString());
					if (sGTab.ObjProps != null && sGTab.ObjProps.Count > 0)
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Columns");
						int num = 0;
						foreach (SummaryGridProperty objProp in sGTab.ObjProps)
						{
							if (objProp.Display && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.VariableDescription) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.CxsPropertyPath) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.AttributeToShow))
							{
								xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Column");
								if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.CustomName))
								{
									xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Name", "Column" + num.ToString().Trim());
								}
								else
								{
									xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Name", objProp.CustomName);
								}
								num++;
								if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.CustomCaption))
								{
									xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Caption", objProp.VariableDescription);
								}
								else
								{
									xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Caption", objProp.CustomCaption);
								}
								xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Path", objProp.CxsPropertyPath);
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.UnderlyingPath))
								{
									xmlWriter.WriteElementString("UnderlyingPath", objProp.UnderlyingPath);
								}
								xmlWriter.WriteElementString("DataType", Enum.GetName(typeof(ColumnDataType), objProp.DataType));
								xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Attribute", objProp.AttributeToShow);
								if (objProp.ReadOnly.HasValue)
								{
									xmlWriter.WriteElementString("ReadOnly", objProp.ReadOnly.ToString());
								}
								if (objProp.UsesUnits.HasValue)
								{
									xmlWriter.WriteElementString("UsesUnits", objProp.UsesUnits.ToString());
								}
								xmlWriter.WriteElementString("IsCurrency", objProp.IsCurrency.ToString());
								xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
							}
						}
						xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
					}
					string text = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sGTab.FlowSheetName) ? "*" : sGTab.FlowSheetName);
					object obj = null;
					try
					{
						obj = _Case.InvokeAction("Top.SummaryGrid", "GetObjects", new object[2] { sGTab.Name, text });
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					string[] array = new string[0];
					if (obj != null)
					{
						array = obj.ToString().Split("\t".ToCharArray(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
					}
					List<string> list = new List<string>();
					List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
					List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
					List<string> list4 = new List<string>();
					List<string> list5 = new List<string>();
					List<string[]> list6 = new List<string[]>();
					if (!UseProvidedRowInfo && !sGTab.ReadRowInfoFromXml)
					{
						if (array.Length != 0 && 2 * array.Length / 2 == array.Length)
						{
							for (int i = 0; i < array.Length / 2; i++)
							{
								if (CheckIfPathContainsObjectName(array[2 * i + 1], array[2 * i]))
								{
									list.Add(array[2 * i]);
									list2.Add(array[2 * i + 1]);
									list3.Add("");
									list4.Add(string.Empty);
									list6.Add(new string[0]);
									list5.Add("");
								}
							}
						}
					}
					else if (_providedRowInfo != null && _providedRowInfo.ContainsKey(sGTab.CaptionFromOrigXml))
					{
						foreach (SingleRowInfo item in _providedRowInfo[sGTab.CaptionFromOrigXml])
						{
							list.Add(item.Name);
							list2.Add(item.Path);
							list3.Add(item.UnderlyingPath);
							list4.Add(item.DetailTab);
							list6.Add(item.StatusReport);
							list5.Add(item.EEStatusPath);
						}
					}
					for (int num2 = list.Count - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
					{
						if (sGTab.IsResultsFilterOn && !DoesNamePassFilter(list[num2], sGTab.ResultsFilter))
						{
							list.RemoveAt(num2);
							list2.RemoveAt(num2);
							list3.RemoveAt(num2);
							list4.RemoveAt(num2);
							list6.RemoveAt(num2);
							list5.RemoveAt(num2);
						}
					}
					xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Rows");
					for (int j = 0; j < list.Count; j++)
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Row");
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Name", list[j]);
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Path", list2[j]);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(list3[j]))
						{
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("UnderlyingPath", list3[j]);
						}
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("DetailTab", list4[j]);
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("EEStatus", list5[j]);
						string[] array2 = list6[j];
						foreach (string value in array2)
						{
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("StatusReport", value);
						}
						xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
					}
					xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
					xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
				}
				xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			}
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlWriter.Flush();
			xmlWriter.Close();
			return Encoding.UTF8.GetString(memoryStream.ToArray());
		}

		private static bool DoesNamePassFilter(string name, string filter)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(filter) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result = true;
			try
			{
				result = new Regex(filter).IsMatch(name);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static bool CheckIfPathContainsObjectName(string path, string obj)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(obj) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path))
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = obj.LastIndexOf("@", StringComparison.Ordinal);
			if (num > -1)
			{
				obj = obj.Substring(0, num);
			}
			string[] array = CxsPath.Split(path);
			string strB = obj;
			string[] array2 = array;
			for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
			{
				if (string.Compare(array2[i], strB, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				string[] array3 = obj.Split(".".ToCharArray(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
				if (array3.Length == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				strB = array3[array3.Length - 1];
				array2 = array;
				for (int i = 0; i < array2.Length; i++)
				{
					if (string.Compare(array2[i], strB, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private void FillProvidedRowInfo(string configXml)
		{
			_providedRowInfo = ConfigSchema.GetRowInfoFromConfig(configXml);
		}

		private void FillProvidedTabRowInfo(string configXml, SummaryGridTab tab)
		{
			ConfigSchema.GetTabRowInfoFromConfig(ref _providedRowInfo, configXml, tab);
		}

		internal void UpdateToolbarButtons()
		{
			if (_sgTemplateCurrent != null)
			{
				if (_sgTemplateCurrent.IsDirty)
				{
					btnSave.IsEnabled = true;
					btnReset.IsEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					btnSave.IsEnabled = false;
					btnReset.IsEnabled = false;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void PasteVariables(bool IsFromClipboard = true, DropVarsInfo DropInfo = null, bool SuppressDialogs = false)
		{
			if (_sgTemplateCurrent == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string selectedTabName = string.Empty;
			if (_tabControl != null && _tabControl.SelectedItem is TabItemEx && (_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header != null && (_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header is TextBlock)
			{
				selectedTabName = ((_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header as TextBlock).Text;
			}
			List<string> list = _sgTemplateCurrent.Paste(SuppressDialogs, selectedTabName, IsFromClipboard, DropInfo);
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				string curXmlConfig = GetCurXmlConfig(_sgTemplateCurrent);
				_isUpdatingConfigInternally = true;
				Configuration = curXmlConfig;
				_isUpdatingConfigInternally = false;
				foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
				{
					if (string.CompareOrdinal((item.Header as TextBlock).Text, list[list.Count - 1]) == 0)
					{
						_tabControl.SelectedItem = item;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			UpdateComboboxNames();
			UpdateToolbarButtons();
		}

		protected void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && _Case != null && _DisposeCase)
			{
				_Case.Dispose();
			}
		}

		private void InitTabbedSummaryGrid(string gridSourceName = "", string savedTemplateColXml = "", bool useProvidedRowInfo = false)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			GridSourceName = gridSourceName;
			UseProvidedRowInfo = useProvidedRowInfo;
			_sgCol = new SummaryGridTemplateCollection(_sgTemplateProvided, savedTemplateColXml);
			if (_sgCol != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null)
			{
				foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
				{
					if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id))
					{
						_dicOrigSgTemplates.Remove(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id);
					}
					_dicOrigSgTemplates.Add(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id, lstSavedSumGridTemplate.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
				}
			}
			PopulateTemplateList();
			int selectedIndex = 0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_sgCol.TemplateSetAsCurrentInXml))
			{
				foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate2 in _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
				{
					if (string.Compare(lstSavedSumGridTemplate2.Name, _sgCol.TemplateSetAsCurrentInXml, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					for (int i = 1; i < cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Count; i++)
					{
						SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i - 1];
						if (summaryGridTemplate != null && summaryGridTemplate.Name == _sgCol.TemplateSetAsCurrentInXml)
						{
							selectedIndex = i;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
			_respondToTemplateSelection = true;
		}

		private void btns_OnSendToExcelClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (_tabControl.Items.Count == 0)
			{
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(Strings.TabbedSummaryGrid_NothingToExport, Strings.TabbedSummaryGrid_ErrorExport, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Hand);
				return;
			}
			Export2DTemplate exportTemplate = GetExportTemplate();
			if (exportTemplate.Tables.Where((Export2DTemplate.Table2D t) => t.FieldLabels.Length == 0).Any())
			{
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(Strings.TabbedSummaryGrid_MissingFields, Strings.TabbedSummaryGrid_ErrorExport, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Hand);
				return;
			}
			if (_Case != null)
			{
				exportTemplate.Case = _Case;
				exportTemplate.CasePath = _Case.CaseFullFilePath;
				exportTemplate.ApplicationName = _Case.ApplicationName;
			}
			exportTemplate.Owner = Window.GetWindow(this);
			exportTemplate.RunningCase = RunningCase;
			try
			{
				exportTemplate.OpenDialog(Strings.TabbedSummaryGridButtons_SendToExcel);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private void btns_OnSaveClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SaveCurrentTemplate();
		}

		private void btns_OnSaveAsNewClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SaveAsCurrentTemplate();
		}

		private void btns_OnResetClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RevertCurrentTemplate();
		}

		private void btns_OnPasteClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			PasteVariables();
		}

		private void PopulateTemplateList()
		{
			_isResettingTplCmbList = true;
			try
			{
				cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Clear();
				cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Add(_defaultSgCaption);
				cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = 0;
				if (_sgCol != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
					{
						string nameWithDirtyMarker = _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].NameWithDirtyMarker;
						cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Add(nameWithDirtyMarker);
					}
				}
				cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Add(_manageSgCaption);
			}
			finally
			{
				_isResettingTplCmbList = false;
			}
		}

		private void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			switch (propertyName)
			{
			case "RunningCase":
				if (_Case != null)
				{
					DisconnectFromRunningCase();
				}
				if (_RunningCase != null && (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CasePath) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ApplicationName)))
				{
					ConnectToRunningCase();
				}
				break;
			case "CasePath":
				if (_Case == null)
				{
					if (_RunningCase != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CasePath))
					{
						ConnectToRunningCase();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_Case.CaseFilePath = _CasePath;
				}
				break;
			case "ApplicationName":
				if (_Case == null)
				{
					if (_RunningCase != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ApplicationName))
					{
						ConnectToRunningCase();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_Case.ApplicationRequirement = _ApplicationName;
				}
				break;
			case "Configuration":
				ReconfigureSummaryTabs();
				break;
			case "Case":
				PropogateCaseToTabs();
				break;
			}
		}

		private SummaryGridControl AddGridTab(string tabName, string tabKey = "")
		{
			SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = new SummaryGridControl();
			summaryGridControl.BeginInit();
			summaryGridControl.Case = _Case;
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tabKey))
			{
				tabKey = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Tab{0}", _tabControl.Items.Count);
			}
			TabItemEx tabItemEx = new TabItemEx();
			tabItemEx.Style = (Style)FindResource("TabItemExStyle");
			try
			{
				tabItemEx.Header = new TextBlock
				{
					Text = tabName,
					FontWeight = FontWeights.Normal,
					HorizontalAlignment = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Stretch,
					VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Stretch
				};
				tabItemEx.Tag = tabKey;
				_tabControl.Items.Add(tabItemEx);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (tabItemEx == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			tabItemEx.Content = summaryGridControl;
			summaryGridControl.TabbedSummaryGrid = this;
			return summaryGridControl;
		}

		private void ReconfigureSummaryTabs()
		{
			_tabControl.Items.Clear();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Configuration))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				string curXmlConfig = GetCurXmlConfig(_sgTemplateCurrent);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(curXmlConfig))
				{
					return;
				}
				XmlTextReader xmlTextReader = new XmlTextReader(new StringReader(curXmlConfig));
				bool flag = false;
				while (xmlTextReader.Read())
				{
					if (xmlTextReader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && xmlTextReader.Name == "TabbedSummaryGrid")
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("TabbedSummaryGrid_CouldNotFindRootElement"));
				}
				while (xmlTextReader.Read())
				{
					if (xmlTextReader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
					{
						if (xmlTextReader.Name == "Tabs")
						{
							ReadTabConfigurations(xmlTextReader);
						}
						else
						{
							ConfigSchema.ThrowExceptionUnexpectedGridXml(xmlTextReader.Name);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (ArgumentException ex)
			{
				Trace.WriteLine(string.Format(" XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX -----> Exception parsing configuration {0}", ex.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal));
			}
		}

		private void DisconnectFromRunningCase()
		{
			CxsCase val = _Case;
			_Case = null;
			PropogateCaseToTabs();
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.Dispose();
			}
		}

		private void ConnectToRunningCase()
		{
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0051: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (_RunningCase == null || (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_CasePath) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_ApplicationName)))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_Case != null)
			{
				if (_DisposeCase)
				{
					_Case.Dispose();
				}
				_Case = null;
			}
			try
			{
				_Case = new CxsCase();
				_DisposeCase = true;
				_Case.CaseFilePath = _CasePath;
				_Case.ApplicationRequirement = _ApplicationName;
				_Case.AttachToRunningCase(_RunningCase);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			if (_Case != null)
			{
				if (_Case.IsConnected)
				{
					PropogateCaseToTabs();
					return;
				}
				_Case.Dispose();
				_Case = null;
			}
		}

		private void PropogateCaseToTabs()
		{
			foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
			{
				if (item.HasContent && item.Content is SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl)
				{
					summaryGridControl.Case = _Case;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateConfigForNewSgCurTemplate()
		{
			string curXmlConfig = GetCurXmlConfig(_sgTemplateCurrent);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(curXmlConfig))
			{
				_isUpdatingConfigInternally = true;
				Configuration = curXmlConfig;
				_isUpdatingConfigInternally = false;
				UpdateToolbarButtons();
			}
		}

		private void SaveCurrentTemplate()
		{
			if (cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			int selectedIndex = cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex;
			if (selectedIndex == 0)
			{
				SaveAsCurrentTemplate();
				return;
			}
			if (_sgCol != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count > cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex - 1)
			{
				_sgTemplateCurrent.IsDirty = false;
				_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex - 1] = _sgTemplateCurrent;
				if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(_sgTemplateCurrent.Id))
				{
					_dicOrigSgTemplates.Remove(_sgTemplateCurrent.Id);
				}
				_dicOrigSgTemplates.Add(_sgTemplateCurrent.Id, _sgTemplateCurrent.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
				UpdateConfigForNewSgCurTemplate();
				RefreshData();
			}
			PopulateTemplateList();
			cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
			UpdateToolbarButtons();
			TriggerOnSummaryGridTplColChange();
		}

		private void SaveAsCurrentTemplate(SummaryGridTemplate sgTplToSaveAs = null)
		{
			if (_sgCol == null || _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (sgTplToSaveAs == null)
			{
				if (cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex == -1)
				{
					return;
				}
				sgTplToSaveAs = ((cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex != 0) ? _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex - 1] : _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate);
			}
			if (sgTplToSaveAs == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = null;
			Guid id = sgTplToSaveAs.Id;
			summaryGridTemplate = sgTplToSaveAs.GetCopy();
			summaryGridTemplate.Name = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sgTplToSaveAs.Name) ? _sgCol.GetNewTemplateName() : _sgCol.GetNewTemplateName(summaryGridTemplate.Name));
			summaryGridTemplate.IsDirty = false;
			_dicOrigSgTemplates[summaryGridTemplate.Id] = summaryGridTemplate.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true);
			Guid id2 = summaryGridTemplate.Id;
			new ManageSummaryGridTemplatesCtl(_sgCol, summaryGridTemplate, _dicOrigSgTemplates).ShowDialog();
			PopulateTemplateList();
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
			{
				if (lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id == id2)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			int selectedIndex = 0;
			if (flag)
			{
				RevertCurrentTemplate(sgTplToSaveAs);
			}
			Guid guid = (flag ? id2 : id);
			for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == guid)
				{
					selectedIndex = i + 1;
					break;
				}
			}
			cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
			UpdateConfigForNewSgCurTemplate();
			UpdateToolbarButtons();
			OnNewCmbItemSelect();
			TriggerOnSummaryGridTplColChange();
		}

		private void RevertCurrentTemplate(SummaryGridTemplate sgToRevert = null)
		{
			if (sgToRevert == null)
			{
				sgToRevert = _sgTemplateCurrent;
			}
			if (sgToRevert == null || !_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(sgToRevert.Id))
			{
				return;
			}
			SetCurSgTemplate(_dicOrigSgTemplates[_sgTemplateCurrent.Id].GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
			if (_sgCol != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null)
			{
				if (_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate != null && _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate.Id == _sgTemplateCurrent.Id)
				{
					_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate = _sgTemplateCurrent;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
					{
						if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == _sgTemplateCurrent.Id)
						{
							_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] = _sgTemplateCurrent;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			PopulateTemplateList();
			SelectSumGridTplInCombobox(_sgTemplateCurrent.Id);
			UpdateToolbarButtons();
		}

		private void SelectSumGridTplInCombobox(Guid guidToSelect)
		{
			int num = -1;
			if (_sgCol == null || _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate != null && guidToSelect == _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate.Id)
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			else
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
				{
					if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == guidToSelect)
					{
						num = i + 1;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = num;
			}
		}

		private bool RevertSpecifiedSgTemplate(ref SummaryGridTemplate sgTpl)
		{
			if (_dicOrigSgTemplates == null || sgTpl == null || !_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(sgTpl.Id) || _dicOrigSgTemplates[sgTpl.Id] == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			sgTpl = _dicOrigSgTemplates[sgTpl.Id].GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true);
			if (_sgCol != null)
			{
				if (_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate != null && _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate.Id == sgTpl.Id)
				{
					_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate = sgTpl;
					return true;
				}
				if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
					{
						if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == sgTpl.Id)
						{
							_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] = sgTpl;
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool SaveSpecifiedSgTemplate(SummaryGridTemplate sgTpl)
		{
			if (_dicOrigSgTemplates == null || sgTpl == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			sgTpl.IsDirty = false;
			if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(sgTpl.Id))
			{
				_dicOrigSgTemplates.Remove(sgTpl.Id);
			}
			_dicOrigSgTemplates.Add(sgTpl.Id, sgTpl.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
			if (_sgCol != null)
			{
				if (_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate != null && _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate.Id == sgTpl.Id)
				{
					_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate = sgTpl;
					return true;
				}
				if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
					{
						if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == sgTpl.Id)
						{
							_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] = sgTpl;
							return true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void UpdateComboboxNames()
		{
			if (cmbSumGridTemplates == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool isResettingTplCmbList = _isResettingTplCmbList;
			_isResettingTplCmbList = true;
			try
			{
				if (cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Count > 0 && _sgCol != null && _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate != null)
				{
					int selectedIndex = cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex;
					cmbSumGridTemplates.Items[0] = (_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate.IsDirty ? _defaultSgCaptionDirty : _defaultSgCaption);
					cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
				}
				if (_sgCol == null || _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
				{
					if (cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Count > i + 1)
					{
						int selectedIndex2 = cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex;
						cmbSumGridTemplates.Items[i + 1] = (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].IsDirty ? _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].NameWithDirtyMarker : _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Name);
						cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex2;
					}
					else
					{
						int selectedIndex3 = cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex;
						string newItem = (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].IsDirty ? _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].NameWithDirtyMarker : _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Name);
						cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Add(newItem);
						cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex3;
					}
				}
				if (cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Count > 1 + _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count)
				{
					cmbSumGridTemplates.Items[1 + _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count] = _manageSgCaption;
				}
				else
				{
					cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Add(_manageSgCaption);
				}
				while (cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Count > _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count + 2)
				{
					cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.RemoveAt(cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Count - 1);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				_isResettingTplCmbList = isResettingTplCmbList;
			}
		}

		private void SetCurSgTemplate(SummaryGridTemplate curSgTpl)
		{
			_sgTemplateCurrent = curSgTpl;
			UpdateConfigForNewSgCurTemplate();
		}

		private void UpdateSgTemplateCases()
		{
			if (_sgCol == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
			{
				lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Case = Case;
			}
		}

		private void OnNewCmbItemSelect()
		{
			if (!_respondToTemplateSelection || _isResettingTplCmbList || _sgCol == null || _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates == null || cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedItem == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (string.Compare(cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedItem.ToString(), _manageSgCaption, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
			{
				ManageSummaryGridTemplatesCtl manageSummaryGridTemplatesCtl = new ManageSummaryGridTemplatesCtl(_sgCol, _dicOrigSgTemplates);
				manageSummaryGridTemplatesCtl.ShowDialog();
				if (manageSummaryGridTemplatesCtl.SummaryGridCollectionChanged)
				{
					TriggerOnSummaryGridTplColChange();
				}
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
				{
					if (_sgTemplateCurrent != null && _sgTemplateCurrent.Id == lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id)
					{
						flag = true;
						SetCurSgTemplate(lstSavedSumGridTemplate);
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					SetCurSgTemplate(_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate);
				}
				PopulateTemplateList();
				int num = -1;
				if (_sgTemplateCurrent != null && _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate != null && _sgTemplateCurrent.Id == _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate.Id)
				{
					num = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
					{
						if (_sgTemplateCurrent != null && _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == _sgTemplateCurrent.Id)
						{
							num = i + 1;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (num == -1)
				{
					num = 0;
				}
				cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = num;
				RefreshData();
				TriggerOnSummaryGridTplColChange();
				UpdateToolbarButtons();
				return;
			}
			bool flag2 = false;
			SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = null;
			if (cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex == 0)
			{
				if (_sgCol != null)
				{
					summaryGridTemplate = _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate;
				}
			}
			else if (_sgCol != null)
			{
				summaryGridTemplate = _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex - 1];
			}
			if (summaryGridTemplate != null && _sgTemplateCurrent != null && summaryGridTemplate.Id == _sgTemplateCurrent.Id)
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (!flag2 && _sgTemplateCurrent != null && _sgTemplateCurrent.IsDirty)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(Global.GetString("TabbedSummaryGrid_PrevTemplateHadUnsavedChanges"));
				stringBuilder.Append("\r\n\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append(Global.GetString("TabbedSummaryGrid_SaveChangesToPrevTemplate"));
				DialogResult dialogResult = System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(stringBuilder.ToString(), SummaryGridTemplate.MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question);
				int num2 = -1;
				if (_sgTemplateCurrent.Id == _sgTemplateProvided.Id)
				{
					num2 = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; j++)
					{
						if (_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[j].Id == _sgTemplateCurrent.Id)
						{
							num2 = j + 1;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					dialogResult = DialogResult.None;
				}
				switch (dialogResult)
				{
				default:
					return;
				case DialogResult.Cancel:
				{
					bool respondToTemplateSelection = _respondToTemplateSelection;
					_respondToTemplateSelection = false;
					cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = num2;
					_respondToTemplateSelection = respondToTemplateSelection;
					UpdateToolbarButtons();
					return;
				}
				case DialogResult.No:
					RevertSpecifiedSgTemplate(ref _sgTemplateCurrent);
					break;
				case DialogResult.Yes:
					if (_sgTemplateCurrent.Id != _sgTemplateProvided.Id)
					{
						SaveSpecifiedSgTemplate(_sgTemplateCurrent);
					}
					else
					{
						SaveAsCurrentTemplate(_sgTemplateCurrent);
					}
					break;
				case DialogResult.None:
					break;
				}
			}
			UpdateComboboxNames();
			if (summaryGridTemplate != null)
			{
				SetCurSgTemplate(summaryGridTemplate);
				RefreshData();
			}
			TriggerOnSummaryGridTplColChange();
			UpdateToolbarButtons();
		}

		public void SetSavedSummaryGridTemplates(string sumGridXml)
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate defaultSumGridTemplate = null;
			if (_sgCol != null && _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate != null)
			{
				defaultSumGridTemplate = _sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate;
			}
			_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Clear();
			_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate = defaultSumGridTemplate;
			SummaryGridTemplateCollection summaryGridTemplateCollection = new SummaryGridTemplateCollection(_sgCol.DefaultSumGridTemplate, sumGridXml);
			foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in summaryGridTemplateCollection.LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
			{
				lstSavedSumGridTemplate.IsDirty = false;
				if (Case != null)
				{
					lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Case = Case;
				}
				_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Add(lstSavedSumGridTemplate);
				if (_dicOrigSgTemplates.ContainsKey(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id))
				{
					_dicOrigSgTemplates.Remove(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id);
				}
				_dicOrigSgTemplates.Add(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id, lstSavedSumGridTemplate.GetCopy(copyIdAsWell: true));
			}
			int num = 0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(summaryGridTemplateCollection.TemplateSetAsCurrentInXml))
			{
				for (int i = 1; i < _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
				{
					if (string.Compare(_sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Name, summaryGridTemplateCollection.TemplateSetAsCurrentInXml, StringComparison.Ordinal) == 0)
					{
						num = i + 1;
					}
				}
			}
			PopulateTemplateList();
			if (cmbSumGridTemplates.Items.Count > num)
			{
				_respondToTemplateSelection = false;
				cmbSumGridTemplates.SelectedIndex = num;
			}
			_respondToTemplateSelection = true;
		}

		private Export2DTemplate GetExportTemplate()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			Export2DTemplate export2DTemplate = new Export2DTemplate();
			foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
			{
				string oldName = (item.Header as TextBlock).Text.ToString();
				oldName = GenerateExcelWorksheetName(oldName, list);
				list.Add(oldName);
				string displayName = (item.Header as TextBlock).Text.ToString();
				SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = null;
				SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl2 = item.Content as SummaryGridControl;
				if (item != null && item.Content != null && item.HasContent && summaryGridControl2 != null)
				{
					summaryGridControl = summaryGridControl2;
				}
				if (summaryGridControl != null)
				{
					string[] FieldLabels = null;
					List<Export2DTemplate.Record> Records = new List<Export2DTemplate.Record>();
					summaryGridControl.GetSummaryGridExportData(ref FieldLabels, ref Records);
					Export2DTemplate.Table2D table2D = new Export2DTemplate.Table2D
					{
						FieldLabels = FieldLabels,
						Records = Records,
						Name = oldName,
						DisplayName = displayName
					};
					if (summaryGridControl.UseHorizontalOrientation)
					{
						table2D.Orientation = Export2DTemplate.Orientation.Horizontal;
					}
					else
					{
						table2D.Orientation = Export2DTemplate.Orientation.Vertical;
					}
					export2DTemplate.Tables.Add(table2D);
				}
			}
			return export2DTemplate;
		}

		public SummaryGridTab GetCurSumGridTab()
		{
			if (_sgTemplateCurrent != null)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (_tabControl != null && _tabControl.SelectedItem != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header.ToString()))
				{
					text = ((_tabControl.SelectedItem as TabItemEx).Header as TextBlock).Text.ToString();
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					return null;
				}
				foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in _sgTemplateCurrent.SGTabs)
				{
					if (string.CompareOrdinal(sGTab.Caption, text) == 0)
					{
						return sGTab;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public string[] GetTabNames()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			try
			{
				if (_tabControl != null)
				{
					foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
					{
						if (item.Header is TextBlock textBlock)
						{
							list.Add(textBlock.Text.ToString());
						}
						else
						{
							list.Add(item.Header.ToString());
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

			return false;
		}

		}

		public void RefreshData()
		{
			foreach (TabItemEx item in (IEnumerable)_tabControl.Items)
			{
				if (item.HasContent && item.Content is SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl)
				{
					summaryGridControl.RefreshDisplay(resetDataCache: true);
				}
			}
		}

		}

			SelectedSummaryGrid?.PasteFromClipboard();
		}

		{
			_ = SelectedSummaryGrid;
		}

		public void SetTemplatesFromCase(string templateKey)
		{
			if (_Case == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				object obj = _Case.InvokeAction("Top.SummaryGrid", "GetSummaryGridTplXml", new object[1] { templateKey });
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(obj.ToString()))
				{
					return;
				}
				text = obj.ToString();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				SetSavedSummaryGridTemplates(text);
			}
		}

		public void SaveTemplatesToCase(string templateKey)
		{
			if (_Case == null || _sgCol == null || _sgCol.LstSavedSumGridTemplates == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SummaryGridTplCol))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				_Case.InvokeAction("Top.SummaryGrid", "SetSummaryGridTplXml", new object[2] { templateKey, SummaryGridTplCol });
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private void _tabControl_SelectionChange(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.AddedItems.Count == 0 || !(e.AddedItems[0] is TabItemEx tabItemEx))
			{
				return;
			}
			OnSelectedTabChanged();
			SubscribeToSelectionChangedEvent();
			string fullName = tabItemEx.Header.GetType().FullName;
			if (!(fullName == "System.String"))
			{
				if (fullName == "System.Windows.Controls.TextBlock")
				{
					(tabItemEx.Header as TextBlock).FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				tabItemEx.Header = new TextBlock
				{
					Text = tabItemEx.Header.ToString(),
					FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold
				};
			}
			if (e.RemovedItems.Count == 0 || !(e.RemovedItems[0] is TabItemEx tabItemEx2))
			{
				return;
			}
			fullName = tabItemEx2.Header.GetType().FullName;
			if (!(fullName == "System.String"))
			{
				if (fullName == "System.Windows.Controls.TextBlock")
				{
					(tabItemEx2.Header as TextBlock).FontWeight = FontWeights.Normal;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				tabItemEx2.Header = new TextBlock
				{
					Text = tabItemEx2.Header.ToString(),
					FontWeight = FontWeights.Normal
				};
			}
		}

		private void SubscribeToSelectionChangedEvent()
		{
			if (_activeSummaryGrid != null)
			{
				_activeSummaryGrid.SelectedItemsChanged -= _activeSummaryGrid_SelectedItemsChanged;
			}
			_activeSummaryGrid = SelectedSummaryGrid;
			if (_activeSummaryGrid != null)
			{
				_activeSummaryGrid.SelectedItemsChanged += _activeSummaryGrid_SelectedItemsChanged;
			}
		}

		private void _activeSummaryGrid_SelectedItemsChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnSelectionChanged();
		}

		private void OnSelectedTabChanged()
		{
			if (this.SelectedTabChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectedTabChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void OnSelectionChanged()
		{
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void TabbedSummaryGrid_LostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsMouseOver && _activeSummaryGrid != null && _activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid != null && ((DataPresenterBase)_activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid).ActiveCell != null && ((DataPresenterBase)_activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid).ActiveCell.IsInEditMode)
			{
				((DataPresenterBase)_activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid).ActiveCell.EndEditMode();
			}
		}

		private void TabbedSummaryGrid_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.IsMouseOver && _activeSummaryGrid != null && _activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid != null && ((DataPresenterBase)_activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid).ActiveCell != null && ((DataPresenterBase)_activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid).ActiveCell.IsInEditMode)
			{
				((DataPresenterBase)_activeSummaryGrid._summaryGrid).ActiveCell.EndEditMode();
			}
		}

	internal class VariableInfo
	{
		private static string sSubobjects = ".Subobjects.";

		private static string sInput = "Input";

		private static string sOutput = "Output";

		public string Container { get; private set; }

		public string ContainerCategory { get; private set; }

		public string ContainerType { get; private set; }

		public string ContainerDisplayName { get; private set; }

		public string PropertyPath { get; private set; }

		public string ContainerPath { get; private set; }

		public string FlowSheetName { get; private set; }

		public string PropertyDisplayName { get; private set; }

		public bool Error { get; private set; }

		public string ErrorDesc { get; private set; }

		public CxsCase Case { get; private set; }

		public string Path { get; private set; }

		public string ContainerUniqueName { get; private set; }

		public VariableInfo(CxsCase simulationCase, string variablePath, string selectedTabName)
		{
			if (simulationCase == null)
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = Global.GetString("VariableInfo_InvalidSimulationCase");
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(variablePath))
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = Global.GetString("VariableInfo_InvalidVariablePath");
			}
			else
			{
				Case = simulationCase;
				Path = variablePath;
				GetVariableValues(selectedTabName);
			}
		}

		private bool IsSubObjectsNode(CxsNode cxsNode)
		{
			if (cxsNode != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(cxsNode.Path) && cxsNode.Path.Contains(sSubobjects))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private string SubObjectsDisplayNameSegment(string sNodePath, string sInputOrOutputSegment)
		{
			int num = sNodePath.IndexOf(sInputOrOutputSegment);
			return sNodePath.Substring(num + sInputOrOutputSegment.Length + 1).Replace(".", "_");
		}

		private string SubObjectDisplayName(CxsNode cxsNode)
		{
			if (cxsNode == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (!IsSubObjectsNode(cxsNode))
			{
				return cxsNode.Name;
			}
			string path = cxsNode.Path;
			if (path.Contains(sInput))
			{
				return SubObjectsDisplayNameSegment(path, sInput);
			}
			if (path.Contains(sOutput))
			{
				return SubObjectsDisplayNameSegment(path, sOutput);
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		private void GetVariableValues(string selectedTabName = "")
		{
			Error = false;
			ErrorDesc = string.Empty;
			CxsNode val = Case.ResolveNode(Path);
			if (val == null)
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = Global.GetString("VariableInfo_InvalidPath");
				return;
			}
			Container = GetContainerPath(Case, Path);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Container))
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = Global.GetString("VariableInfo_InvalidContainer");
				return;
			}
			object obj = (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(selectedTabName) ? Case.InvokeAction("Top.SummaryGrid", "GetContainerInfo", new object[2]
			{
				Container,
				TabbedSummaryGrid.CFlagIsPaste
			}) : Case.InvokeAction("Top.SummaryGrid", "GetContainerInfo", new object[3]
			{
				Container,
				TabbedSummaryGrid.CFlagIsPaste,
				TabbedSummaryGrid.CFlagCurSelectedTab + selectedTabName
			}));
			string[] array = new string[0];
			if (obj != null)
			{
				array = obj.ToString().Split("\t".ToCharArray(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			}
			if (obj == null || array.Length != 4)
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = Global.GetString("VariableInfo_ErrorRetrievingPathInfoFromCase");
				return;
			}
			ContainerCategory = array[0];
			ContainerType = array[1];
			FlowSheetName = array[2];
			ContainerDisplayName = array[3];
			ContainerUniqueName = ContainerType;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(FlowSheetName) && FlowSheetName != "*")
			{
				ContainerUniqueName += "@";
				ContainerUniqueName += FlowSheetName;
			}
			PropertyDisplayName = ((!IsSubObjectsNode(val)) ? val.Name : SubObjectDisplayName(val));
			string text = val.PathFromContainer;
			if (string.Compare(val.Path, text, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				string[] array2 = Container.Split(".".ToCharArray(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
				if (array2.Length != 0)
				{
					string strB = array2[array2.Length - 1];
					StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
					bool flag = false;
					string[] array3 = text.Split(".".ToCharArray(), StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
					for (int i = 0; i < array3.Length; i++)
					{
						if (flag)
						{
							if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(".");
							}
							stringBuilder.Append(array3[i]);
						}
						else if (string.Compare(array3[i], strB, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					text = stringBuilder.ToString();
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(val.Container))
			{
				text = text.Replace(val.Container + ".", "");
			}
			PropertyPath = text;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Container))
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = Global.GetString("VariableInfo_InvalidContainer");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ContainerType))
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = "Invalid ContainerType";
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PropertyDisplayName))
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = "Invalid PropertyDisplayName";
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PropertyPath))
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = "Invalid PropertyPath";
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ContainerDisplayName))
			{
				Error = true;
				ErrorDesc = "Invalid ContainerDisplayName";
			}
		}

		private static string GetContainerPath(CxsCase curCase, string origPath)
		{
			string text = origPath;
			CxsNode val = null;
			try
			{
				val = curCase.ResolveNode(text);
			}
			catch
			{
				val = null;
			}
			while (val != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				if (val == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (val.IsContainer)
				{
					return text;
				}
				text = val.ParentPath;
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
				{
					try
					{
						val = curCase.ResolveNode(text);
					}
					catch
					{
						val = null;
					}
				}
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}
	}
	internal class SingleRowInfo
	{
		public string EEStatusPath { get; set; }

		public string DetailTab { get; set; }

		public string Name { get; set; }

		public string Path { get; set; }

		public string UnderlyingPath { get; set; }

		public string[] StatusReport { get; set; }

		public SingleRowInfo()
		{
		}

		public SingleRowInfo(string name, string path, string underlyingPath, string detailtab = "", string[] statusReport = null, string eeStatusPath = "")
		{
			Name = name;
			Path = path;
			UnderlyingPath = underlyingPath;
			DetailTab = detailtab;
			EEStatusPath = eeStatusPath;
			if (statusReport == null)
			{
				StatusReport = new string[0];
			}
			else
			{
				StatusReport = statusReport;
			}
		}
	}
	public class RelayCommand : ICommand
	{
		private readonly Action<object> _execute;

		private readonly Predicate<object> _canExecute;

		public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged
		{
			add
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				CommandManager.RequerySuggested -= value;
			}
		}

		public RelayCommand(Action<object> execute)
			: this(execute, null)
		{
		}

		public RelayCommand(Action<object> execute, Predicate<object> canExecute)
		{
			if (execute == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("execute");
			}
			_execute = execute;
			_canExecute = canExecute;
		}

		[DebuggerStepThrough]
		public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (_canExecute != null)
			{
				return _canExecute(parameter);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void Execute(object parameter)
		{
			_execute(parameter);
		}
	}

		private Dictionary<string, string> _TypeOverrides = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private bool _UsesUnits;

		private bool _Hidden;

		private string[] _UnitsList;

		private Guid _Guid;

		private bool? _HasOptionList;

		private bool _IsCurrency;

		private bool _unitsDirty = true;

		private bool _valuesDirty = true;

		public string Name
		{
			get

				return _Name;
			}
			set

				_Name = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("Name");

		{
			get

				return _Path;
			}
			set
			{
				_Path = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("Path");
			}
		}

		public string UnderlyingPath

			get
			{
				return _UnderlyingPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_UnderlyingPath = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("UnderlyingPath");

		public Dictionary<string, string> TypeOverrides => _TypeOverrides;

		public ColumnDataType DataType
		{
			get

				return _DataType;
			}
			set

		public bool IsCurrency
		{
			get

				return _IsCurrency;
			}
			set
			{
				_IsCurrency = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("IsCurrency");
			}
		}

		public bool UsesUnits
		{
			get
			{
				if (_DataType != ColumnDataType.Real && _DataType != ColumnDataType.Unspecified)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return _UsesUnits;
			}
			set
			{
				_UsesUnits = value;
			}
		}

		public string Units
		{
			get

				return _Units;
			}
			set
			{
				_Units = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("Units");
			}
		}

		{
			get

				return _UnitsList;
			}
			set
			{
				_UnitsList = value;
			}
		}

		{
			get
			{
				return _IsReadOnly;
			}
			set
			{
				_IsReadOnly = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("IsReadOnly");

			get

				return _Category;

		{
			get

				return _Description;
			}
			set

				_Description = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("Description");
			}
		}

		{
			get

				return _Hidden;
			}
			set
			{
				_Hidden = value;

		}

		public string Attribute
		{
			get
			{
				return _Attribute;
			}
			set
			{
				_Attribute = value;
				RaisePropertyChanged("Attribute");
			}
		}

		public string ResolvedAttribute
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Attribute))
				{
					return _Attribute;
				}
				return "value";
			}
		}

		internal Type PropertyType
		{
			get
			{
				switch (_DataType)
				{
				case ColumnDataType.Boolean:
					return typeof(bool);
				case ColumnDataType.Real:
					return typeof(double);
				case ColumnDataType.Integer:
					return typeof(int);
				case ColumnDataType.Enumeration:
				case ColumnDataType.String:
					return typeof(string);
				case ColumnDataType.Object:
					return typeof(object);
				default:
					return typeof(string);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool UnitsDirty
		{
			get
			{
				return _unitsDirty;
			}
			set
			{
				_unitsDirty = value;
			}
		}

		public bool ValuesDirty
		{
			get
			{
				return _valuesDirty;
			}
			set
			{
				_valuesDirty = value;
			}
		}

		public bool? HasOptionList
		{
			get
			{
				switch (_DataType)
				{
				case ColumnDataType.Boolean:
					return true;
				case ColumnDataType.String:
					return _HasOptionList;
				case ColumnDataType.Enumeration:
					return true;
				case ColumnDataType.Integer:
				case ColumnDataType.Real:
					return false;
				default:
					return _HasOptionList;
				}
			}
			set
			{
				_HasOptionList = value;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public SummaryGridColumn()
		{
			_PathKey = string.Empty;
			_Name = string.Empty;
			_Caption = string.Empty;
			_Category = string.Empty;
			_Description = string.Empty;
			_Path = string.Empty;
			_UnderlyingPath = string.Empty;
			_Units = string.Empty;
			_Attribute = "value";
			_UsesUnits = true;
			_Guid = Guid.NewGuid();
		}

		internal int CompareKeyTo(SummaryGridColumn other)
		{
			return _Guid.CompareTo(other._Guid);
		}

		internal int GetKeyHashCode()
		{
			return _Guid.GetHashCode();
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeCaption()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Caption);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializePathKey()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_PathKey);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializePath()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Path);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeUnderlyingPath()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_UnderlyingPath);
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}
	}
