	{
	}
	public class SummaryGridRow
	{
		private string _eeStatus = string.Empty;

		private string _Name = string.Empty;

		private string _Path = string.Empty;

		private string _TypeKey = string.Empty;

		private Dictionary<string, string> _SecondaryPaths = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private Dictionary<string, string> _SecondaryTypeKeys = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private Dictionary<string, string> _ColumnPathOverrides = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private Guid _Guid;

		private string _detailTab;

		private string _eeStatusPath = string.Empty;

		private List<string> _statusReportList = new List<string>();

		public string Name
		{
			get
			{
				return _Name;
			}
			set
			{
				_Name = value;
			}
		}

		public string DetailTab
		{
			get
			{
				return _detailTab;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != null)
				{
					string text = value.ToString();
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
					{
						_detailTab = text;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public string EEStatusPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _eeStatusPath;
			}
			set
			{
				_eeStatusPath = value;
			}
		}

		public string EEStatus
		{
			get
			{
				return _eeStatus;
			}
			set
			{
				_eeStatus = value;
			}
		}

		public string[] StatusReportPaths => _statusReportList.ToArray();

		public List<string> StatusReportPathList
		{
			get
			{
				return _statusReportList;
			}
			set
			{
				_statusReportList = value;
			}
		}

		public string Group { get; set; }

		public string UnderlyingPath { get; set; }

		public string Path
		{
			get
			{
				return _Path;
			}
			set
			{
				_Path = value;
			}
		}

		public string TypeKey
		{
			get
			{
				return _TypeKey;
			}
			set
			{
				_TypeKey = value;
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Dictionary<string, string> SecondaryPaths => _SecondaryPaths;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Dictionary<string, string> SecondaryTypeKeys => _SecondaryTypeKeys;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public Dictionary<string, string> ColumnOverrides => _ColumnPathOverrides;

		public SummaryGridRow()
		{
			_Guid = Guid.NewGuid();
		}

		public static bool IsRealPath(string path)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				return path[0] != '#';
			}
			return false;
		}

		public string CalculateColumnPath(SummaryGridColumn column)
		{
			string value = string.Empty;
			if (_ColumnPathOverrides.TryGetValue(column.Name, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			string value2 = string.Empty;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.PathKey) || !_SecondaryPaths.TryGetValue(column.PathKey, out value2))
			{
				value2 = _Path;
			}
			string value3 = string.Empty;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.PathKey) || !_SecondaryTypeKeys.TryGetValue(column.PathKey, out value3))
			{
				value3 = _TypeKey;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value3) || column.TypeOverrides.TryGetValue(value3, out var value4))
			{
				value4 = column.Path;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2) && value2[0] == '#')
			{
				return value2;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value4) && value4[0] == '#')
			{
				return value4;
			}
			return CxsPath.CombinePaths(value2, value4);
		}

		public string CalculateColumnUnderlyingPath(SummaryGridColumn column)
		{
			string value = string.Empty;
			if (_ColumnPathOverrides.TryGetValue(column.Name, out value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			string value2 = string.Empty;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.PathKey) || !_SecondaryPaths.TryGetValue(column.PathKey, out value2))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(UnderlyingPath))
				{
					return CalculateColumnPath(column);
				}
				value2 = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(UnderlyingPath) ? _Path : UnderlyingPath);
			}
			string value3 = string.Empty;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.PathKey) || !_SecondaryTypeKeys.TryGetValue(column.PathKey, out value3))
			{
				value3 = _TypeKey;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value3) || column.TypeOverrides.TryGetValue(value3, out var value4))
			{
				value4 = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.UnderlyingPath) ? column.Path : column.UnderlyingPath);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value2) && value2[0] == '#')
			{
				return value2;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value4) && value4[0] == '#')
			{
				return value4;
			}
			return CxsPath.CombinePaths(value2, value4);
		}

		internal int CompareKeyTo(SummaryGridRow other)
		{
			return _Guid.CompareTo(other._Guid);
		}

		internal int GetKeyHashCode()
		{
			return _Guid.GetHashCode();
		}
	}
	public class SummaryGridRowCollection : BindingList<SummaryGridRow>
	{
	}
	public class SummaryGridTemplateCollection
	{
		private SummaryGridTemplate _sgDefaultTemplate;

		private ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTemplate> _lstSgSavedTemplates = new ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTemplate>();

		private readonly string _sumGridNewTplBaseName = Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplateCollection_SummaryGridTemplate");

		private readonly string _sumGridNewTplCopyString = Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplateCollection_Copy");

		public SummaryGridTemplate DefaultSumGridTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return _sgDefaultTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				_sgDefaultTemplate = value;
			}
		}

		public ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTemplate> LstSavedSumGridTemplates
		{
			get
			{
				return _lstSgSavedTemplates;
			}
			set
			{
				_lstSgSavedTemplates = value;
			}
		}

		public string TemplateSetAsCurrentInXml { get; set; }

		public SummaryGridTemplateCollection(SummaryGridTemplate defaultTemplate, string savedTemplateXml)
		{
			_sgDefaultTemplate = defaultTemplate;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(savedTemplateXml))
			{
				CreateTemplateColFromXml(savedTemplateXml);
			}
		}

		public bool UpdateSgTemplate(SummaryGridTemplate tplUpdated)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count; i++)
			{
				if (LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] != null && LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i].Id == tplUpdated.Id)
				{
					LstSavedSumGridTemplates[i] = tplUpdated;
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool DeleteTemplate(Guid idToDelete)
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = null;
			if (LstSavedSumGridTemplates == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
			{
				if (lstSavedSumGridTemplate != null && lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id == idToDelete)
				{
					summaryGridTemplate = lstSavedSumGridTemplate;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (summaryGridTemplate != null)
			{
				LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Remove(summaryGridTemplate);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public SummaryGridTemplate ImportSummaryGridTemplate()
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = new SummaryGridTemplate();
			if (_sgDefaultTemplate != null && _sgDefaultTemplate.Case != null)
			{
				summaryGridTemplate.Case = _sgDefaultTemplate.Case;
			}
			if (summaryGridTemplate.ConfigureFromXmlFile())
			{
				LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Add(summaryGridTemplate);
				return summaryGridTemplate;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public string GetNewTemplateName(string baseName = "")
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			baseName = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(baseName)) ? (baseName + "_" + _sumGridNewTplCopyString) : _sumGridNewTplBaseName);
			stringBuilder.Append(baseName);
			HashSet<string> hashSet = new HashSet<string>();
			if (LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null)
			{
				foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
				{
					if (!hashSet.Contains(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Name))
					{
						hashSet.Add(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Name);
					}
				}
			}
			int num = 1;
			string text = baseName + "_" + num.ToString().Trim();
			while (hashSet.Contains(text))
			{
				text = baseName + "_" + num.ToString().Trim();
				num++;
			}
			return text;
		}

		public string GetXml(Dictionary<Guid, SummaryGridTemplate> dicOrigTemplates = null, string currentTemplateName = "")
		{
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = new UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: false);
			xmlWriterSettings.ConformanceLevel = ConformanceLevel.Document;
			xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
			XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(memoryStream, xmlWriterSettings);
			xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("TabbedSummaryGridTemplateCol");
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentTemplateName))
			{
				xmlWriter.WriteAttributeString("TabbedSummaryGridTemplateCurName", currentTemplateName);
			}
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("TabbedSummaryGridTemplates");
			if (LstSavedSumGridTemplates != null && LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (SummaryGridTemplate lstSavedSumGridTemplate in LstSavedSumGridTemplates)
				{
					if (lstSavedSumGridTemplate == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (dicOrigTemplates != null && lstSavedSumGridTemplate.IsDirty)
					{
						if (dicOrigTemplates.ContainsKey(lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id) && dicOrigTemplates[lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id] != null)
						{
							dicOrigTemplates[lstSavedSumGridTemplate.Id].AppendToXmlDoc(xmlWriter);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						lstSavedSumGridTemplate.AppendToXmlDoc(xmlWriter);
					}
				}
			}
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlWriter.Flush();
			xmlWriter.Close();
			return Encoding.UTF8.GetString(memoryStream.ToArray());
		}

		private bool CreateTemplateColFromXml(string templateColXml)
		{
			try
			{
				using MemoryStream input = new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(templateColXml));
				XmlReader reader = XmlReader.Create(input);
				ReadTemplateConfigCol(reader);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ShowError(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplateCollection_ErrorCreating") + "  " + ex.Message);
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void ReadTemplateConfigCol(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "TabbedSummaryGridTemplateCol")
				{
					if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (reader.HasAttributes)
					{
						string text = string.Empty;
						try
						{
							if (reader.HasAttributes)
							{
								text = reader.GetAttribute("TabbedSummaryGridTemplateCurName");
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							TemplateSetAsCurrentInXml = text;
						}
					}
					ReadTemplateConfigurations(reader);
					continue;
				}
				throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplateCollection_UnexptectedElement") + " " + reader.Name);
			}
		}

		private void ReadTemplateConfigurations(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read() && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !(reader.Name == name)))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					if (!(reader.Name == "TabbedSummaryGridTemplates"))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplateCollection_UnexptectedElement") + " " + reader.Name);
					}
					if (!reader.IsEmptyElement)
					{
						ReadTemplateConfiguration(reader);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReadTemplateConfiguration(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.Name == "TabbedSummaryGridTemplate")
				{
					SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = new SummaryGridTemplate();
					if (reader.HasAttributes)
					{
						string text = string.Empty;
						try
						{
							if (reader.HasAttributes)
							{
								text = reader.GetAttribute("SummaryGridTemplateName");
							}
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							summaryGridTemplate.Name = text;
						}
					}
					summaryGridTemplate.ReadTemplateConfiguration(reader);
					LstSavedSumGridTemplates.Add(summaryGridTemplate);
					summaryGridTemplate.IsDirty = false;
					continue;
				}
				throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplateCollection_UnexptectedElement") + " " + reader.Name);
			}
		}

		private static void ShowError(string msg)
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(msg, SummaryGridTemplate.MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
		}
	}
	internal class DisplayNamePropertyDescriptor : PropertyDescriptor
	{
		private readonly string displayName;

		private readonly PropertyDescriptor parent;

		public override string DisplayName => displayName;

		public override bool IsReadOnly => parent.IsReadOnly;

		public override bool SupportsChangeEvents => parent.SupportsChangeEvents;

		public override Type PropertyType => parent.PropertyType;

		public override TypeConverter Converter => parent.Converter;

		public override Type ComponentType => parent.ComponentType;

		public override string Description => parent.Description;

		public override string Name => parent.Name;

		public DisplayNamePropertyDescriptor(PropertyDescriptor parent, string displayName)
			: base(parent)
		{
			this.displayName = displayName;
			this.parent = parent;
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeValue(object component)
		{
			return parent.ShouldSerializeValue(component);
		}

		public override void SetValue(object component, object value)
		{
			parent.SetValue(component, value);
		}

		public override object GetValue(object component)
		{
			return parent.GetValue(component);
		}

		public override void ResetValue(object component)
		{
			parent.ResetValue(component);
		}

		public override bool CanResetValue(object component)
		{
			return parent.CanResetValue(component);
		}

		public override void AddValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			parent.AddValueChanged(component, handler);
		}

		public override void RemoveValueChanged(object component, EventHandler handler)
		{
			parent.RemoveValueChanged(component, handler);
		}

		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetChildProperties(object instance, Attribute[] filter)
		{
			return parent.GetChildProperties(instance, filter);
		}
	}
	public class TemplateSchema
	{
		public const string XElementTabbedSumGridTplCol = "TabbedSummaryGridTemplateCol";

		public const string XAttributeTabbedSumGridTplCurName = "TabbedSummaryGridTemplateCurName";

		public const string XElementTabbedSumGridTpls = "TabbedSummaryGridTemplates";

		public const string XElementTabbedSumGridTpl = "TabbedSummaryGridTemplate";

		public const string XAttributeTabbedSumGridName = "SummaryGridTemplateName";

		public const string XElementTabs = "Tabs";

		public const string XElementTab = "Tab";

		public const string XElementName = "Name";

		public const string XElementCaption = "Caption";

		public const string XElementFlowSheetName = "FlowSheetName";

		public const string XElementCaptionOrigXml = "CaptionFromOrigXml";

		public const string XElementProperties = "Properties";

		public const string XElementProperty = "Property";

		public const string XElementCxsPath = "DefaultPath";

		public const string XElementDescription = "Description";

		public const string XElementDataType = "DataType";

		public const string XElementReadOnly = "ReadOnly";

		public const string XElementIsCurrency = "IsCurrency";

		public const string XElementDisplay = "Display";

		public const string XElementCustomName = "Name";

		public const string XElementCustomCaption = "Caption";

		public const string XElementIsHorizontal = "IsHorizontal";

		public const string XElementIsResultsFilterOn = "IsResultsFilterOn";

		public const string XElementResultsFilter = "ResultsFilter";

		public const string XElementUseProvidedRowInfo = "UseProvidedRowInfo";

		private TemplateSchema()
		{
		}

		public static string ReadStringElement(XmlReader reader)
		{
			if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			reader.Read();
			while (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
			{
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Text)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("TemplateSchema_ElementIsNotSimpleElement"));
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(reader.Value);
				reader.Read();
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static bool ReadBooleanElement(XmlReader reader)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (bool.TryParse(ReadStringElement(reader), out var result))
			{
				return result;
			}
			throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("TemplateSchema_BoolExpected"));
		}
	}
	public class SummaryGridTemplate : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private string _name = string.Empty;

		private string _path = string.Empty;

		private bool _isDefault;

		private CxsCase _cxsCase;

		private ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTab> _tabs = new ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTab>();

		private bool _localChangeMadeSinceSave;

		private Guid _id = Guid.Empty;

		public string Name
		{
			get
			{
				return _name;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _name)
				{
					IsDirty = true;
					_name = value;
					PropChange("Name");
					PropChange("NameWithDirtyMarker");
				}
			}
		}

		public string NameWithDirtyMarker
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsDirty)
				{
					return _name;
				}
				return _name + "*";
			}
		}

		public string Path
		{
			get
			{
				return _path;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_path != value)
				{
					_path = value;
					PropChange("Path");
				}
			}
		}

		public string Filename
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Path))
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				try
				{
					return System.IO.Path.GetFileName(Path);
				}
				catch
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsDefault => _isDefault;

		public ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTab> SGTabs
		{
			get
			{
				return _tabs;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_tabs == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_tabs != null)
				{
					try
					{
						_tabs.CollectionChanged -= _tabs_CollectionChanged;
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
				_tabs = value;
				PropChange("SGTabs");
				_tabs.CollectionChanged += _tabs_CollectionChanged;
			}
		}

		public CxsCase Case
		{
			get
			{
				return _cxsCase;
			}
			set
			{
				_cxsCase = value;
				PropChange("Case");
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public static string MessageBoxTitle => Global.GetString("Global_SumGridTitle");

		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool IsDirty
		{
			get
			{
				if (_localChangeMadeSinceSave)
				{
					return true;
				}
				foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in SGTabs)
				{
					if (sGTab.IsDirty)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
				if (IsDirty == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value)
				{
					_localChangeMadeSinceSave = true;
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in SGTabs)
					{
						sGTab.IsDirty = false;
					}
					_localChangeMadeSinceSave = false;
				}
				PropChange("IsDirty");
				PropChange("NameWithDirtyMarker");
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public Guid Id
		{
			get
			{
				return _id;
			}
			set
			{
				_id = value;
				PropChange("Id");
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public bool ConfigureFromXmlFile(string filename, bool isDefaultTemplate = false)
		{
			if (!File.Exists(filename))
			{
				ShowError(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_SpecifiedFileNotFound"));
				return false;
			}
			string xml = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				using StreamReader streamReader = File.OpenText(filename);
				xml = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ShowError(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_ErrorReadingConfigFile") + "  " + ex.Message);
				return false;
			}
			bool flag = ConfigureFromXmlString(xml);
			if (flag)
			{
				Path = filename;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Name))
				{
					string name = Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_SummaryGridTemplate");
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(filename))
					{
						name = System.IO.Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(filename);
					}
					Name = name;
				}
				_isDefault = isDefaultTemplate;
				foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in SGTabs)
				{
					sGTab.IsDirty = false;
				}
				_localChangeMadeSinceSave = false;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		public bool ConfigureFromXmlFile()
		{
			string filename = string.Empty;
			using (OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new OpenFileDialog())
			{
				openFileDialog.CheckFileExists = true;
				openFileDialog.Multiselect = false;
				openFileDialog.Title = Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_ImportTemplate");
				openFileDialog.Filter = Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_SummaryGridTemplateFiles") + " (*.sgtxml)|*.sgtxml|All files (*.*)|*.*";
				if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
				{
					return false;
				}
				filename = openFileDialog.FileName;
			}
			return ConfigureFromXmlFile(filename);
		}

		internal void ChildPropertyChanged(object propOwner, string propName)
		{
			_localChangeMadeSinceSave = true;
			PropChange("SGTabs");
		}

		public bool Save()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Path))
			{
				return SaveAs();
			}
			return SaveToFile(Path);
		}

		public bool SaveAs()
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			using (SaveFileDialog saveFileDialog = new SaveFileDialog())
			{
				saveFileDialog.Title = Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_ExportTemplate");
				saveFileDialog.CheckFileExists = false;
				saveFileDialog.CheckPathExists = true;
				saveFileDialog.AddExtension = true;
				saveFileDialog.OverwritePrompt = true;
				saveFileDialog.Filter = Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_SummaryGridTemplateFiles") + " (*.sgtxml)|*.sgtxml|All files (*.*)|*.*";
				if (saveFileDialog.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
				{
					return false;
				}
				text = saveFileDialog.FileName;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return SaveToFile(text);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public SummaryGridTemplate GetCopy(bool copyIdAsWell = false)
		{
			SummaryGridTemplate summaryGridTemplate = new SummaryGridTemplate();
			summaryGridTemplate.ConfigureFromXmlString(GetXml());
			summaryGridTemplate.Case = _cxsCase;
			summaryGridTemplate.Name = _name;
			summaryGridTemplate.Path = _path;
			summaryGridTemplate.IsDirty = IsDirty;
			if (copyIdAsWell)
			{
				summaryGridTemplate.Id = Id;
			}
			return summaryGridTemplate;
		}

		public bool IsBasicMatchTo(SummaryGridTemplate tmpToCheck)
		{
			if (Case != tmpToCheck.Case)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Name != tmpToCheck.Name)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Filename != tmpToCheck.Filename)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (SGTabs == null && tmpToCheck.SGTabs != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (SGTabs != null && tmpToCheck.SGTabs == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (SGTabs == null && tmpToCheck.SGTabs == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (SGTabs.Count != tmpToCheck.SGTabs.Count)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < SGTabs.Count; i++)
			{
				if (!SGTabs[i].IsBasicMatchTo(tmpToCheck.SGTabs[i]))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private Dictionary<string, PastedSumGridInfo> GetDragDropVars(DropVarsInfo DropInfo, bool suppressDialogs = false, string selectedTab = "")
		{
			if (DropInfo == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(DropInfo.FileMoniker) || DropInfo.Paths == null || DropInfo.Paths.Length == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (Case == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (!IsMatchToCurSimPath(DropInfo.FileMoniker))
			{
				if (!suppressDialogs)
				{
					System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_DroppedVarsAreFromDifSimulation"), MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
				}
				return null;
			}
			string[] paths = DropInfo.Paths;
			GetPathsFromMonikers(DropInfo.Paths, ref paths);
			List<VariableInfo> list = new List<VariableInfo>();
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			if (paths != null && paths.Length != 0)
			{
				string[] array = paths;
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					VariableInfo variableInfo = new VariableInfo(Case, text, selectedTab);
					if (!variableInfo.Error)
					{
						list.Add(variableInfo);
					}
					else
					{
						list2.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
			Dictionary<string, PastedSumGridInfo> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, PastedSumGridInfo>();
			foreach (VariableInfo item in list)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.ContainerDisplayName))
				{
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(item.ContainerUniqueName))
					{
						dictionary.Add(item.ContainerUniqueName, new PastedSumGridInfo(item.ContainerType, item.FlowSheetName, item.ContainerDisplayName));
					}
					dictionary[item.ContainerUniqueName].lstProps.Add(new SummaryGridProperty(null, item.PropertyPath, item.PropertyDisplayName, typeof(string)));
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		public Dictionary<string, PastedSumGridInfo> GetCxsClipboardVars(bool suppressDialogs = false, string selectedTab = "")
		{
			CxsCaseInfo caseInfoFromClipboard = CxsServices.GetCaseInfoFromClipboard();
			if (caseInfoFromClipboard == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(caseInfoFromClipboard.FileMoniker) || caseInfoFromClipboard.ItemMonikers == null || caseInfoFromClipboard.ItemMonikers.Length == 0)
			{
				if (!suppressDialogs)
				{
					System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_NoValsFoundInClipboard"), MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
				}
				return null;
			}
			if (Case == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (!IsMatchToCurSimPath(caseInfoFromClipboard.FileMoniker))
			{
				if (!suppressDialogs)
				{
					System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_PastedVarsNotFromCurSimCase"), MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
				}
				return null;
			}
			string[] paths = null;
			GetPathsFromMonikers(caseInfoFromClipboard.ItemMonikers, ref paths, suppressDialogs);
			List<VariableInfo> list = new List<VariableInfo>();
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			if (paths != null && paths.Length != 0)
			{
				string[] array = paths;
				foreach (string text in array)
				{
					VariableInfo variableInfo = new VariableInfo(Case, text, selectedTab);
					if (!variableInfo.Error)
					{
						list.Add(variableInfo);
					}
					else
					{
						list2.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
			Dictionary<string, PastedSumGridInfo> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, PastedSumGridInfo>();
			foreach (VariableInfo item in list)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.ContainerDisplayName))
				{
					if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(item.ContainerUniqueName))
					{
						dictionary.Add(item.ContainerUniqueName, new PastedSumGridInfo(item.ContainerType, item.FlowSheetName, item.ContainerDisplayName));
					}
					dictionary[item.ContainerUniqueName].lstProps.Add(new SummaryGridProperty(null, item.PropertyPath, item.PropertyDisplayName, typeof(string)));
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		internal List<string> Paste(bool suppressDialogs, string selectedTabName = "", bool IsFromClipboard = true, DropVarsInfo DropInfo = null)
		{
			int num = 0;
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			Dictionary<string, PastedSumGridInfo> dictionary = null;
			dictionary = ((!IsFromClipboard) ? GetDragDropVars(DropInfo, suppressDialogs, selectedTabName) : GetCxsClipboardVars(suppressDialogs, selectedTabName));
			if (dictionary == null || dictionary.Keys.Count == 0)
			{
				return list;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, PastedSumGridInfo> item in dictionary)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Key))
				{
					continue;
				}
				SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab = null;
				foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in SGTabs)
				{
					if (string.Compare(sGTab.ID, item.Key, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						summaryGridTab = sGTab;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (summaryGridTab == null)
				{
					summaryGridTab = new SummaryGridTab(this);
					summaryGridTab.Name = item.Value.Name;
					summaryGridTab.Caption = item.Value.DisplayName;
					summaryGridTab.FlowSheetName = item.Value.Flowsheet;
					summaryGridTab.IsDirty = true;
					SGTabs.Add(summaryGridTab);
				}
				list.Add(summaryGridTab.Caption);
				foreach (SummaryGridProperty lstProp in item.Value.lstProps)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (SummaryGridProperty objProp in summaryGridTab.ObjProps)
					{
						if (string.Compare(objProp.CxsPropertyPath, lstProp.CxsPropertyPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						summaryGridTab.ObjProps.Add(lstProp);
						lstProp.Owner = summaryGridTab;
						summaryGridTab.IsDirty = true;
						num++;
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public bool RemoveTab(SummaryGridTab tabToRemove)
		{
			if (tabToRemove == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_tabs.Contains(tabToRemove))
			{
				_tabs.Remove(tabToRemove);
				_localChangeMadeSinceSave = true;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public SummaryGridTemplate(bool isDefaultTemplate = false)
		{
			Id = Guid.NewGuid();
			_isDefault = isDefaultTemplate;
		}

		public SummaryGridTemplate(string xmlFilename)
		{
			ConfigureFromXmlFile(xmlFilename);
			Id = Guid.NewGuid();
		}

		public bool ConfigureFromXmlString(string xml)
		{
			_tabs = new ObservableCollection<SummaryGridTab>();
			try
			{
				using MemoryStream input = new MemoryStream(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(xml));
				XmlReader reader = XmlReader.Create(input);
				ReadTemplateConfiguration(reader);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ShowError(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_ConfigurationFailed") + " " + ex.Message);
				return false;
			}
			IsDirty = false;
			return true;
		}

		private bool IsMatchToCurSimPath(string pathToCheck)
		{
			if (Case == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			string text = Case.CaseFullFilePath;
			try
			{
				if (pathToCheck == null || pathToCheck.Length < 2)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (string.Compare(text, pathToCheck, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (pathToCheck.Substring(0, 2) == "\\\\")
				{
					string mappedPath = GetMappedPath(text);
					if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(mappedPath))
					{
						return result;
					}
					text = mappedPath;
				}
				return string.Compare(text, pathToCheck, ignoreCase: true) == 0;
			}
			catch
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		private static string GetMappedPath(string originalPath)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(originalPath))
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (originalPath.Length > 2)
			{
				empty = originalPath.Substring(0, 2);
				empty2 = originalPath.Substring(2);
				try
				{
					string queryString = "SELECT Providername FROM Win32_LogicalDisk WHERE Name = '" + empty + "'";
					using ManagementObjectSearcher managementObjectSearcher = new ManagementObjectSearcher("root\\CIMV2", queryString);
					using ManagementObjectCollection managementObjectCollection = managementObjectSearcher.Get();
					if (managementObjectCollection == null)
					{
						return result;
					}
					foreach (ManagementObject item in managementObjectCollection)
					{
						result = item.GetPropertyValue("Providername")?.ToString() + empty2;
					}
					return result;
				}
				catch
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool GetPathsFromMonikers(string[] moniker, ref string[] paths, bool suppressDialogs = true)
		{
			if (Case == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			paths = null;
			try
			{
				paths = Case.ConvertMonikersToPaths(moniker);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (!suppressDialogs)
				{
					System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_ErrorConvertingClipDataToPaths") + "\r\n\r\n" + ex.Message, MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool SaveToFile(string filename)
		{
			bool result = false;
			string value = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				value = GetXml();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				ShowError(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_ErrorGeneratingXml") + " " + ex.Message);
				result = false;
			}
			try
			{
				using TextWriter textWriter = File.CreateText(filename);
				textWriter.Write(value);
				result = true;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				ShowError(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_ErrorWritingXmlToFile") + " " + ex2.Message);
				result = false;
			}
			Path = filename;
			foreach (SummaryGridTab sGTab in SGTabs)
			{
				sGTab.IsDirty = false;
			}
			_localChangeMadeSinceSave = false;
			return result;
		}

		private static void ShowError(string msg)
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(msg, MessageBoxTitle, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
		}

		private void _tabs_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_localChangeMadeSinceSave = true;
			PropChange("SGTabs");
		}

		public void ReadTemplateConfiguration(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string name2 = reader.Name;
				if (!(name2 == "TabbedSummaryGridTemplate"))
				{
					if (!(name2 == "Tabs"))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_UnexpectedElementInConfigXml " + reader.Name));
					}
					if (!reader.IsEmptyElement)
					{
						ReadTabConfigurations(reader);
					}
				}
				else if (!reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReadTabConfigurations(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read() && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !(reader.Name == name)))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					if (!(reader.Name == "Tab"))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_UnexpectedElementInConfigXml " + reader.Name));
					}
					if (!reader.IsEmptyElement)
					{
						ReadTabConfiguration(reader);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReadTabConfiguration(XmlReader reader)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			SummaryGridTab summaryGridTab = new SummaryGridTab(this, createNameProp: false, createGroupProp: false);
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				switch (reader.Name)
				{
				case "Caption":
				{
					string caption = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					summaryGridTab.Caption = caption;
					break;
				}
				case "FlowSheetName":
				{
					string flowSheetName = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					summaryGridTab.FlowSheetName = flowSheetName;
					break;
				}
				case "CaptionFromOrigXml":
				{
					string captionFromOrigXml = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					summaryGridTab.CaptionFromOrigXml = captionFromOrigXml;
					break;
				}
				case "UseProvidedRowInfo":
					if (TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader) == "True")
					{
						summaryGridTab.ReadRowInfoFromXml = true;
					}
					break;
				case "Name":
				{
					string name2 = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					summaryGridTab.Name = name2;
					break;
				}
				case "IsHorizontal":
				{
					bool isHorizontal = TemplateSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					summaryGridTab.IsHorizontal = isHorizontal;
					break;
				}
				case "IsResultsFilterOn":
				{
					bool isResultsFilterOn = TemplateSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					summaryGridTab.IsResultsFilterOn = isResultsFilterOn;
					break;
				}
				case "ResultsFilter":
				{
					string resultsFilter = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					summaryGridTab.ResultsFilter = resultsFilter;
					break;
				}
				case "Properties":
					ReadPropertyConfigurations(reader, summaryGridTab);
					break;
				default:
					throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_UnexpectedElementInConfigXml " + reader.Name));
				}
			}
			_tabs.Add(summaryGridTab);
		}

		private void ReadPropertyConfigurations(XmlReader reader, SummaryGridTab curTab)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					if (!(reader.Name == "Property"))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_UnexpectedElementInConfigXml " + reader.Name));
					}
					ReadPropertyConfiguration(reader, curTab);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void ReadPropertyConfiguration(XmlReader reader, SummaryGridTab curTab)
		{
			string name = reader.Name;
			SummaryGridProperty summaryGridProperty = new SummaryGridProperty(curTab);
			while (reader.Read())
			{
				if (reader.IsEmptyElement)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.EndElement && reader.Name == name)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element || reader.Name == "Property")
				{
					continue;
				}
				switch (reader.Name)
				{
				case "DefaultPath":
					summaryGridProperty.SetPath(TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader));
					break;
				case "Display":
					summaryGridProperty.Display = TemplateSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					break;
				case "Description":
					summaryGridProperty.VariableDescription = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					break;
				case "ReadOnly":
					summaryGridProperty.ReadOnly = TemplateSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					break;
				case "Name":
					summaryGridProperty.CustomName = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					break;
				case "IsCurrency":
					summaryGridProperty.IsCurrency = TemplateSchema.ReadBooleanElement(reader);
					break;
				case "Caption":
					summaryGridProperty.CustomCaption = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					break;
				case "DataType":
				{
					string value = TemplateSchema.ReadStringElement(reader);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) && Enum.IsDefined(typeof(ColumnDataType), value))
					{
						summaryGridProperty.DataType = (ColumnDataType)Enum.Parse(typeof(ColumnDataType), value, ignoreCase: true);
					}
					break;
				}
				default:
					throw new ArgumentException(Global.GetString("SummaryGridTemplate_UnexpectedElementInConfigXml " + reader.Name));
				}
			}
			curTab.AddOrReplaceProp(summaryGridProperty);
		}

		public string GetXml()
		{
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = new UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: false);
			xmlWriterSettings.ConformanceLevel = ConformanceLevel.Document;
			xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
			XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(memoryStream, xmlWriterSettings);
			xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
			AppendToXmlDoc(xmlWriter);
			xmlWriter.Flush();
			xmlWriter.Close();
			return Encoding.UTF8.GetString(memoryStream.ToArray());
		}

		public bool AppendToXmlDoc(XmlWriter xml)
		{
			try
			{
				xml.WriteStartElement("TabbedSummaryGridTemplate");
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Name))
				{
					xml.WriteAttributeString("SummaryGridTemplateName", Name);
				}
				if (_tabs != null && _tabs.Count > 0)
				{
					xml.WriteStartElement("Tabs");
					foreach (SummaryGridTab tab in _tabs)
					{
						xml.WriteStartElement("Tab");
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tab.Name))
						{
							xml.WriteElementString("Name", tab.Name);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tab.Caption))
						{
							xml.WriteElementString("Caption", tab.Caption);
						}
						xml.WriteElementString("UseProvidedRowInfo", tab.ReadRowInfoFromXml ? "True" : "False");
						xml.WriteElementString("IsHorizontal", tab.IsHorizontal.ToString());
						if (tab.IsResultsFilterOn)
						{
							xml.WriteElementString("IsResultsFilterOn", tab.IsResultsFilterOn.ToString());
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tab.ResultsFilter))
						{
							xml.WriteElementString("ResultsFilter", tab.ResultsFilter);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tab.FlowSheetName))
						{
							xml.WriteElementString("FlowSheetName", tab.FlowSheetName);
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tab.CaptionFromOrigXml))
						{
							xml.WriteElementString("CaptionFromOrigXml", tab.CaptionFromOrigXml);
						}
						if (tab.ObjProps != null && tab.ObjProps.Count > 0)
						{
							xml.WriteStartElement("Properties");
							foreach (SummaryGridProperty objProp in tab.ObjProps)
							{
								xml.WriteStartElement("Property");
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.CxsPropertyPath))
								{
									xml.WriteElementString("DefaultPath", objProp.CxsPropertyPath);
								}
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.VariableDescription))
								{
									xml.WriteElementString("Description", objProp.VariableDescription);
								}
								if (objProp.IsCurrency)
								{
									xml.WriteElementString("IsCurrency", objProp.IsCurrency.ToString());
								}
								xml.WriteElementString("Display", objProp.Display.ToString());
								xml.WriteElementString("ReadOnly", objProp.ReadOnly.ToString());
								xml.WriteElementString("DataType", Enum.GetName(typeof(ColumnDataType), objProp.DataType));
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.CustomName))
								{
									xml.WriteElementString("Name", objProp.CustomName);
								}
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objProp.CustomCaption))
								{
									xml.WriteElementString("Caption", objProp.CustomCaption);
								}
								xml.WriteEndElement();
							}
							xml.WriteEndElement();
						}
						xml.WriteEndElement();
					}
					xml.WriteEndElement();
				}
				xml.WriteEndElement();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void PropChange(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return Name;
		}
	}
	public class SummaryGridTab : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private readonly string[] cStreamTypes = new string[4] { "Material", "Heat", "Load", "Work" };

		private bool _addNameProp;

		private bool _addGroupProp;

		private bool _readRowInfoFromXml;

		private string _caption = string.Empty;

		private string _name = string.Empty;

		private bool _isDirty;

		private bool _isHorizontal = true;

		private bool _isResultsFilterOn;

		private string _resultsFilter = string.Empty;

		private BindingList<SummaryGridProperty> _objProps = new BindingList<SummaryGridProperty>();

		private string _flowSheetName = string.Empty;

		private string _captionFromOrigXml = string.Empty;

		private SummaryGridTemplate _owner;

		public BindingList<SummaryGridProperty> ObjProps
		{
			get
			{
				return _objProps;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _objProps)
				{
					_objProps = value;
					PropChange("ObjProps");
				}
			}
		}

		public string Name
		{
			get
			{
				return _name;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value != _name))
				{
					return;
				}
				string text = string.Empty;
				string[] array = cStreamTypes;
				foreach (string text2 in array)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) && string.Compare(text2, value, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == 0)
					{
						text = text2;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					Caption = $"Streams ({text})";
				}
				_name = value;
				PropChange("Name");
			}
		}

		public string Caption
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_caption))
				{
					return Name;
				}
				return _caption;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_caption != value)
				{
					_caption = value;
					PropChange("Caption");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsDirty
		{
			get
			{
				return _isDirty;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isDirty != value)
				{
					_isDirty = value;
					PropChange("IsDirty");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsHorizontal
		{
			get
			{
				return _isHorizontal;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isHorizontal != value)
				{
					_isHorizontal = value;
					PropChange("IsHorizontal");
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsResultsFilterOn
		{
			get
			{
				return _isResultsFilterOn;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_isResultsFilterOn != value)
				{
					_isResultsFilterOn = value;
					PropChange("IsResultsFilterOn");
				}
			}
		}

		public string ResultsFilter
		{
			get
			{
				return _resultsFilter;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_resultsFilter != value)
				{
					_resultsFilter = value;
					PropChange("ResultsFilter");
					PropChange("IsResultsFilterOn");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public string FlowSheetName
		{
			get
			{
				return _flowSheetName;
			}
			set
			{
				_flowSheetName = value;
				PropChange("FlowSheetName");
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public string CaptionFromOrigXml
		{
			get
			{
				return _captionFromOrigXml;
			}
			set
			{
				_captionFromOrigXml = value;
				PropChange("CaptionFromOrigXml");
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public string ID
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(FlowSheetName) || FlowSheetName == "*")
				{
					return Name;
				}
				return Name + "@" + FlowSheetName;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool ReadRowInfoFromXml
		{
			get
			{
				return _readRowInfoFromXml;
			}
			set
			{
				_readRowInfoFromXml = value;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public SummaryGridTab(SummaryGridTemplate template, bool createNameProp = true, bool createGroupProp = true)
		{
			_owner = template;
			ObjProps = new BindingList<SummaryGridProperty>();
			if (createNameProp)
			{
				_addNameProp = true;
				SummaryGridProperty item = new SummaryGridProperty(this, "#Name", "Name", typeof(string))
				{
					ReadOnly = true
				};
				ObjProps.Add(item);
			}
			if (createGroupProp)
			{
				_addGroupProp = true;
				SummaryGridProperty item2 = new SummaryGridProperty(this, "#Group", "Group", typeof(string), display: false)
				{
					ReadOnly = true
				};
				ObjProps.Add(item2);
			}
		}

		public bool IsBasicMatchTo(SummaryGridTab tabToCheck)
		{
			if (string.CompareOrdinal(Caption, tabToCheck.Caption) != 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (string.CompareOrdinal(Name, tabToCheck.Name) != 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (ObjProps == null && tabToCheck.ObjProps != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (ObjProps != null && tabToCheck.ObjProps == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (ObjProps == null && tabToCheck == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (ObjProps.Count != tabToCheck.ObjProps.Count)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < ObjProps.Count; i++)
			{
				if (!ObjProps[i].IsBasicMatchTo(tabToCheck.ObjProps[i]))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void AddOrReplaceProp(SummaryGridProperty sgProp)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < ObjProps.Count; i++)
			{
				if (string.CompareOrdinal(ObjProps[i].CxsPropertyPath, sgProp.CxsPropertyPath) == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			ObjProps.Add(sgProp);
		}

		internal void ChildPropertyChanged(object propOwner)
		{
			IsDirty = true;
			PropChange("ObjProps");
		}

		private void PropChange(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
			if (_owner != null)
			{
				_owner.ChildPropertyChanged(this, propName);
			}
		}

		public List<string> GetPathsAddedByDefault()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (_addNameProp)
			{
				list.Add("#Name");
			}
			if (_addGroupProp)
			{
				list.Add("#Group");
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class SummaryGridProperty : INotifyPropertyChanged, ICustomTypeDescriptor
	{
		private bool? _usesUnits;

		private string _attributeToShow = "value";

		private string _cxsPropertyPath = string.Empty;

		private string _variableDescription = string.Empty;

		private bool _display;

		private bool? _readOnly;

		private ColumnDataType _dataType;

		private bool _isCurrency;

		private string _customName = string.Empty;

		private string _customCaption = string.Empty;

		private SummaryGridTab _owner;

		[DisplayName("Display")]
		public bool Display
		{
			get
			{
				return _display;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_display != value)
				{
					_display = value;
					PropChange("Display");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool? UsesUnits
		{
			get
			{
				return _usesUnits;
			}
			set
			{
				_usesUnits = value;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public string AttributeToShow
		{
			get
			{
				return _attributeToShow;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _attributeToShow)
				{
					_attributeToShow = value;
					PropChange("AttributeToShow");
				}
			}
		}

		[DisplayName("Variable Description")]
		public string VariableDescription
		{
			get
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(CxsPropertyPath) && CxsPropertyPath.StartsWith("#"))
				{
					string text = Global.GetString("SummaryGridProperty_CustomProp_" + CxsPropertyPath.Substring(1).Replace(" ", ""));
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
					{
						return text;
					}
				}
				return _variableDescription;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _variableDescription)
				{
					_variableDescription = value;
					PropChange("VariableDescription");
				}
			}
		}

		[DisplayName("Path")]
		public string CxsPropertyPath
		{
			get
			{
				return _cxsPropertyPath;
			}
			private set
			{
				if (value != _cxsPropertyPath)
				{
					_cxsPropertyPath = value;
					PropChange("CxsPropertyPath");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public string CustomName
		{
			get
			{
				return _customName;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _customName)
				{
					_customName = value;
					PropChange("CustomName");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public string CustomCaption
		{
			get
			{
				return _customCaption;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _customCaption)
				{
					_customCaption = value;
					PropChange("CustomCaption");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool? ReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				return _readOnly;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _readOnly)
				{
					_readOnly = value;
					PropChange("ReadOnly");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public ColumnDataType DataType
		{
			get
			{
				return _dataType;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _dataType)
				{
					_dataType = value;
					PropChange("DataType");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public bool IsCurrency
		{
			get
			{
				return _isCurrency;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _isCurrency)
				{
					_isCurrency = value;
					PropChange("IsCurrency");
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public SummaryGridTab Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return _owner;
			}
			set
			{
				_owner = value;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public string UnderlyingPath { get; set; }

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public SummaryGridProperty(SummaryGridTab sgTab, string cxsPath, string variableDescription, Type dataType, bool display = true)
		{
			CxsPropertyPath = cxsPath;
			VariableDescription = variableDescription;
			Display = display;
			switch (dataType.Name)
			{
			case "string":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.String;
				break;
			case "double":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Real;
				break;
			case "int":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Integer;
				break;
			case "enum":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Integer;
				break;
			case "bool":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Boolean;
				break;
			default:
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Unspecified;
				break;
			}
		}

		public SummaryGridProperty(SummaryGridTab sgTab, string cxsPath, string variableDescription, string columnDataType, bool display = true, bool isCurrency = false)
		{
			_owner = sgTab;
			CxsPropertyPath = cxsPath;
			VariableDescription = variableDescription;
			Display = display;
			IsCurrency = isCurrency;
			switch (columnDataType)
			{
			case "String":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.String;
				break;
			case "Real":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Real;
				break;
			case "Integer":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Integer;
				break;
			case "Enum":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Integer;
				break;
			case "Boolean":
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Boolean;
				break;
			default:
				DataType = ColumnDataType.Unspecified;
				break;
			}
		}

		public SummaryGridProperty(SummaryGridTab sgTab)
		{
			_owner = sgTab;
		}

		public bool IsBasicMatchTo(SummaryGridProperty propToCheck)
		{
			if (string.CompareOrdinal(AttributeToShow, propToCheck.AttributeToShow) != 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (string.CompareOrdinal(CxsPropertyPath, propToCheck.CxsPropertyPath) != 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (DataType != propToCheck.DataType)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Display != propToCheck.Display)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (ReadOnly != propToCheck.ReadOnly)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (string.CompareOrdinal(VariableDescription, propToCheck.VariableDescription) != 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void SetPath(string newPath)
		{
			CxsPropertyPath = newPath;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return VariableDescription;
		}

		private void PropChange(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
			if (_owner != null)
			{
				_owner.ChildPropertyChanged(this);
			}
		}

		public TypeConverter GetConverter()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public EventDescriptorCollection GetEvents(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetEvents(this, attributes, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		EventDescriptorCollection ICustomTypeDescriptor.GetEvents()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetEvents(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public string GetComponentName()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetComponentName(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public object GetPropertyOwner(PropertyDescriptor pd)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public AttributeCollection GetAttributes()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetAttributes(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(this, attributes, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
			PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptorCollection = new PropertyDescriptorCollection(new PropertyDescriptor[0]);
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor item in properties)
			{
				string displayName = item.DisplayName;
				string text = Global.GetString("SummaryGridProperty_" + displayName.Replace(" ", ""));
				DisplayNamePropertyDescriptor value = new DisplayNamePropertyDescriptor(item, string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) ? displayName : text);
				propertyDescriptorCollection.Add(value);
			}
			return propertyDescriptorCollection;
		}

		PropertyDescriptorCollection ICustomTypeDescriptor.GetProperties()
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(GetType());
			PropertyDescriptorCollection propertyDescriptorCollection = new PropertyDescriptorCollection(new PropertyDescriptor[0]);
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor item in properties)
			{
				string displayName = item.DisplayName;
				string text = Global.GetString("SummaryGridProperty_" + displayName.Replace(" ", ""));
				DisplayNamePropertyDescriptor value = new DisplayNamePropertyDescriptor(item, string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) ? displayName : text);
				propertyDescriptorCollection.Add(value);
			}
			return propertyDescriptorCollection;
		}

		public object GetEditor(Type editorBaseType)
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetEditor(this, editorBaseType, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public PropertyDescriptor GetDefaultProperty()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetDefaultProperty(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public EventDescriptor GetDefaultEvent()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetDefaultEvent(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public string GetClassName()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetClassName(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}
	}
	public class PastedSumGridInfo
	{
		private string _id = string.Empty;

		public string Name { get; private set; }

		public string Flowsheet { get; private set; }

		public string DisplayName { get; private set; }

		public string ID
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_id))
				{
					string text = Name;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Flowsheet) && Flowsheet != "*")
					{
						text += "@";
						text += Flowsheet;
					}
					return text;
				}
				return _id;
			}
			set
			{
				_id = value;
			}
		}

		public List<SummaryGridProperty> lstProps { get; private set; }

		public PastedSumGridInfo()
		{
			lstProps = new List<SummaryGridProperty>();
		}

		public PastedSumGridInfo(string name, string flowsheet, string displayName)
		{
			Name = name;
			Flowsheet = flowsheet;
			DisplayName = displayName;
			lstProps = new List<SummaryGridProperty>();
		}
	}
	[MarkupExtensionReturnType(typeof(string), typeof(string))]
	public class TranslateExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		private string _key;

		private const string NotFoundError = "# XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX  StringNotFound XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX  #";

		public TranslateExtension(string key)
		{
			_key = key;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_key))
			{
				return _key;
			}
			return Strings.ResourceManager.GetString(_key) ?? _key;
		}
	}

		public string[] SelectedRows
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (DataRecord record in ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records)
				{
					if (record.DataItem is RowProxy rowProxy)
					{
						list.Add(rowProxy.GridRow.Name);
					}
				}
				return list.ToArray();
			}
		}

		public string[] SelectedCells
		{
			get
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				foreach (Cell cell in ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Cells)
				{
					if (cell != null && cell.Record != null && cell.Record.DataItem != null && cell.Field != null && ((FieldItem)cell.Field).Name != null)
					{
						RowProxy row = cell.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
						RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)cell.Field).Name.ToString());
						CellCache cellCache = _GridCache.GetCellCache(row, property);
						if (cellCache != null && cellCache.Path != null)
						{
							list.Add(cellCache.Path.ToString());
						}
					}
				}
				return list.ToArray();
			}
		}

		public bool UseHorizontalOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				if (_summaryGrid.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					_summaryGrid.ViewSettings.Orientation = System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal;
					((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).FieldSettings.LabelTextAlignment = TextAlignment.Left;
				}
				else
				{
					_summaryGrid.ViewSettings.Orientation = System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
					((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).FieldSettings.LabelTextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				}
			}
		}

		{

		public new void EndInit()
		{
			if (_WaitingForEndInit)
			{
				_WaitingForEndInit = false;
				((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).DataSource = null;
				_GridCache.EndInit();
				((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).DataSource = _GridCache;
				base.EndInit();
			}
		}

		public SummaryGridControl()
		{
			_GridCache = new GridCache(this);
			_GridCache.CellDataTypeChanged += delegate(GridCache.CellDataTypeChangedEventArgs args)
			{
				CellNeedsReformatting(args.Cell);
			};
			_GridCache.ColumnsListChanged += _GridCache_ColumnsListChanged;
			_GridCache.RowsListChanged += _GridCache_RowsListChanged;
			_GridCache.DataInputExceptionRaised += _GridCache_DataInputExceptionRaised;
			_GridCache.GetAdjacentColumnsHandler = GetAdjacentColumns;
			_GridCache.GetAdjacentRowsHandler = GetAdjacentRows;
			try
			{
				InitializeComponent();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			RegisterStaticEvents();
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(CopyCommand, ExecutedCopyCommand, CanExecuteCopyCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ClearCommand, ExecutedClearCommand, CanExecuteClearCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(PasteCommand, ExecutedPasteCommand, CanExecutePasteCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(SelectAllCommand, ExecutedSelectAllCommand, CanExecuteSelectAllCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(SelectColumnCommand, ExecutedSelectColumnCommand, CanExecuteSelectColumnCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(SelectRowCommand, ExecutedSelectRowCommand, CanExecuteSelectRowCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(SortAscendingCommand, ExecutedSortAscendingCommand, CanExecuteSortAscendingCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(SortDescendingCommand, ExecutedSortDescendingCommand, CanExecuteSortDescendingCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ClearSortCommand, ExecutedClearSortCommand, CanExcuteClearSortCommand));
			((UIElement)(object)_summaryGrid).CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(UnitsChangeCommand, ExecutedUnitsChangeCommand, CanExecuteUnitsChangeCommand));
			((FrameworkElement)(object)_summaryGrid).Tag = this;
		}

		public bool GetSummaryGridExportData(ref string[] FieldLabels, ref List<Export2DTemplate.Record> Records)
		{
			//IL_003c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0043: Expected O, but got Unknown
			bool result = false;
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			int count = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records.Count;
			foreach (DataRecord item in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records)
			{
				DataRecord val = item;
				List<object> list2 = new List<object>();
				List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
				RowProxy rowProxy = val.DataItem as RowProxy;
				if (!flag)
				{
					FieldLayout fieldLayout = ((Record)val).FieldLayout;
					if (fieldLayout != null)
					{
						num = ((Collection<Field>)(object)fieldLayout.Fields).Count;
						foreach (Field item2 in (Collection<Field>)(object)fieldLayout.Fields)
						{
							list.Add(((FieldItem)item2).Label.ToString());
						}
						if (list.Count > 0)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
				}
				foreach (Cell item3 in (IEnumerable<Cell>)val.Cells)
				{
					Field field = item3.Field;
					if (field == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = rowProxy.GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)field).Name);
					if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					CellCache cellCache = rowProxy.GridCache.GetCellCache(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
					if (cellCache != null && cellCache.Value == null)
					{
						_GridCache.GetValue(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
					}
					if (cellCache != null && cellCache.Value != null)
					{
						if (cellCache.Value is Hyperlink)
						{
							list2.Add(((cellCache.Value as Hyperlink).Tag as HyperLinkInfo).Text);
						}
						else
						{
							list2.Add(cellCache.Value);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						list2.Add(string.Empty);
					}
					string text = "cxs:" + cellCache.Path;
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cellCache.Units))
					{
						text = text + ExportTable.CommentMetaDataDelimiter + cellCache.Units;
					}
					list3.Add(text);
				}
				Export2DTemplate.Record record = new Export2DTemplate.Record();
				record.Values = list2.ToArray();
				record.Name = rowProxy.GridRow.Name;
				record.Paths = list3.ToArray();
				Records.Add(record);
			}
			if (list.Count == num && Records.Count == count)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			FieldLabels = list.ToArray();
			return result;
		}

		public static void SortField(FieldSortDescription Sort, XamDataGrid thisgrid)
		{
			try
			{
				((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).FieldLayouts[0].SortedFields.Clear();
				((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).FieldLayouts[0].SortedFields.Add(Sort);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		public static void ClearSort(XamDataGrid thisgrid)
		{
			((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).FieldLayouts[0].SortedFields.Clear();
		}

		public void RefreshDisplay(bool resetDataCache)
		{
			if (resetDataCache)
			{
				UpdateFields(_HaveGridData);
			}
			_GridCache.RefreshData(resetDataCache);
			((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).DataSource = _GridCache;
			_rowStatusNeedsUpdate = true;
			_hLinksNeedUpdate = HasHyperlinks;
		}

		internal void CellNeedsReformatting(CellCache cellCache)
		{
			RowProxy rowProxy = cellCache.RowProxy;
			RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = cellCache.Property;
			foreach (Record item in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records)
			{
				DataRecord val = (DataRecord)(object)((item is DataRecord) ? item : null);
				if (val == null || val.DataItem != rowProxy)
				{
					continue;
				}
				{
					foreach (Cell item2 in (IEnumerable<Cell>)val.Cells)
					{
						if (item2.Field.GetPropertyDescriptor(val) == property)
						{
							switch (property.GridColumn.DataType)
							{
							case ColumnDataType.Integer:
								item2.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Int"];
								break;
							case ColumnDataType.Real:
								item2.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Real"];
								break;
							case ColumnDataType.String:
								item2.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["String"];
								break;
							case ColumnDataType.Boolean:
								item2.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Check"];
								break;
							case ColumnDataType.Enumeration:
								break;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ExecutedPasteCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			DoPaste();
		}

		private void CanExecutePasteCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = CanDoPaste(this);
		}

		private static bool CanDoPaste(SummaryGridControl gridControl)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (gridControl == null || gridControl._summaryGrid == null)
			{
				return flag;
			}
			if (!System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.StringFormat) && !System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.CommaSeparatedValue) && !System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.Text))
			{
				return flag;
			}
			List<List<Cell>> selectedCells = GetSelectedCells(gridControl._summaryGrid);
			if (selectedCells != null)
			{
				foreach (List<Cell> item in selectedCells)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						break;
					}
					foreach (Cell item2 in item)
					{
						if (item2 != null && item2.Record != null && item2.Field != null)
						{
							RowProxy rowProxy = item2.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
							RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = gridControl._GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item2.Field).Name);
							bool? flag2 = gridControl._GridCache.IsReadOnly(rowProxy, property);
							if (flag2.HasValue && !flag2.Value)
							{
								flag = true;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private void ExecutedCopyCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CopyToClipboard();
		}

		private void CanExecuteCopyCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

		private void ExecutedClearCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			List<List<Cell>> selectedCells = GetSelectedCells(_summaryGrid);
			if (selectedCells == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (List<Cell> item in selectedCells)
			{
				foreach (Cell item2 in item)
				{
					if (item2 != null && item2.Record != null && item2.Field != null)
					{
						RowProxy rowProxy = item2.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
						RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item2.Field).Name);
						bool? flag = _GridCache.IsReadOnly(rowProxy, property);
						if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
						{
							item2.Value = null;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void CanExecuteClearCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = false;
			if (_summaryGrid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<List<Cell>> selectedCells = GetSelectedCells(_summaryGrid);
			if (selectedCells == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (List<Cell> item in selectedCells)
			{
				foreach (Cell item2 in item)
				{
					if (item2 != null && item2.Record != null && item2.Field != null)
					{
						RowProxy rowProxy = item2.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
						RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item2.Field).Name);
						bool? flag = _GridCache.IsReadOnly(rowProxy, property);
						if (flag.HasValue && !flag.Value)
						{
							e.CanExecute = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void ExecutedSelectColumnCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Parameter != null)
			{
				SelectColumnorRow(e.Parameter, SelectColumn: true);
				return;
			}
			Cell activeCell = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).ActiveCell;
			if (activeCell != null)
			{
				SelectColumnorRow(CellValuePresenter.FromCell(activeCell), SelectColumn: true);
			}
		}

		}

		private void ExecutedSortAscendingCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0030: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			object parameter = e.Parameter;
			LabelPresenter val = (LabelPresenter)((parameter is LabelPresenter) ? parameter : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				DataPresenterBase dataPresenter = ((DataItemPresenter)val).DataPresenter;
				XamDataGrid val2 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter : null);
				if (val != null && val2 != null && val2 == _summaryGrid)
				{
					SortField(new FieldSortDescription
					{
						Direction = ListSortDirection.Ascending,
						FieldName = ((FieldItem)((DataItemPresenter)val).Field).Name,
						Field = ((DataItemPresenter)val).Field
					}, val2);
					_hLinksNeedUpdate = HasHyperlinks;
				}
			}
		}

		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0045: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
