		private void ExecutedUnitsChangeCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(e.Parameter is object[] array))
			{
				return;
			}
			object obj = array[0];
			Field val = (Field)((obj is Field) ? obj : null);
			if (val != null && array[1] is string units)
			{
				RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)val).Name);
				if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor != null)
				{
					rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn.Units = units;
					_GridCache.OnColumnUnitsChanged(rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn);
					((FieldItem)val).Label = _GridCache.FormatColumnCaption(rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn);
					RefreshDisplay(resetDataCache: false);
				}
			}
		}

		private void CanExecuteUnitsChangeCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

		public void CopyToClipboard()
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.Clipboard.Clear();
			List<List<Cell>> selectedCells = GetSelectedCells(_summaryGrid);
			if (selectedCells == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SGClipboardMetaData sGClipboardMetaData = new SGClipboardMetaData();
			sGClipboardMetaData.ContainsColumnHeaders = ((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Fields).Count > 0 || ((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records).Count > 0 || _IsAllSelected;
			System.Windows.DataObject dataObject = new System.Windows.DataObject();
			CreateTextClipboard(ref dataObject, selectedCells);
			CreateCsvClipboard(ref dataObject, selectedCells);
			CreateCxsClipboard(ref dataObject, selectedCells);
			dataObject.SetData(System.Windows.DataFormats.Serializable, sGClipboardMetaData, autoConvert: true);
			try
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.Clipboard.SetDataObject(dataObject, copy: true, 20, 50);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		public bool PasteFromClipboard()
		{
			//IL_02b3: Expected O, but got Unknown
			SGClipboardMetaData sGClipboardMetaData = null;
			if (System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.Serializable))
			{
				sGClipboardMetaData = System.Windows.Clipboard.GetData(System.Windows.DataFormats.Serializable) as SGClipboardMetaData;
			}
			List<List<string>> list = null;
			if (System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.Text) || System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.UnicodeText))
			{
				string text = null;
				if (System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.UnicodeText))
				{
					text = System.Windows.Clipboard.GetText(System.Windows.TextDataFormat.UnicodeText);
				}
				else if (System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.Text))
				{
					text = System.Windows.Clipboard.GetText(System.Windows.TextDataFormat.Text);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && (text.IndexOf('\t') >= 0 || text.IndexOf('\r') >= 0))
				{
					list = DecodeClipboard(text, '\t');
				}
				else
				{
					list = new List<List<string>>();
					List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
					list2.Add(text);
					list.Add(list2);
				}
			}
			else if (System.Windows.Clipboard.ContainsData(System.Windows.DataFormats.CommaSeparatedValue))
			{
				string text2 = System.Windows.Clipboard.GetText(System.Windows.TextDataFormat.CommaSeparatedValue);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && System.Windows.Clipboard.GetData(System.Windows.DataFormats.CommaSeparatedValue) is Stream stream)
				{
					text2 = new StreamReader(stream, new UTF8Encoding()).ReadToEnd();
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					list = DecodeClipboard(text2, ',');
				}
			}
			if (list == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Cell topLeftSelectedCell = GetTopLeftSelectedCell();
			if (topLeftSelectedCell == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			try
			{
				_DoingPaste = true;
				bool flag = sGClipboardMetaData?.ContainsColumnHeaders ?? false;
				if (flag && !UseHorizontalOrientation && list.Count > 0)
				{
					list.RemoveAt(0);
					flag = false;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
				{
					if (flag && list[i].Count > 0)
					{
						list[i].RemoveAt(0);
					}
					List<string> list3 = list[i];
					for (int j = 0; j < list3.Count; j++)
					{
						string text3 = list3[j];
						Cell cellRelativeTo = GetCellRelativeTo(topLeftSelectedCell, i, j);
						if (cellRelativeTo == null)
						{
							break;
						}
						RowProxy rowProxy = cellRelativeTo.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
						RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)cellRelativeTo.Field).Name);
						bool? flag2 = _GridCache.IsReadOnly(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
						if (flag2.HasValue && flag2.Value)
						{
							continue;
						}
						try
						{
							switch (Type.GetTypeCode(rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.PropertyType))
							{
							case TypeCode.Boolean:
							{
								bool result2 = true;
								if (bool.TryParse(text3, out result2))
								{
									_GridCache.SetValue(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor, result2);
								}
								break;
							}
							case TypeCode.Double:
							{
								if (double.TryParse(text3, out var result3))
								{
									_GridCache.SetValue(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor, result3);
								}
								break;
							}
							case TypeCode.Int32:
							{
								if (int.TryParse(text3, out var result))
								{
									_GridCache.SetValue(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor, result);
								}
								break;
							}
							default:
								_GridCache.SetValue(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor, text3);
								break;
							}
						}
						catch (CxsException ex)
						{
							CxsException ex2 = ex;
							string text4 = string.Empty;
							if (((Exception)(object)ex2).InnerException != null)
							{
								text4 = ((Exception)(object)ex2).InnerException.Message;
							}
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text4))
							{
								System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(text4, Global.GetString("SummaryGridControl_InputError"), MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
							}
							else if (((Exception)(object)ex2).Message == "BatchSet failed")
							{
								System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(Global.GetString("SummaryGridControl_BatchSetFailed"), Global.GetString("SummaryGridControl_InputError"), MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
							}
							else
							{
								System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(((Exception)(object)ex2).Message, Global.GetString("SummaryGridControl_InputError"), MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex3)
			{
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show("Error occurred during paste. " + Environment.NewLine + ex3.Message + Environment.NewLine + ex3.StackTrace, "Paste Error", MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Hand);
			}
			finally
			{
				_DoingPaste = false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void AddStandardColumns()
		{
			_GridCache.AddStandardColumns(ExcludeGroupProperty, ExcludeNameProperty);
		}

		private void PopUpSubscribeToEvent(bool IsEnabled)
		{
			if (_popUp == null)
			{
				_popUpSelectionChangeSubscribed = false;
				return;
			}
			if (!IsEnabled)
			{
				_popUp.SelectionChanged -= PopUp_SelectionChanged;
			}
			else if (!_popUpSelectionChangeSubscribed)
			{
				_popUp.SelectionChanged += PopUp_SelectionChanged;
			}
			_popUpSelectionChangeSubscribed = IsEnabled;
		}

		private void PopUp_SelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				_editModeEndedTriggeredByPopUp = true;
				((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).ActiveCell.EndEditMode();
			}
			finally
			{
				_editModeEndedTriggeredByPopUp = false;
			}
		}

		internal static SummaryGridControl GetSummaryGridControl(XamDataGrid grid)
		{
			SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = null;
			if (grid != null)
			{
				summaryGridControl = ((FrameworkElement)(object)grid).Tag as SummaryGridControl;
				if (summaryGridControl == null)
				{
					summaryGridControl = GetFEParent<SummaryGridControl>((FrameworkElement)(object)grid);
				}
			}
			return summaryGridControl;
		}

		private static T GetFEParent<T>(FrameworkElement control) where T : Visual
		{
			T val = null;
			if (!(control.Parent is FrameworkElement frameworkElement))
			{
				return null;
			}
			val = frameworkElement as T;
			if (val == null)
			{
				val = GetFEParent<T>(frameworkElement);
			}
			return val;
		}

		private static void RegisterStaticEvents()
		{
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(DataRecordPresenter), FrameworkElement.ContextMenuOpeningEvent, new ContextMenuEventHandler(RecordContextMenuOpen));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(LabelPresenter), FrameworkElement.ContextMenuOpeningEvent, new ContextMenuEventHandler(FieldContextMenuOpen));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(CellValuePresenter), FrameworkElement.ContextMenuOpeningEvent, new ContextMenuEventHandler(CellContextMenuOpen));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(CellValuePresenter), FrameworkElement.ToolTipOpeningEvent, new ToolTipEventHandler(CVPToolTip));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(LabelPresenter), System.Windows.Controls.Control.MouseDoubleClickEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(Field_DoubleClick));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(LabelPresenter), UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(Field_SingleClick));
		}

		private static bool IsValidKey(Key key)
		{
			if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Alt || Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Control || Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.Windows)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (key >= Key.A && key <= Key.Z)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (key >= Key.NumPad0 && key <= Key.NumPad9)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (Keyboard.Modifiers == ModifierKeys.None && key >= Key.D0 && key <= Key.D9)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (key == Key.Back || key == Key.Delete || key == Key.Space)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private Cell GetTopLeftSelectedCell()
		{
			//IL_00ff: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0106: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01f6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01fd: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Cell val = null;
			if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Cells).Count > 0)
			{
				val = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Cells[0];
				foreach (Cell cell in ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Cells)
				{
					if (cell.Field.Index < val.Field.Index && ((Record)cell.Record).VisibleIndex < ((Record)val.Record).VisibleIndex)
					{
						val = cell;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records).Count > 0)
			{
				Record obj = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records[0];
				DataRecord val2 = (DataRecord)(object)((obj is DataRecord) ? obj : null);
				foreach (DataRecord record in ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records)
				{
					DataRecord val3 = record;
					if (((Record)val3).VisibleIndex < ((Record)val2).VisibleIndex)
					{
						val2 = val3;
					}
				}
				val = val2.Cells[0];
				foreach (Cell item in (IEnumerable<Cell>)val2.Cells)
				{
					if (item.Field.Index < val.Field.Index)
					{
						val = item;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Fields).Count > 0)
			{
				Field val4 = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Fields[0];
				Record obj2 = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records[0];
				val = ((DataRecord)((obj2 is DataRecord) ? obj2 : null)).Cells[val4];
				foreach (DataRecord item2 in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records)
				{
					DataRecord val5 = item2;
					if (((Record)val5).VisibleIndex < ((Record)val.Record).VisibleIndex)
					{
						val = val5.Cells[val4];
					}
				}
			}
			else if (((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).ActiveCell != null)
			{
				val = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).ActiveCell;
			}
			return val;
		}

		private Cell GetCellRelativeTo(Cell referenceCell, int rowOffset, int columnOffset)
		{
			//IL_0055: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (rowOffset == 0 && columnOffset == 0)
			{
				return referenceCell;
			}
			int visibleIndex = ((Record)referenceCell.Record).VisibleIndex;
			int index = referenceCell.Field.Index;
			if (UseHorizontalOrientation)
			{
				visibleIndex += columnOffset;
				index += rowOffset;
			}
			else
			{
				visibleIndex += rowOffset;
				index += columnOffset;
			}
			Cell result = null;
			foreach (DataRecord item in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records)
			{
				DataRecord val = item;
				if (((Record)val).VisibleIndex != visibleIndex)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (Cell item2 in (IEnumerable<Cell>)val.Cells)
				{
					if (item2.Field.Index == index)
					{
						result = item2;
						break;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static List<List<string>> DecodeClipboard(string text, char fieldSeparator)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			List<List<string>> list2 = new List<List<string>>();
			list2.Add(list);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			for (int i = 0; i < text.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = text[i];
				if (stringBuilder.Length == 0 && c == '"')
				{
					for (i++; i < text.Length; i++)
					{
						c = text[i];
						if (c != '"')
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(c);
							continue;
						}
						if (i + 1 >= text.Length || text[i + 1] != '"')
						{
							break;
						}
						i++;
						stringBuilder.Append('"');
					}
				}
				else if (c == '\r')
				{
					if (i + 1 < text.Length && text[i + 1] == '\n')
					{
						i++;
						if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
						{
							list.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
							stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
						}
						list = new List<string>();
						list2.Add(list);
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
				}
				else if (c == fieldSeparator)
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length != 0)
					{
						list.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
					}
					else
					{
						list.Add(null);
					}
					stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
			{
				list.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
			}
			return list2;
		}

		private void CreateTextClipboard(ref System.Windows.DataObject dataObject, List<List<Cell>> selectedCells)
		{
			char[] anyOf = new char[4] { '\t', '"', '\n', '\r' };
			char value = '\t';
			bool flag = ((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Fields).Count > 0 || ((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records).Count > 0 || _IsAllSelected;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (flag && !UseHorizontalOrientation)
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (List<Cell> selectedCell in selectedCells)
				{
					int count = selectedCell.Count;
					if (count > num)
					{
						num = count;
					}
				}
				bool flag2 = true;
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					foreach (List<Cell> selectedCell2 in selectedCells)
					{
						if (selectedCell2.Count <= i)
						{
							continue;
						}
						Cell val = selectedCell2[i];
						if (val == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (!flag2)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(value);
						}
						string text = ((FieldItem)val.Field).Label.ToString();
						if (text.IndexOfAny(anyOf) < 0)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(text);
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.Append('"');
							string text2 = text;
							foreach (char c in text2)
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(c);
								if (c == '"')
								{
									stringBuilder.Append(c);
								}
							}
							stringBuilder.Append('"');
						}
						flag2 = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(Environment.NewLine);
			}
			bool flag3 = true;
			foreach (List<Cell> selectedCell3 in selectedCells)
			{
				if (!flag3 && selectedCell3.Count > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(Environment.NewLine);
				}
				bool flag4 = true;
				foreach (Cell item in selectedCell3)
				{
					if (flag4 && flag && UseHorizontalOrientation)
					{
						if (item != null && item.Field != null && ((FieldItem)item.Field).Label != null)
						{
							string text3 = ((FieldItem)item.Field).Label.ToString();
							if (text3.IndexOfAny(anyOf) < 0)
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(text3);
							}
							else
							{
								stringBuilder.Append('"');
								string text2 = text3;
								foreach (char c2 in text2)
								{
									stringBuilder.Append(c2);
									if (c2 == '"')
									{
										stringBuilder.Append(c2);
									}
								}
								stringBuilder.Append('"');
							}
						}
						flag4 = false;
					}
					if (!flag4)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(value);
					}
					string text4 = string.Empty;
					if (item != null && item.Value != null)
					{
						text4 = ((!(item.Value is Hyperlink)) ? item.Value.ToString() : ((item.Value as Hyperlink).Tag as HyperLinkInfo).Text);
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
					{
						if (text4.IndexOfAny(anyOf) < 0)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(text4);
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.Append('"');
							string text2 = text4;
							foreach (char c3 in text2)
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(c3);
								if (c3 == '"')
								{
									stringBuilder.Append(c3);
								}
							}
							stringBuilder.Append('"');
						}
					}
					flag4 = false;
				}
				flag3 = false;
			}
			string data = stringBuilder.ToString();
			dataObject.SetData(System.Windows.DataFormats.UnicodeText, data);
		}

		private void CreateCsvClipboard(ref System.Windows.DataObject dataObject, List<List<Cell>> selectedCells)

			bool flag = ((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Fields).Count > 0 || ((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records).Count > 0 || _IsAllSelected;

					}

								{

								}
							}
							stringBuilder.Append('"');
						}
					}
					flag3 = false;
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(Environment.NewLine);
			}
			string s = stringBuilder.ToString();
			UTF8Encoding uTF8Encoding = new UTF8Encoding();
			dataObject.SetData(System.Windows.DataFormats.CommaSeparatedValue, new MemoryStream(uTF8Encoding.GetBytes(s)));
		}

		private void CreateCxsClipboard(ref System.Windows.DataObject dataObject, List<List<Cell>> selectedCells)
		{
			if (selectedCells == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (List<Cell> selectedCell in selectedCells)
			{
				foreach (Cell item in selectedCell)
				{
					if (item == null || item.Record == null || item.Field == null || !(item.Record.DataItem is RowProxy row))
					{
						continue;
					}
					RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item.Field).Name);
					if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					CellCache cellCache = null;
					try
					{
						cellCache = _GridCache.GetCellCache(row, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					if (cellCache != null)
					{
						string path = cellCache.Path;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) && path[0] != '#')
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			new XmlWriterSettings().OmitXmlDeclaration = true;
			XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(stringBuilder);
			xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Selection");
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Format", "Grid");
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Product", Case.ApplicationName);
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("FileMoniker", Case.CaseFullFilePath);
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Items");
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records).Count > 0 || ((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Fields).Count > 0)
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (flag2 && !UseHorizontalOrientation && selectedCells.Count > 0)
			{
				List<Cell> list = selectedCells[0];
				if (list != null)
				{
					int num3 = 0;
					foreach (Cell item2 in list)
					{
						if (item2 != null || item2.Field != null)
						{
							xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Text");
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Row", num2.ToString());
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Col", num3.ToString());
							xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Value", (((FieldItem)item2.Field).Label != null) ? ((FieldItem)item2.Field).Label.ToString() : string.Empty);
							xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
						}
						num3++;
					}
					num2++;
				}
			}
			int num4 = -1;
			int num5 = -1;
			if (!flag2)
			{
				foreach (List<Cell> selectedCell2 in selectedCells)
				{
					if (selectedCell2 == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (Cell item3 in selectedCell2)
					{
						if (item3 != null)
						{
							int num6 = (UseHorizontalOrientation ? item3.Field.Index : ((Record)item3.Record).Index);
							int num7 = (UseHorizontalOrientation ? ((Record)item3.Record).Index : item3.Field.Index);
							num4 = ((num4 == -1) ? num6 : ((num6 < num4) ? num6 : num4));
							num5 = ((num5 == -1) ? num7 : ((num7 < num5) ? num7 : num5));
						}
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (List<Cell> selectedCell3 in selectedCells)
			{
				num = 0;
				bool flag3 = true;
				foreach (Cell item4 in selectedCell3)
				{
					if (item4 == null || item4.Record == null || item4.Field == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (flag3 && flag2 && UseHorizontalOrientation)
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Text");
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Row", num2.ToString());
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Col", num.ToString());
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Value", (((FieldItem)item4.Field).Label != null) ? ((FieldItem)item4.Field).Label.ToString() : string.Empty);
						xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
						num++;
					}
					flag3 = false;
					if (!(item4.Record.DataItem is RowProxy row2))
					{
						continue;
					}
					RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor2 = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item4.Field).Name);
					if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor2 == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					CellCache cellCache2 = null;
					try
					{
						cellCache2 = _GridCache.GetCellCache(row2, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor2);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
					if (cellCache2 == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					string path2 = cellCache2.Path;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path2) || path2[0] == '#')
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Text");
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Row", flag2 ? num2.ToString() : (UseHorizontalOrientation ? (item4.Field.Index - num4).ToString() : (((Record)item4.Record).Index - num4).ToString()));
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Col", flag2 ? num.ToString() : (UseHorizontalOrientation ? (((Record)item4.Record).Index - num5).ToString() : (item4.Field.Index - num5).ToString()));
						string value = string.Empty;
						if (item4.Value != null)
						{
							value = ((!(item4.Value is Hyperlink)) ? item4.Value.ToString() : ((item4.Value as Hyperlink).Tag as HyperLinkInfo).Text);
						}
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Value", value);
						xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
					}
					else
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Item");
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Row", flag2 ? num2.ToString() : (UseHorizontalOrientation ? (item4.Field.Index - num4).ToString() : (((Record)item4.Record).Index - num4).ToString()));
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Col", flag2 ? num.ToString() : (UseHorizontalOrientation ? (((Record)item4.Record).Index - num5).ToString() : (item4.Field.Index - num5).ToString()));
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Attribute", "Value");
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Path", "cxs:" + path2);
						xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
					}
					num++;
				}
				num2++;
			}
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlWriter.WriteEndDocument();
			xmlWriter.Flush();
			xmlWriter.Close();
			UTF8Encoding uTF8Encoding = new UTF8Encoding();
			dataObject.SetData(System.Windows.Forms.DataFormats.GetFormat("ASI Format").Name, new MemoryStream(uTF8Encoding.GetBytes(stringBuilder.ToString())));
		}

		private static List<List<Cell>> GetSelectedCells(XamDataGrid grid)
		{
			//IL_0090: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_013c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0143: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (((DataPresenterBase)grid).SelectedItems == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<Cell> list = new List<Cell>();
			if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)grid).SelectedItems.Cells).Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (Cell cell in ((DataPresenterBase)grid).SelectedItems.Cells)
				{
					list.Add(cell);
				}
			}
			else if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)grid).SelectedItems.Records).Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (DataRecord record in ((DataPresenterBase)grid).SelectedItems.Records)
				{
					foreach (Cell item in (IEnumerable<Cell>)record.Cells)
					{
						if (((FieldItem)item.Field).Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
						{
							list.Add(item);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (((SelectedItemCollectionBase)((DataPresenterBase)grid).SelectedItems.Fields).Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (Field field in ((DataPresenterBase)grid).SelectedItems.Fields)
				{
					foreach (DataRecord item2 in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)grid).Records)
					{
						DataRecord val = item2;
						if (((Record)val).VisibleIndex >= 0)
						{
							list.Add(val.Cells[field]);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (((DataPresenterBase)grid).ActiveCell != null)
			{
				list.Add(((DataPresenterBase)grid).ActiveCell);
			}
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = ((Record)list[0].Record).VisibleIndex;
			int num2 = list[0].Field.Index;
			foreach (Cell item3 in list)
			{
				int index = item3.Field.Index;
				int visibleIndex = ((Record)item3.Record).VisibleIndex;
				if (visibleIndex < num)
				{
					num = visibleIndex;
				}
				if (index < num2)
				{
					num2 = index;
				}
			}
			if (grid.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				int num3 = num;
				num = num2;
				num2 = num3;
			}
			List<List<Cell>> list2 = new List<List<Cell>>();
			foreach (Cell item4 in list)
			{
				int num4 = item4.Field.Index;
				int num5 = ((Record)item4.Record).VisibleIndex;
				if (grid.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					int num6 = num5;
					num5 = num4;
					num4 = num6;
				}
				num5 -= num;
				num4 -= num2;
				for (int i = list2.Count; i < num5 + 1; i++)
				{
					list2.Add(new List<Cell>());
				}
				List<Cell> list3 = list2[num5];
				for (int j = list3.Count; j < num4 + 1; j++)
				{
					list3.Add(null);
				}
				list3[num4] = item4;
			}
			return list2;
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_FieldLayoutInitialized(object sender, FieldLayoutInitializedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_HaveGridData && _GridCache != null)
			{
				_HaveGridData = _GridCache.GetAllGridData();
			}
			UpdateFields(GetUnits: false);
		}

		private static void Field_SingleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			LabelPresenter val = (LabelPresenter)((sender is LabelPresenter) ? sender : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				DataPresenterBase dataPresenter = ((DataItemPresenter)val).DataPresenter;
				XamDataGrid val2 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter : null);
				if (GetSummaryGridControl(val2) != null && val2 != null)
				{
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).ActiveCell = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void Field_DoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0039: Expected O, but got Unknown
			LabelPresenter val = (LabelPresenter)((sender is LabelPresenter) ? sender : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SortStatus sortStatus = ((DataItemPresenter)val).Field.SortStatus;
			DataPresenterBase dataPresenter = ((DataItemPresenter)val).DataPresenter;
			XamDataGrid val2 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter : null);
			if (val2 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = GetSummaryGridControl(val2);
			if (summaryGridControl != null)
			{
				FieldSortDescription val3 = new FieldSortDescription();
				val3.FieldName = ((FieldItem)((DataItemPresenter)val).Field).Name;
				val3.Field = ((DataItemPresenter)val).Field;
				switch (sortStatus)
				{
				case SortStatus.NotSorted:
					val3.Direction = ListSortDirection.Ascending;
					break;
				case SortStatus.Ascending:
					val3.Direction = ListSortDirection.Descending;
					break;
				case SortStatus.Descending:
					val3.Direction = ListSortDirection.Ascending;
					break;
				}
				SortField(val3, val2);
				e.Handled = true;
				summaryGridControl._hLinksNeedUpdate = summaryGridControl.HasHyperlinks;
			}
		}

		private string CreateDraggingText()
		{
			int? num = null;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			foreach (List<Cell> item in _SelectedCellsforDrag)
			{
				foreach (Cell item2 in item)
				{
					int index = ((Record)item2.Record).Index;
					object value = item2.Value;
					string value2 = ((value == null) ? string.Empty : value.ToString());
					if (!num.HasValue)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(value2);
					}
					else if (num.Value == index)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append('\t').Append(value2);
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendLine().Append(value2);
					}
					num = index;
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private string CreateDraggingXml()
		{
			if (_SelectedCellsforDrag == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			XmlWriterSettings xmlWriterSettings = new XmlWriterSettings();
			xmlWriterSettings.Encoding = new UTF8Encoding(encoderShouldEmitUTF8Identifier: false);
			xmlWriterSettings.ConformanceLevel = ConformanceLevel.Document;
			xmlWriterSettings.Indent = true;
			XmlWriter xmlWriter = XmlWriter.Create(memoryStream, xmlWriterSettings);
			xmlWriter.WriteStartDocument();
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Selection");
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("FileMoniker", Case.CaseFullFilePath.ToString());
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Product", Case.ApplicationName.ToString());
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Items");
			foreach (List<Cell> item in _SelectedCellsforDrag)
			{
				foreach (Cell item2 in item)
				{
					RowProxy rowProxy = item2.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
					RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = rowProxy.GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item2.Field).Name);
					CellCache cellCache = _GridCache.GetCellCache(rowProxy, property);
					if (!cellCache.Path.StartsWith("#"))
					{
						xmlWriter.WriteStartElement("Item");
						xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Path", cellCache.Path.ToString());
						xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
					}
				}
			}
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
			xmlWriter.Flush();
			xmlWriter.Close();
			return Encoding.UTF8.GetString(memoryStream.ToArray());
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_DragOver(object sender, System.Windows.DragEventArgs e)
		{
			_DropInfo = null;
			string asiFormatTextFromDataObject = GetAsiFormatTextFromDataObject(e.Data);
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(asiFormatTextFromDataObject))
			{
				ParseCxsPathFromAsiFormat(asiFormatTextFromDataObject, Case.CaseFullFilePath).ToArray();
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_Drop(object sender, System.Windows.DragEventArgs e)
		{
			TabbedSummaryGrid.PasteVariables(IsFromClipboard: false, _DropInfo);
		}

		public IList<string> ParseCxsPathFromAsiFormat(string asiFormatText, string baseCaseFilePath)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(asiFormatText))
			{
				try
				{
					_DropInfo = new DropVarsInfo();
					XDocument xDocument = XDocument.Parse(asiFormatText);
					if (xDocument != null)
					{
						XElement xElement = xDocument.Root.Element("FileMoniker");
						if (xElement != null && string.Equals(xElement.Value, baseCaseFilePath, StringComparison.CurrentCulture))
						{
							_DropInfo.FileMoniker = xElement.Value;
							List<string> list = new List<string>();
							IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable = xDocument.Root.XPathSelectElements("Items/Item");
							if (enumerable != null)
							{
								foreach (XElement item in enumerable)
								{
									XElement xElement2 = item.XPathSelectElement("Path");
									if (xElement2 != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(xElement2.Value) && !list.Contains(xElement2.Value))
									{
										list.Add(xElement2.Value);
									}
								}
							}
							_DropInfo.Paths = list.ToArray();
							return list;
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					return null;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static string GetAsiFormatTextFromDataObject(System.Windows.IDataObject dataObject)
		{
			if (dataObject != null)
			{
				object data = dataObject.GetData("ASI Format");
				if (data is string result)
				{
					return result;
				}
				if (data is Stream stream)
				{
					using StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(stream);
					return streamReader.ReadToEnd();
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_DataError(object sender, DataErrorEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_DoingPaste)
			{
				ShowInputErrorMessageBox(e.Exception.Message);
				((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_CellsInViewChanged(object sender, CellsInViewChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_hLinksNeedUpdate = HasHyperlinks;
		}

		private void OnPreviewMouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((_popUp != null && _popUp.IsOpen) || !_IsMouseClickSelected || _SelectedCellsforDrag.Count == 0 || Mouse.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed || !((e.GetPosition((IInputElement)_summaryGrid) - _mouseDownPosition).Length >= 10.0))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				foreach (List<Cell> item in _SelectedCellsforDrag)
				{
					foreach (Cell item2 in item)
					{
						item2.IsSelected = true;
					}
				}
				System.Windows.DataObject dataObject = new System.Windows.DataObject();
				dataObject.SetData(System.Windows.DataFormats.Text, CreateDraggingText());
				dataObject.SetData("ASI Format", CreateDraggingXml());
				DragDrop.DoDragDrop(this, dataObject, System.Windows.DragDropEffects.Copy);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		public HitTestResultBehavior HitTestResultFunc(HitTestResult result)
		{
			DependencyObject visualHit = result.VisualHit;
			Clicked = ((visualHit is CellValuePresenter) ? visualHit : null);
			if (Clicked != null)
			{
				return HitTestResultBehavior.Stop;
			}
			Clicked = ((visualHit is LabelPresenter) ? visualHit : null);
			if (Clicked != null)
			{
				return HitTestResultBehavior.Stop;
			}
			if (visualHit is Grid { Uid: "RecordSelectorGrid" } grid)
			{
				Clicked = grid.Tag;
				return HitTestResultBehavior.Stop;
			}
			return HitTestResultBehavior.Continue;
		}

		public HitTestFilterBehavior HitTestFilterFunc(DependencyObject potentialHitTestTarget)
		{
			return HitTestFilterBehavior.Continue;
		}

		private void DoPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_IsMouseClickSelected = false;
			_mouseDownPosition = e.GetPosition((IInputElement)_summaryGrid);
			Clicked = null;
			if (_popUp == null || !_popUp.IsOpen)
			{
				VisualTreeHelper.HitTest((Visual)(object)_summaryGrid, HitTestFilterFunc, HitTestResultFunc, new PointHitTestParameters(e.GetPosition((UIElement)sender)));
				if (Clicked is CellValuePresenter)
				{
					object clicked = Clicked;
					CellValuePresenter val = (CellValuePresenter)((clicked is CellValuePresenter) ? clicked : null);
					_IsMouseClickSelected = val.IsSelected;
				}
				else if (Clicked is LabelPresenter)
				{
					object clicked2 = Clicked;
					Field field = ((DataItemPresenter)((clicked2 is LabelPresenter) ? clicked2 : null)).Field;
					_IsMouseClickSelected = field.IsSelected;
				}
				else if (Clicked is int)
				{
					int num = (int)Clicked;
					_IsMouseClickSelected = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records[num].IsSelected;
				}
				_SelectedCellsforDrag = GetSelectedCells(_summaryGrid) ?? new List<List<Cell>>();
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_SelectedItemsChanged(object sender, SelectedItemsChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnSelectedItemsChanged();
		}

		private void OnSelectedItemsChanged()
		{
			if (this.SelectedItemsChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectedItemsChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_CellChanged(object sender, CellChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			XamComboEditor xamComboEditor = e.Editor as XamComboEditor;
			if (sender != null)
			{
				XamDataGrid val = (XamDataGrid)((sender is XamDataGrid) ? sender : null);
				if (xamComboEditor != null)
				{
					((DataPresenterBase)val).ExecuteCommand(DataPresenterCommands.EndEditModeAndAcceptChanges);
					((UIElement)(object)val).Focus();
				}
				else if (e.Editor is XamCheckEditor)
				{
					((DataPresenterBase)val).ExecuteCommand(DataPresenterCommands.EndEditModeAndAcceptChanges);
				}
			}
		}

		{
			_IsAllSelected = false;
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_EditModeEnded(object sender, EditModeEndedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell != null && ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.Record != null && ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.Record.DataItem != null && ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.Field != null)
			{
				RowProxy row = ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
				RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.Field).Name);
				CellCache cellCache = _GridCache.GetCellCache(row, property);
				if (cellCache != null)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					if (!((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.IsDataChanged && _popUp != null && _popUp.UnitsChanged)
					{
						try
						{
							flag = true;
							Case.BatchSet(cellCache.Path, "value", _popUp.SelectedUnits, ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.Value);
						}
						catch (CxsException)
						{
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
					cellCache.ResetValueCache();
					if (flag)
					{
						TabbedSummaryGrid.TriggerOnBatchSetEvent();
						RefreshDisplay(resetDataCache: false);
					}
				}
			}
			if (_popUp != null)
			{
				PopUpSubscribeToEvent(IsEnabled: false);
				_popUp.IsOpen = false;
				_popUp = null;
			}
		}

		private bool IsMouseOverPopUp()
		{
			if (_popUp == null || _popUp.Combo == null || !_popUp.IsOpen)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_popUp.IsFocused || _popUp.IsMouseCaptured || _popUp.IsMouseCaptureWithin || _popUp.IsMouseDirectlyOver || _popUp.IsMouseOver || _popUp.Combo.IsFocused || _popUp.Combo.IsMouseCaptured || _popUp.Combo.IsMouseCaptureWithin || _popUp.Combo.IsMouseDirectlyOver || _popUp.Combo.IsMouseOver)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_EditModeEnding(object sender, EditModeEndingEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_popUp != null && !_editModeEndedTriggeredByPopUp && IsMouseOverPopUp())
			{
				e.Cancel = true;
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsValidKey(e.Key) && _summaryGrid != null)
			{
				Cell activeCell = ((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).ActiveCell;
				if (activeCell != null)
				{
					((ValuePresenter)(object)CellValuePresenter.FromCell(activeCell))?.StartEditMode();
				}
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_EditModeValidationError(object sender, EditModeValidationErrorEventArgs e)
		{
			e.InvalidValueBehavior = InvalidValueBehavior.RevertValue;
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.ErrorMessage))
			{
				((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_InitializeRecord(object sender, InitializeRecordEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_HaveGridData)
			{
				_HaveGridData = _GridCache.GetAllGridData();
			}
			if (e.ReInitialize)
			{
				((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
				return;
			}
			Record record = e.Record;
			DataRecord val = (DataRecord)(object)((record is DataRecord) ? record : null);
			if (val != null && val.DataItem is RowProxy rowProxy)
			{
				if (!HasHyperlinks && rowProxy.HasDetailTab)
				{
					HasHyperlinks = true;
				}
				int num = 0;
				foreach (Cell item in (IEnumerable<Cell>)val.Cells)
				{
					if (_FieldStyleDictionary.ContainsKey(((FieldItem)item.Field).Name))
					{
						item.EditorStyle = _FieldStyleDictionary[((FieldItem)item.Field).Name];
						continue;
					}
					RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item.Field).Name);
					CellCache cellCache = _GridCache.GetCellCache(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
					if (cellCache != null && cellCache.Path.StartsWith("#") && !rowProxy.HasDetailTab)
					{
						item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["String"];
					}
					else
					{
						bool? flag = _GridCache.HasOptionList(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
						if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
						{
							item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Combo"];
						}
						else if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn.IsCurrency)
						{
							item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Currency"];
						}
						else
						{
							switch (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn.DataType)
							{
							case ColumnDataType.Integer:
								item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Int"];
								break;
							case ColumnDataType.Real:
								item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Real"];
								break;
							case ColumnDataType.String:
								item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["String"];
								break;
							case ColumnDataType.Boolean:
								item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Check"];
								break;
							default:
								if (!rowProxy.HasDetailTab)
								{
									if (item.Value != null && double.TryParse(item.Value.ToString(), out var _))
									{
										item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["Real"];
									}
									else
									{
										item.EditorStyle = StyleDictionary["String"];
									}
								}
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					_FieldStyleDictionary.Add(((FieldItem)item.Field).Name, item.EditorStyle);
					num++;
				}
			}
			((RoutedEventArgs)(object)e).Handled = true;
		}

		private static void ShowInputErrorMessageBox(string message)
		{
			if (message == "BatchSet failed")
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(Global.GetString("SummaryGridControl_BatchSetFailed"), Global.GetString("SummaryGridControl_InputError"), MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(message, Global.GetString("SummaryGridControl_InputError"), MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			}
		}

		private void _GridCache_DataInputExceptionRaised(object sender, DataInputExceptionEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_DoingPaste)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (e.Exception.InnerException != null)
			{
				string message = e.Exception.InnerException.Message;
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(message))
				{
					ShowInputErrorMessageBox(message);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				ShowInputErrorMessageBox(e.Exception.Message);
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		internal SummaryGridColumnCollection GetAdjacentColumns(SummaryGridColumn referenceColumn)
		{
			return SummaryColumns;
		}

		internal SummaryGridRowCollection GetAdjacentRows(SummaryGridRow referenceRow)
		{
			return SummaryRows;
		}

		}

		private void UpdateforOrientationChange(bool setTabToDirty = false)
		{
			((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).ClearCustomizations((CustomizationType)4);
			((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).DataSource = null;
			RefreshDisplay(resetDataCache: false);
			if (_summaryGrid.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				UseHorizontalOrientation = true;
			}
			else
			{
				UseHorizontalOrientation = false;
			}
			SummaryGridTab curSumGridTab = _tabbedSummaryGrid.GetCurSumGridTab();
			if (curSumGridTab != null)
			{
				curSumGridTab.IsHorizontal = UseHorizontalOrientation;
				if (setTabToDirty)
				{
					curSumGridTab.IsDirty = true;
				}
				_tabbedSummaryGrid.UpdateComboboxNames();
				_tabbedSummaryGrid.UpdateToolbarButtons();
			}
		}

		private void CVP_SizeChanged(object sender, SizeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			CellValuePresenter val = (CellValuePresenter)((sender is CellValuePresenter) ? sender : null);
			if (val != null && ((ContentControl)(object)val).Content is Hyperlink)
			{
				RefreshHyperlink(val);
			}
		}

		private void RefreshHyperlink(CellValuePresenter cvp)
		{
			if (cvp == null || ((DataItemPresenter)cvp).Field == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataRecord record = ((DataItemPresenter)cvp).Record;
			if (record == null || !(record.DataItem is RowProxy { GridCache: not null } rowProxy))
			{
				return;
			}
			RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = rowProxy.GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)((DataItemPresenter)cvp).Field).Name);
			if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CellCache cellCache = rowProxy.GridCache.GetCellCache(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
			if (cellCache != null && cellCache.Value != null)
			{
				HyperLinkInfo hyperLinkInfo = null;
				if (cellCache.Value is Hyperlink)
				{
					hyperLinkInfo = (cellCache.Value as Hyperlink).Tag as HyperLinkInfo;
					Hyperlink hyperlink = new Hyperlink(new Run(hyperLinkInfo.Text));
					hyperlink.ToolTip = hyperLinkInfo.Text;
					hyperlink.Click += Hyperlink_Click;
					hyperlink.Tag = hyperLinkInfo;
					((ContentControl)(object)cvp).Content = null;
					((ContentControl)(object)cvp).Content = hyperlink;
				}
				else
				{
					hyperLinkInfo = new HyperLinkInfo(string.Copy(cellCache.Value.ToString()), rowProxy.GridRow.DetailTab);
					Hyperlink hyperlink2 = new Hyperlink(new Run(string.Copy(cellCache.Value.ToString())));
					hyperlink2.ToolTip = string.Copy(cellCache.Value.ToString());
					hyperlink2.Click += Hyperlink_Click;
					hyperlink2.Tag = hyperLinkInfo;
					((ContentControl)(object)cvp).Content = null;
					((ContentControl)(object)cvp).Content = hyperlink2;
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddHyperlinks()
		{
			//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			foreach (DataRecord item in (IEnumerable<Record>)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records)
			{
				foreach (Cell item2 in (IEnumerable<Cell>)item.Cells)
				{
					if (item2.Field.Index == 0)
					{
						RefreshHyperlink(CellValuePresenter.FromCell(item2));
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_hLinksNeedUpdate = HasHyperlinks;
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_LayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (_hLinksNeedUpdate)
			{
				AddHyperlinks();
				_hLinksNeedUpdate = false;
			}
			if (_rowStatusNeedsUpdate)
			{
				_GridCache.UpdateRowEEStatus();
				_rowStatusNeedsUpdate = false;
			}
		}

		private void DoPaste()
		{
			bool suppressDialogs = PasteFromClipboard();
			TabbedSummaryGrid.PasteVariables(IsFromClipboard: true, null, suppressDialogs);
		}

		private void SelectAll(XamDataGrid thisgrid)
		{
			((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
			((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
			((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
			((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).ActiveCell = null;
			SelectAllRecords(((DataPresenterBase)thisgrid).Records);
		}

		private void SelectAll()
		{
			((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
			((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
			((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
			if (!_ClearSelected)
			{
				SelectAllRecords(((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).Records);
				_IsAllSelected = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_ClearSelected = false;
				_IsAllSelected = false;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateFields(bool GetUnits)
		{
			if (_GridCache == null || _summaryGrid == null || ((Collection<FieldLayout>)(object)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).FieldLayouts).Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (GetUnits)
			{
				_GridCache.BatchGetColUnits();
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (Field item in (Collection<Field>)(object)((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).FieldLayouts[0].Fields)
			{
				RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)item).Name);
				if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor != null && rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn.UnitsDirty)
				{
					((FieldItem)item).Label = _GridCache.FormatColumnCaption(rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn);
					rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn.UnitsDirty = false;
				}
				double num2 = MeasureStringWidth(((FieldItem)item).Label.ToString());
				if (num2 > num)
				{
					num = num2;
				}
			}
			((DataPresenterBase)_summaryGrid).FieldSettings.LabelWidth = (int)num;
		}

		private double MeasureStringWidth(string testString)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(testString))
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			int num = 20;
			return new FormattedText(testString, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, System.Windows.FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(base.FontFamily, base.FontStyle, base.FontWeight, base.FontStretch), base.FontSize, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black).Width + (double)num;
		}

		{
			GridResources_LClick(sender, e);
		}

		private void TextBlock_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)

			GridResources_RClick(sender, e);
		}

		private void GridResources_LClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (TabbedSummaryGrid.SelectedSummaryGrid == this)
			{
				if (_IsAllSelected)
				{
					_ClearSelected = true;
				}
				SelectAll();
			}
		}

		{
			if (TabbedSummaryGrid.SelectedSummaryGrid == this && sender is TextBlock textBlock)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
				{
					Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_Copy,
					Command = CopyCommand,
					CommandTarget = (IInputElement)_summaryGrid,
					InputGestureText = "Ctrl+C",
					IsEnabled = _IsAllSelected,
					Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
					{
						Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.Copy16.ToImageSource()
					}
				});
				contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
				{
					Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_Paste,
					Command = PasteCommand,
					CommandTarget = (IInputElement)_summaryGrid,
					InputGestureText = "Ctrl+V",
					IsEnabled = CanDoPaste(this),
					Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
					{
						Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.Paste16.ToImageSource()
					}
				});
				textBlock.ContextMenu = null;
				textBlock.ContextMenu = contextMenu;
				textBlock.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
			}
		}

		private void SelectAllRecords(RecordCollectionBase records)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_001d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0043: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0075: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_004b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0051: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (Record item in (IEnumerable<Record>)records)
			{
				if ((int)item.RecordType == 1 || (int)item.RecordType == 2)
				{
					GroupByRecord val = (GroupByRecord)item;
					if (((Record)val).HasChildren)
					{
						SelectAllRecords((RecordCollectionBase)(object)val.ChildRecords);
					}
				}
				else if ((int)item.RecordType == 0)
				{
					DataRecord val2 = (DataRecord)item;
					((Record)val2).IsSelected = true;
					if (((Record)val2).HasChildren)
					{
						SelectAllRecords((RecordCollectionBase)(object)val2.ChildRecords);
					}
				}
				else if ((int)item.RecordType == 3)
				{
					ExpandableFieldRecord val3 = (ExpandableFieldRecord)item;
					((Record)val3).IsSelected = true;
					if (((Record)val3).HasChildren)
					{
						SelectAllRecords(val3.ChildRecords);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void SelectColumnorRow(object parameter, bool SelectColumn)
		{
			if (parameter == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (parameter.GetType().Name)
			{
			case "LabelPresenter":
			{
				LabelPresenter val3 = (LabelPresenter)((parameter is LabelPresenter) ? parameter : null);
				if (val3 != null)
				{
					DataPresenterBase dataPresenter2 = ((DataItemPresenter)val3).DataPresenter;
					XamDataGrid val4 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter2 is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter2 : null);
					if (val4 != null && val4 == _summaryGrid)
					{
						((DataPresenterBase)val4).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)val4).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)val4).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)val4).ActiveCell = null;
						((DataPresenterBase)val4).SelectedItems.Fields.Add(((DataItemPresenter)val3).Field);
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "CellValuePresenter":
			{
				CellValuePresenter val5 = (CellValuePresenter)((parameter is CellValuePresenter) ? parameter : null);
				if (val5 == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				DataPresenterBase dataPresenter3 = ((DataItemPresenter)val5).DataPresenter;
				XamDataGrid val6 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter3 is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter3 : null);
				if (val6 != null && val6 == _summaryGrid)
				{
					((DataPresenterBase)val6).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val6).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val6).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val6).ActiveCell = null;
					if (val6.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal && SelectColumn)
					{
						((Record)((DataItemPresenter)val5).Record).IsSelected = true;
						val5.IsActive = true;
					}
					else if (val6.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical && SelectColumn)
					{
						((DataItemPresenter)val5).Field.IsSelected = true;
					}
					else if (val6.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal && !SelectColumn)
					{
						((DataItemPresenter)val5).Field.IsSelected = true;
					}
					else if (val6.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical && !SelectColumn)
					{
						((Record)((DataItemPresenter)val5).Record).IsSelected = true;
						val5.IsActive = true;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "DataRecordPresenter":
			{
				DataRecordPresenter val = (DataRecordPresenter)((parameter is DataRecordPresenter) ? parameter : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					DataPresenterBase dataPresenter = ((RecordPresenter)val).DataPresenter;
					XamDataGrid val2 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter : null);
					if (val2 != null && val2 == _summaryGrid)
					{
						((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
						((DataPresenterBase)val2).ActiveCell = null;
						((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Records.Add(((RecordPresenter)val).Record);
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_EditModeStarting(object sender, EditModeStartingEventArgs e)
		{
			Cell cell = ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell;
			if (cell == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			RowProxy rowProxy = cell.Record.DataItem as RowProxy;
			RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = _GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)cell.Field).Name.ToString());
			if (rowProxy == null || rowProxyPropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool? flag = _GridCache.IsReadOnly(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
			{
				e.Cancel = true;
				return;
			}
			if (((EditModeEventArgs)e).Editor is XamComboEditor xamComboEditor)
			{
				string[] array = _GridCache.GetDisplayOptionList(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
				if (array == null)
				{
					array = _GridCache.GetOptionList(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
				}
				xamComboEditor.ItemsSource = array;
			}
			CellCache cellCache = _GridCache.GetCellCache(rowProxy, rowProxyPropertyDescriptor);
			OpenPopUpCombo(e, cellCache);
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_EditModeStarted(object sender, EditModeStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e != null && ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell != null && ((EditModeEventArgs)e).Editor is XamComboEditor xamComboEditor)
			{
				xamComboEditor.IsDropDownOpen = true;
			}
		}

		private void OpenPopUpCombo(EditModeStartingEventArgs e, CellCache cellcache)
		{
			if (_GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell.Field).Name) == null || cellcache == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string columnUnits = _GridCache.GetColumnUnits(cellcache.GridColumn);
			string[] cellCacheUnitsList = _GridCache.GetCellCacheUnitsList(cellcache);
			if (cellCacheUnitsList == null || cellCacheUnitsList.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!cellCacheUnitsList.Contains(columnUnits))
			{
				columnUnits = _GridCache.GetCellCacheUnits(cellcache);
			}
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			columnUnits = cellcache.Units;
			string[] array = cellCacheUnitsList;
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array[i] == columnUnits)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				num = -1;
			}
			if (_popUp != null)
			{
				_popUp.IsOpen = false;
				PopUpSubscribeToEvent(IsEnabled: false);
				_popUp = null;
			}
			_popUp = new PopupComboBox(cellCacheUnitsList.ToList(), num, columnUnits);
			PopUpSubscribeToEvent(IsEnabled: true);
			_popUp.Placement = PlacementMode.Right;
			CellValuePresenter val = CellValuePresenter.FromCell(((EditModeEventArgs)e).Cell);
			if (val != null && val != null)
			{
				_popUp.PlacementTarget = (UIElement)(object)val;
			}
			_popUp.StaysOpen = true;
			_popUp.OpenCombo();
		}

		private static void FieldContextMenuOpen(object sender, ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			LabelPresenter val = (LabelPresenter)((sender is LabelPresenter) ? sender : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataPresenterBase dataPresenter = ((DataItemPresenter)val).DataPresenter;
			XamDataGrid val2 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter : null);
			SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = GetSummaryGridControl(val2);
			if (val2 == null || summaryGridControl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!((DataItemPresenter)val).Field.IsSelected)
			{
				((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
				((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
				((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
				((DataPresenterBase)val2).ActiveCell = null;
				((DataItemPresenter)val).Field.IsSelected = true;
			}
			System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_Copy,
				Command = CopyCommand,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)val2,
				InputGestureText = "Ctrl+C",
				IsEnabled = ((DataItemPresenter)val).Field.IsSelected,
				Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.Copy16.ToImageSource()
				}
			});
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_Paste,
				Command = PasteCommand,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)val2,
				InputGestureText = "Ctrl+V",
				IsEnabled = CanDoPaste(summaryGridControl),
				Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.Paste16.ToImageSource()
				}
			});
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_SortAscending,
				Command = SortAscendingCommand,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)val2,
				Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.SortAscend16.ToImageSource()
				},
				CommandParameter = val,
				IsEnabled = true
			});
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_SortDescending,
				Command = SortDescendingCommand,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)val2,
				Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.SortDescend16.ToImageSource()
				},
				CommandParameter = val,
				IsEnabled = true
			});
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_ClearSort,
				Command = ClearSortCommand,
				CommandTarget = (IInputElement)val2,
				CommandParameter = ((DataItemPresenter)val).DataPresenter,
				Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.SortClear16.ToImageSource()
				},
				IsEnabled = true
			});
			contextMenu.Items.Add(new Separator());
			System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem menuItem = new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
			{
				Header = Strings.SummaryGridControl_ChangeUnitsEtc
			};
			menuItem.IsEnabled = false;
			menuItem.Icon = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
			{
				Source = AspenTech.AspenCxs.SummaryGrid.Properties.Resources.UnitSets16.ToImageSource()
			};
			RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProxyPropertyDescriptor = summaryGridControl._GridCache.FindColumnProperty(((FieldItem)((DataItemPresenter)val).Field).Name);
			if (rowProxyPropertyDescriptor != null)
			{
				string[] columnUnitsList = summaryGridControl._GridCache.GetColumnUnitsList(rowProxyPropertyDescriptor.GridColumn);
				if (columnUnitsList != null && columnUnitsList.Length != 0)
				{
					menuItem.IsEnabled = true;
					menuItem.Items.Clear();
					string[] array = columnUnitsList;
					foreach (string text in array)
					{
						menuItem.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
						{
							Header = text,
							Command = UnitsChangeCommand,
							CommandTarget = (IInputElement)val2,
							CommandParameter = new object[2]
							{
								((DataItemPresenter)val).Field,
								text
							},
							IsEnabled = true
						});
					}
				}
				else
				{
					menuItem.IsEnabled = false;
				}
			}
			contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem);
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).ContextMenu = null;
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).ContextMenu = contextMenu;
			e.Handled = true;
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
		}

		private static void CellContextMenuOpen(object sender, ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			CellValuePresenter val = (CellValuePresenter)((sender is CellValuePresenter) ? sender : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DataPresenterBase dataPresenter = ((DataItemPresenter)val).DataPresenter;
			XamDataGrid val2 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter : null);
			if (val2 != null && GetSummaryGridControl(val2) != null)
			{
				if (!val.IsSelected && !((DataItemPresenter)val).Field.IsSelected && !((Record)((DataItemPresenter)val).Record).IsSelected)
				{
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Records.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Fields.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).SelectedItems.Cells.Clear();
					((DataPresenterBase)val2).ActiveCell = null;
					val.IsSelected = true;
					val.IsActive = true;

				contextMenu.Items.Add(new System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem
				{

				});

		private void Hyperlink_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Hyperlink hyperlink = e.OriginalSource as Hyperlink;
			if (hyperlink.Tag != null)
			{
				if (hyperlink.Tag.ToString() == "ChangeOrientation")
				{
					UpdateforOrientationChange();
				}
				else if (hyperlink.Tag is HyperLinkInfo)
				{
					string detailTab = (hyperlink.Tag as HyperLinkInfo).DetailTab;
					if (detailTab == null)
					{
						e.Handled = true;
						return;
					}
					TabbedSummaryGrid.MoveToLocation(detailTab, (hyperlink.Tag as HyperLinkInfo).Text);
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private static void CVPToolTip(object sender, ToolTipEventArgs e)
		{
			CellValuePresenter val = (CellValuePresenter)((sender is CellValuePresenter) ? sender : null);
			TextBlock textBlock = new TextBlock();
			if (val.Value != null)
			{
				if (((ContentControl)(object)val).Content is Hyperlink)
				{
					textBlock.Text = ((val.Value as Hyperlink).Tag as HyperLinkInfo).Text;
				}
				else if (((ContentControl)(object)val).Content != null)
				{
					textBlock.Text = ((ContentControl)(object)val).Content.ToString();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				textBlock.Text = "";
			}
			DataPresenterBase dataPresenter = ((DataItemPresenter)val).DataPresenter;
			XamDataGrid val2 = (XamDataGrid)(object)((dataPresenter is XamDataGrid) ? dataPresenter : null);
			if (val2 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = GetSummaryGridControl(val2);
			if (summaryGridControl == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (summaryGridControl._GridCache.Case != null && summaryGridControl._GridCache.Case.ApplicationName == "Aspen Plus")
			{
				try
				{
					if (string.CompareOrdinal(((FieldItem)((DataItemPresenter)val).Field).Name, "Name") == 0 && ((DataItemPresenter)val).Record.DataItem is RowProxy rowProxy)
					{
						string path = rowProxy.GridRow.Path;
						if (summaryGridControl._GridCache.Case != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path))
						{
							List<string> list = new List<string>();
							list.Add(path + ".Input.DESCRIPTION");
							list.Add(path + ".Output.DESCRIPTION");
							CxsVerificationResult[] array = summaryGridControl._GridCache.Case.VerifyPaths(list.ToArray());
							if (array != null && array.Length == list.Count)
							{
								for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
								{
									if ((int)array[i] == 0)
									{
										object obj = summaryGridControl._GridCache.Case.BatchGet(list[i], "value");
										if (obj != null && obj is string)
										{
											textBlock.Text = textBlock.Text + Environment.NewLine + obj;
											break;
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			((FrameworkElement)(object)val).ToolTip = textBlock;
		}

		private void _summaryGrid_FieldChooserOpening(object sender, FieldChooserOpeningEventArgs e)
		{
			FieldChooser fieldChooser = e.FieldChooser;
			if (fieldChooser != null)
			{
				fieldChooser.FieldDisplayOrder = (FieldChooserDisplayOrder)1;
				fieldChooser.FieldGroupSelectorVisibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
		}

	internal class CellForegroundConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] value, Type targettype, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			RowProxy rowProxy = value[0] as RowProxy;
			string text = value[1] as string;
			if (rowProxy != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
			{
				RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = rowProxy.GridCache.FindColumnProperty(text);
				bool? flag = rowProxy.GridCache.IsReadOnly(rowProxy, property);
				if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
				{
					return System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
				}
				return System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Blue;
			}
			return System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	internal class MultiValueLabelConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			System.Windows.Media.Brush result = (System.Windows.Media.Brush)new BrushConverter().ConvertFromString("#FFCFDDEE");
			if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				return result;
			}
			int num = (int)values[0];
			object obj = values[1];
			XamDataGrid val = (XamDataGrid)((obj is XamDataGrid) ? obj : null);
			SummaryGridControl summaryGridControl = SummaryGridControl.GetSummaryGridControl(val);
			if (val != null)
			{
				if (val.ViewSettings.Orientation == System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					if (num % 2 != 0)
					{
						return result;
					}
					return System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White;
				}
				return summaryGridControl.FindResource("ColumnHeaderBrush");

		}

	public class Comparer : IComparer
	{
		public int Compare(object x, object y)
		{
			if (x == null && y != null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (x != null && y == null)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			if (x == null && y == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			Hyperlink hyperlink = x as Hyperlink;
			Hyperlink hyperlink2 = y as Hyperlink;
			if (hyperlink != null && hyperlink2 != null)
			{
				HyperLinkInfo hyperLinkInfo = hyperlink.Tag as HyperLinkInfo;
				HyperLinkInfo hyperLinkInfo2 = hyperlink2.Tag as HyperLinkInfo;
				if (hyperLinkInfo == null || hyperLinkInfo2 == null)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				int num = string.Compare(hyperLinkInfo.Text, hyperLinkInfo2.Text, StringComparison.InvariantCulture);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (num > 0)
				{
					return 1;
				}
			}
			int? num2 = x as int?;
			int? num3 = y as int?;
			if (num2.HasValue && num3.HasValue)
			{
				if (num2 == num3)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (num2 > num3)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (num2 < num3)
				{
					return -1;
				}
			}
			double? num4 = x as double?;
			double? num5 = y as double?;
			if (num4.HasValue && num5.HasValue)
			{
				if (num4 == num5)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (num4 > num5)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (num4 < num5)
				{
					return -1;
				}
			}
			string strA = x.ToString();
			string strB = y.ToString();
			int num6 = string.Compare(strA, strB, StringComparison.InvariantCulture);
			if (num6 < 0)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (num6 == 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (num6 > 0)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			return -1;
		}
	}
}

		internal void UpdateFromBatchGet(CxsBatchRequestData requestData)
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			foreach (CxsBatchRequest request in requestData.Requests)
			{
				CxsBatchRequest current = request;
				if (((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Tag != this)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Attribute == GridColumn.ResolvedAttribute)
				{
					_ValueNeedsUpdate = false;
					Value = ((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Value;
					if (Value != null && GridColumn.DataType == ColumnDataType.Unspecified)
					{
						bool flag = true;
						switch (Type.GetTypeCode(Value.GetType()))
						{
						case TypeCode.Boolean:
							GridColumn.DataType = ColumnDataType.Boolean;
							break;
						case TypeCode.Single:
						case TypeCode.Double:
							GridColumn.DataType = ColumnDataType.Real;
							break;
						case TypeCode.Int16:
						case TypeCode.Int32:
						case TypeCode.Int64:
							GridColumn.DataType = ColumnDataType.Integer;
							break;
						case TypeCode.String:
							if (HasOptionList == true)
							{
								GridColumn.DataType = ColumnDataType.Enumeration;
							}
							else
							{
								GridColumn.DataType = ColumnDataType.String;
							}
							break;
						default:
							GridColumn.DataType = ColumnDataType.Unspecified;
							flag = false;
							break;
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							RowProxy.GridCache.OnCellDataTypeChanged(this);
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				switch (((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Attribute)
				{
				case "uom":
					_Units = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Value);
					break;
				case "option_list":
					_OptionList = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionArray(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Value);
					break;
				case "display_option_list":
					_displayOptionList = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionArray(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Value);
					break;
				case "uom_options":
					_UnitsList = CxsConverter.ValueToUnitsArray(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Value);
					break;
				case "can_enter":
				{
					bool? flag2 = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref current)).Value);
					if (flag2.HasValue)
					{
						_IsReadOnly = !flag2.Value;
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
		}

		private static Attribute[] CreateAttributeArray(SummaryGridColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<Attribute> list = new List<Attribute>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.Category))
			{
				list.Add(new CategoryAttribute(column.Category));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.Description))
			{
				list.Add(new DescriptionAttribute(column.Description));
			}
			bool? isReadOnly = column.IsReadOnly;
			if (isReadOnly.HasValue && isReadOnly.Value)
			{
				list.Add(new ReadOnlyAttribute(isReadOnly: true));
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		}

		internal void AddStandardColumns(bool excludeNameProperty, bool excludeGroupProperty)
		{

			bool flag2 = false;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			foreach (SummaryGridColumn column in _Columns)

				if (!(text == "NAME"))
				{

					{
						flag2 = true;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					flag = true;
					num = num2;
				}
				if (flag2 && flag)
				{
					break;
				}
				num2++;
			}

			{
				_Columns.Insert(0, new SummaryGridColumn
				{
					Name = "Name",
					DataType = ColumnDataType.String,
					Path = "#Name",
					IsReadOnly = true
				});
				num = 0;
			}
			if (!flag2 && !excludeGroupProperty)
			{
				_Columns.Insert(num + 1, new SummaryGridColumn
				{

					Path = "#Group",

					HasOptionList = false

		}

		}

						if (flag)
						{
							val5 = new CxsBatchRequestData();
							val5.AddRequest(val2.CommonBasePath, "ignore_errors", (string)null, (object)1);
							val5.AddRequests((IEnumerable<CxsBatchRequest>)val2.Requests);
							Case.BatchSet(val5);
						}
						RefreshData(resetDataCache: true);
						_summaryGridControl.TabbedSummaryGrid.TriggerOnBatchSetEvent();
					}
					else
					{
						_summaryGridControl.RefreshDisplay(resetDataCache: false);
					}
				}
				else if (((Exception)(object)ex2).InnerException != null && ((Exception)(object)ex2).InnerException.Message == "refresh needed")
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						flag = false;
						try
						{
							Case.BatchSet(val2);
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
					if (_summaryGridControl != null && _summaryGridControl.TabbedSummaryGrid != null)
					{
						_summaryGridControl.TabbedSummaryGrid.TriggerOnBatchSetEvent();
					}
					GetCellCache(rowProxy, property).Value = value;
				}
				else
				{
					bool flag6 = false;
					if (this.DataInputExceptionRaised != null)
					{
						DataInputExceptionEventArgs e = new DataInputExceptionEventArgs((Exception)(object)ex2);
						this.DataInputExceptionRaised(this, e);
						flag6 = e.Handled;
					}
					if (!flag6)
					{
						throw;
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		}

		public void GridRowsChanged()
		{
			List<SummaryGridRow> list = new List<SummaryGridRow>();
			List<RowProxy> list2 = new List<RowProxy>();
