			{
				bool flag = false;
				using (IEnumerator<RowProxy> enumerator2 = GetEnumerator())
				{
					while (enumerator2.MoveNext())
					{
						if (enumerator2.Current.GridRow == summaryRow)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					list.Add(summaryRow);
				}
			}
			using (IEnumerator<RowProxy> enumerator2 = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator2.MoveNext())
				{
					RowProxy current2 = enumerator2.Current;
					bool flag2 = false;
					foreach (SummaryGridRow summaryRow2 in SummaryRows)
					{
						if (current2.GridRow == summaryRow2)
						{
							flag2 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag2)
					{
						list2.Add(current2);
					}
				}
			}
			if (list2.Count == 0 && list.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (RowProxy item2 in list2)
			{
				Remove(item2);
			}
			foreach (SummaryGridRow item3 in list)

				Add(item);
			}
		}

		private RowProxy FindRowProxy(SummaryGridRow row)
		{
			using (IEnumerator<RowProxy> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					RowProxy current = enumerator.Current;
					if (current.GridRow == row)
					{
						return current;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void GridColumnsChanged(bool resetExistingProperties)
		{
			List<SummaryGridColumn> list = new List<SummaryGridColumn>();
			List<PropertyDescriptor> list2 = new List<PropertyDescriptor>();
			List<PropertyDescriptor> list3 = new List<PropertyDescriptor>();

					{
						flag = true;

							list3.Add(rowProperty);
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					list.Add(summaryColumn);
				}
			}
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor rowProperty2 in _RowProperties)

			{

			}

			}
		}

		internal RowProxyPropertyDescriptor FindColumnProperty(string name)
		{
			if (_RowProperties == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			foreach (RowProxyPropertyDescriptor rowProperty in _RowProperties)
			{

				{
					return rowProperty;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private string CreateUniquePropertyNameForColumn(SummaryGridColumn column)
		{
			string caption = column.Caption;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(caption))
			{
				caption = column.Caption;
			}
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(caption))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(caption.Length);
				string text = caption;
				foreach (char c in text)
				{
					if (char.IsLetterOrDigit(c))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
				}
				if (char.IsDigit(stringBuilder[0]))
				{
					stringBuilder.Insert(0, '_');
				}
				empty = stringBuilder.ToString();
				if (FindColumnProperty(empty) == null)
				{
					return empty;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				empty = "Column";
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < 99; j++)
			{
				string text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}{1}", empty, j);
				if (FindColumnProperty(text2) == null)
				{
					return text2;
				}
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		public virtual string GetListName(PropertyDescriptor[] listAccessors)
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (listAccessors != null)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				foreach (PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor in listAccessors)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(propertyDescriptor.PropertyType.Name);
					stringBuilder.Append("_");
				}
				return stringBuilder.ToString().TrimEnd('_');
			}
			return GetType().Name;
		}

		}

		public string FormatColumnCaption(SummaryGridColumn column)
		{
			if (column == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string text = column.Caption;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = column.Name;
			}
			string text2 = (column.UsesUnits ? GetColumnUnits(column) : string.Empty);
			if (text.Contains("%Units%"))
			{
				text = (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2) ? text.Replace("%Units%", "") : text.Replace("%Units%", " [" + text2 + "]"));
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2))
			{
				text = text + " [" + text2 + "]";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void BatchGetColUnits()
		{
			//IL_00a6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_00f6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0118: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_011d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_013a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_013f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_015b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0160: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (Case == null || !Case.IsConnected)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < SummaryRows.Count; i++)
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData val = CreateColUnitsRequestData(i);
				if (val == null || val.RequestsCount == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				object[] array = null;
				try
				{
					array = Case.BatchGet(val);
				}
				catch
				{
					break;
				}
				bool flag = true;
				if (array != null)
				{
					object[] array2 = array;
					for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
					{
						if (array2[j] != null)
						{
							flag = false;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (array != null)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < array.Length; k++)
					{
						string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[k]);
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							continue;
						}
						_ = val.Requests[k];
						CxsBatchRequest val2 = val.Requests[k];
						if (((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						val2 = val.Requests[k];

						{
							val2 = val.Requests[k];
							if (string.CompareOrdinal(text, (((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag as SummaryGridColumn).Units) != 0)
							{
								val2 = val.Requests[k];
								(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag as SummaryGridColumn).Units = text;
								val2 = val.Requests[k];
								(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag as SummaryGridColumn).UnitsDirty = true;
								val2 = val.Requests[k];
								(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag as SummaryGridColumn).UsesUnits = true;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				{
					foreach (SummaryGridRow summaryRow in SummaryRows)
					{
						RowProxy row = FindRowProxy(summaryRow);
						foreach (SummaryGridColumn summaryColumn in SummaryColumns)
						{
							RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = FindColumnProperty(summaryColumn);
							GetCellCache(row, property).Units = summaryColumn.Units;
						}
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private CxsBatchRequestData CreateColUnitsRequestData(int RowIndex)
		{
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_006d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (RowIndex >= SummaryRows.Count)
			{
				return null;
			}
			CxsBatchRequestData val = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (SummaryGridColumn summaryColumn in SummaryColumns)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(summaryColumn.Units))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (summaryColumn.ValuesDirty || summaryColumn.UnitsDirty)
				{
					SummaryGridRow summaryGridRow = SummaryRows[RowIndex];
					if (!summaryGridRow.Path.StartsWith("#", StringComparison.Ordinal))
					{
						CxsBatchRequest val2 = default(CxsBatchRequest);
						((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Path = summaryGridRow.CalculateColumnPath(summaryColumn);
						if (((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Path.StartsWith("#"))
						{
							continue;
						}
						((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Attribute = "uom";
						((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag = summaryColumn;
						val.AddRequest(val2);
					}
				}
				summaryColumn.ValuesDirty = false;
			}
			return val;
		}

		public string GetColumnUnits(SummaryGridColumn column)
		{
			//IL_0150: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0155: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00b2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00eb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(column.Units))
			{
				return column.Units;
			}
			if (_summaryGridControl.TabbedSummaryGrid.UseProvidedRowInfo && !column.UsesUnits)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (Case == null || !Case.IsConnected)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			bool flag = true;
			foreach (SummaryGridRow summaryRow in SummaryRows)
			{
				CxsBatchRequestData val = new CxsBatchRequestData();
				foreach (SummaryGridColumn summaryColumn in SummaryColumns)
				{
					if ((!_summaryGridControl.TabbedSummaryGrid.UseProvidedRowInfo || summaryColumn.UsesUnits) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(summaryColumn.Units))
					{
						CxsBatchRequest val2 = default(CxsBatchRequest);
						((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Path = summaryRow.CalculateColumnPath(summaryColumn);
						if (SummaryGridRow.IsRealPath(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Path))
						{
							((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Attribute = "uom";
							((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag = summaryColumn;
							val.AddRequest(val2);
						}
					}
				}
				if (val.RequestsCount == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				object[] array = null;
				try
				{
					array = Case.BatchGet(val);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				if (array != null)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(array[i]);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							CxsBatchRequest val3 = val.Requests[i];
							(((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val3)).Tag as SummaryGridColumn).Units = text;
						}
					}
				}
				flag = false;

				{
					if (summaryColumn2.UsesUnits && string.IsNullOrEmpty(summaryColumn2.Units))
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag)

				foreach (SummaryGridColumn summaryColumn3 in SummaryColumns)
				{
					if (summaryColumn3.UsesUnits && string.IsNullOrEmpty(summaryColumn3.Units))
					{
						summaryColumn3.UsesUnits = false;
					}
				}
			}
			return column.Units;
		}

		internal void RefreshData(bool resetDataCache)
		{
			if (resetDataCache)
			{
				if (_CellCache.Count == 0)
				{
					foreach (SummaryGridRow row2 in _Rows)
					{
						RowProxy row = FindRowProxy(row2);
						foreach (SummaryGridColumn summaryColumn in _summaryGridControl.SummaryColumns)
						{
							RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = FindColumnProperty(summaryColumn);
							GetCellCache(row, property);
						}
					}
				}
				foreach (CellCache value in _CellCache.Values)
				{
					value.ResetValueCache();
				}
			}
			OnListChanged(new ListChangedEventArgs(ListChangedType.Reset, 0, base.Count - 1));
			UpdateRowEEStatus();
		}

		internal void UpdateRowEEStatus()
		{

				if (!(rowProxy2.GridRow.EEStatus == "Warning") && !(rowProxy2.GridRow.EEStatus == "Error"))

					array3 = Case.BatchGet(val3);
				}

				for (int k = 0; k < array3.Length; k++)
				{
					if (array3[k] == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					string value = array3[k].ToString();
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
					{
						if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append("\n\r");
						}
						stringBuilder.Append(value);
					}
				}
				rowProxy2.StatusReport = stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
		}

		internal void BatchGetCellNodeTraits()
		{
			//IL_0108: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_010e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			List<CellCache> list2 = new List<CellCache>();
			foreach (SummaryGridColumn summaryColumn in SummaryColumns)
			{
				RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property = FindColumnProperty(summaryColumn);
				foreach (SummaryGridRow summaryRow in SummaryRows)
				{
					RowProxy row = FindRowProxy(summaryRow);
					CellCache cellCache = GetCellCache(row, property);
					if (cellCache != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cellCache.Path) && !cellCache.Path.ToString().StartsWith("#", StringComparison.Ordinal))
					{
						list.Add(cellCache.Path);
						list2.Add(cellCache);
					}
				}
			}
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			CxsNodeTraits[] nodeTraits = Case.GetNodeTraits(list.ToArray());
			if (nodeTraits == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < list2.Count && nodeTraits.Length > i; i++)
			{
				if (nodeTraits[i] == null)
				{
					nodeTraits[i] = new CxsNodeTraits(new string[0]);
				}
				list2[i].NodeTraits = nodeTraits[i];
			}
		}

		internal bool? HasOptionList(RowProxy rowProxy, RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			bool? hasOptionList = property.GridColumn.HasOptionList;
			if (hasOptionList.HasValue)
			{
				return hasOptionList;
			}
			CellCache cellCache = GetCellCache(rowProxy, property);
			if (cellCache.Path.StartsWith("#", StringComparison.Ordinal))
			{
				return false;
			}
			hasOptionList = cellCache.HasOptionList;
			if (!hasOptionList.HasValue && cellCache.TraitsNeedsUpdate)
			{
				BatchGetCellNodeTraits();
				hasOptionList = cellCache.HasOptionList;
			}
			return hasOptionList;
		}

		internal bool? IsReadOnly(RowProxy rowProxy, RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			//IL_004e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0054: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00c3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ec: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			bool? isReadOnly = property.GridColumn.IsReadOnly;
			if (isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				return isReadOnly.Value;
			}
			CellCache cellCache = GetCellCache(rowProxy, property);
			isReadOnly = cellCache.IsReadOnly;
			if (isReadOnly.HasValue)
			{
				return isReadOnly.Value;
			}
			List<CellCache> list = new List<CellCache>();
			CxsBatchRequestData val = new CxsBatchRequestData();
			foreach (SummaryGridRow adjacentRow in GetAdjacentRows(rowProxy.GridRow))
			{
				RowProxy row = FindRowProxy(adjacentRow);
				CellCache cellCache2 = GetCellCache(row, property);
				if (!cellCache2.IsReadOnly.HasValue && (cellCache2.NodeTraits == null || cellCache2.NodeTraits.IsValidAttribute("can_enter")))
				{
					list.Add(cellCache2);
					CxsBatchRequest val2 = default(CxsBatchRequest);
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Path = cellCache2.Path;
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Attribute = "can_enter";
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag = cellCache2;
					val.AddRequest(val2);
				}
			}
			if (val.RequestsCount > 0)
			{
				object[] array = Case.BatchGet(val);
				val.UpdateRequestValues(array);
				foreach (CellCache item in list)
				{
					item.UpdateFromBatchGet(val);
				}
				isReadOnly = cellCache.IsReadOnly;
			}
			return isReadOnly;
		}

		internal string[] GetDisplayOptionList(RowProxy row, RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0084: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string[] array = null;
			CellCache cellCache = GetCellCache(row, property);
			array = cellCache.DisplayOptionList;
			if (array == null)
			{
				bool? flag = HasOptionList(row, property);
				if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
				{
					CxsBatchRequestData val = new CxsBatchRequestData();
					string path = cellCache.Path;
					if (cellCache.UseUnderlyingPathForOptions ?? false)
					{
						path = cellCache.UnderlyingPath;
					}
					CxsBatchRequest val2 = default(CxsBatchRequest);
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Path = path;
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Attribute = "display_option_list";
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag = cellCache;
					val.AddRequest(val2);
					object[] array2 = Case.BatchGet(val);
					val.UpdateRequestValues(array2);
					cellCache.UpdateFromBatchGet(val);
					array = cellCache.DisplayOptionList;
				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal string[] GetOptionList(RowProxy row, RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0084: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string[] array = null;
			CellCache cellCache = GetCellCache(row, property);
			array = cellCache.OptionList;
			if (array == null)
			{
				bool? flag = HasOptionList(row, property);
				if (flag.HasValue && flag.Value)
				{

					string path = cellCache.Path;
					if (cellCache.UseUnderlyingPathForOptions ?? false)
					{
						path = cellCache.UnderlyingPath;
					}

					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Path = path;
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Attribute = "option_list";
					((CxsBatchRequest)(ref val2)).Tag = cellCache;
					val.AddRequest(val2);

					cellCache.UpdateFromBatchGet(val);

				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal CellCache GetCellCache(RowProxy row, RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			CellKey key = new CellKey(row, property);
			CellCache value = null;
			if (!_CellCache.TryGetValue(key, out value))
			{
				value = new CellCache(row, property);
				_CellCache[key] = value;
			}
			return value;
		}

		public void OnCellDataTypeChanged(CellCache cell)
		{
			if (this.CellDataTypeChanged != null)
			{
				this.CellDataTypeChanged(new CellDataTypeChangedEventArgs
				{
					Cell = cell
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class RowProxy : ICustomTypeDescriptor, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private string _statusReport = string.Empty;

		private GridCache _GridCache;

		private SummaryGridRow _GridRow;

		private bool _hasDetailTab;

		private bool _checkStatus;

		internal GridCache GridCache => _GridCache;

		internal SummaryGridRow GridRow => _GridRow;

		internal bool CheckStatus => _GridRow.StatusReportPaths.Length != 0;

		internal bool HasDetailTab => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_GridRow.DetailTab);

		internal string StatusReport
		{
			get
			{
				return _statusReport;
			}
			set
			{
				_statusReport = value;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		internal RowProxy(GridCache gridCache, SummaryGridRow gridRow)
		{
			_GridCache = gridCache;
			_GridRow = gridRow;
		}

		internal int CompareKeyTo(RowProxy other)
		{
			return _GridRow.CompareKeyTo(other.GridRow);
		}

		internal int GetKeyHashCode()
		{
			return _GridRow.GetKeyHashCode();
		}

		internal object GetValue(RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			return _GridCache.GetValue(this, property);
		}

		internal void SetValue(RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property, object value)
		{
			_GridCache.SetValue(this, property, value);
		}

		internal bool CanResetValue(RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
			return false;
		}

		internal void ResetValue(RowProxyPropertyDescriptor property)
		{
		}

		internal void RaisePropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, args);
			}
		}

		public virtual AttributeCollection GetAttributes()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetAttributes(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual string GetClassName()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetClassName(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual string GetComponentName()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetComponentName(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual TypeConverter GetConverter()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual EventDescriptor GetDefaultEvent()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetDefaultEvent(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual PropertyDescriptor GetDefaultProperty()
		{
			return _GridCache.RowDefaultProperty;
		}

		public virtual object GetEditor(Type editorBaseType)
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetEditor(this, editorBaseType, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual EventDescriptorCollection GetEvents()
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetEvents(this, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual EventDescriptorCollection GetEvents(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return TypeDescriptor.GetEvents(this, attributes, noCustomTypeDesc: true);
		}

		public virtual PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties()
		{
			return GetProperties(null);
		}

		public virtual PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			return _GridCache.GetRowProperties(attributes);
		}

		public virtual object GetPropertyOwner(PropertyDescriptor pd)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}
}
